  if (T.weapon === 'ram') b.w.ram -= 1; else if (T.weapon) b.w[T.weapon] -= troops;
  if (cargo) for (k in cargo) b.w[k] -= cargo[k];
  var u = { id: G.nextUnit++, f: b.owner, c: spot[0], r: spot[1], type: spec.type, off: os.map(function (o) { return o.id; }), troops: troops, max: troops,
    morale: b.morale, food: food, gold: gold, cargo: cargo, status: null, stT: 0, done: false, moved: false, mp: 0, home: b.id, ox: -1, oy: -1, omp: 0, ozin: -1, zin: -1, ai: null, dest: -1 };
  for (var i = 0; i < os.length; i++) { os[i].unit = u.id; os[i].acted = true; }
  u.mp = uMaxMp(u);
  if (spec.dest !== undefined && spec.dest >= 0) u.dest = spec.dest;
  addUnit(u);
  ev({ t: 'deploy', id: u.id, f: u.f, n: uName(u), base: b.id, c: u.c, r: u.r });
  return { ok: true, unit: u };
}

/* ---------- 부대 이동 ---------- */
function unitMove(u, c, r, rc) {
  if (u.done) return ERR('이미 행동을 마쳤습니다');
  rc = rc || unitReach(u);
  var k = K(c, r);
  if (!rc.stop[k]) return ERR('갈 수 없는 곳입니다');
  var path = pathFromReach(rc, u, c, r);
  if (!path) return ERR('경로가 없습니다');
  var from = [u.c, u.r];
  u.ox = u.c; u.oy = u.r; u.omp = u.mp; u.ozin = (u.zin === undefined ? -1 : u.zin);
  var b = baseAt(c, r);
  if (b) {
    u.mp = 0; u.moved = true;
    ev({ t: 'move', id: u.id, f: u.f, n: uName(u), from: from, path: path, c: c, r: r, into: b.id });
    if (b.owner === u.f) { enterBase(u, b); return { ok: true, path: path, from: from, entered: b.id }; }
    if (b.owner === -1) { placeUnit(u, c, r); captureBase(b, u); return { ok: true, path: path, from: from, captured: b.id }; }
    return ERR('적의 거점입니다');
  }
  placeUnit(u, c, r);
  u.mp = Math.max(0, u.mp - rc.cost[k]);
  if (ZONE[k] !== ZONE[K(from[0], from[1])]) { u.mp = 0; u.zin = ZONE[k]; }   // 성역의 경계를 넘었으면 이번 턴 이동은 여기까지. 막 들어선 성역의 성은 이번 턴에 칠 수 없다(zin)
  u.moved = true;
  var res = { ok: true, path: path, from: from };
  ev({ t: 'move', id: u.id, f: u.f, n: uName(u), from: from, path: path, c: c, r: r });
  if (G.fires[k] && !uHas(u, '화신')) { var fd = ri(150, 250); res.burn = fd; u.ox = -1; if (hurtUnit(u, fd, -1)) res.dead = true; }
  return res;
}
function unitUndoMove(u) {
  if (u.done || u.ox < 0 || !UMAP[u.id]) return false;
  if (UAT[K(u.ox, u.oy)]) return false;
  placeUnit(u, u.ox, u.oy); u.mp = u.omp; u.zin = (u.ozin === undefined ? -1 : u.ozin); u.moved = false; u.ox = -1;
  return true;
}
function unitWait(u) { u.done = true; u.ox = -1; return { ok: true }; }

function autoRetreat(u) {
  var nb = nearestOwnBase(u.f, u.c, u.r);
  if (!nb) return;
  var gk = K(nb.c, nb.r);
  var path = longPath(u, function (k) { return k === gk; }, 0, [nb.c, nb.r, 0]);
  if (!path) return;
  var rc = unitReach(u), best = -1;
  for (var i = 0; i < path.length; i++) if (rc.stop[path[i]]) best = path[i];
  if (best >= 0) { var c = best % W, r = (best - c) / W; unitMove(u, c, r, rc); }
}

/* ---------- 행군(목적지 지정 이동) ---------- */
function marchPath(u, gk) { var gc = gk % W; return longPath(u, function (k) { return k === gk; }, 3000, [gc, (gk - gc) / W, 0]); }
function marchTurns(u, path) {
  var full = uMaxMp(u), left = u.done ? 0 : u.mp, turns = 1, z = ZONE[K(u.c, u.r)], halt = false;
  for (var i = 0; i < path.length; i++) {
    var c = path[i] % W, mc = moveCost(u, c, (path[i] - c) / W);
    if (halt || mc > left) { turns++; left = full; halt = false; }
    left -= mc;
    if (ZONE[path[i]] !== z) { z = ZONE[path[i]]; halt = true; }       // 성역의 경계를 넘으면 그 턴은 거기까지
  }
  return turns;
}
function unitMarch(u) {
  if (u.done || u.dest === undefined || u.dest === null || u.dest < 0) return null;
  if (K(u.c, u.r) === u.dest) { u.dest = -1; return null; }
  var path = marchPath(u, u.dest);
  if (!path) { u.dest = -1; return { ok: false, lost: true }; }
  var rc = unitReach(u), best = -1;
  for (var i = 0; i < path.length; i++) if (rc.stop[path[i]] && (!G.fires[path[i]] || BAT[path[i]] !== undefined)) best = path[i];
  if (best < 0) {
    // 목적지가 바로 옆의 적 거점이면 도착한 것으로 본다(들어갈 수는 없으니 공격은 직접 명령)
    var dc0 = u.dest % W, dr0 = (u.dest - dc0) / W, db0 = baseAt(dc0, dr0);
    if (db0 && db0.owner >= 0 && db0.owner !== u.f && hdist(u.c, u.r, dc0, dr0) <= 1) { u.dest = -1; return { ok: false, front: db0.id }; }
    return null;
  }
  var c = best % W, r = (best - c) / W, dk = u.dest, res = unitMove(u, c, r, rc);
  if (res.ok && (best === dk || res.entered !== undefined || res.captured !== undefined || res.dead)) u.dest = -1;
  else if (res.ok && UMAP[u.id]) {                         // 적 거점 바로 앞에 닿았으면 행군은 여기까지
    var dc1 = dk % W, dr1 = (dk - dc1) / W, db1 = baseAt(dc1, dr1);
    if (db1 && db1.owner >= 0 && db1.owner !== u.f && hdist(u.c, u.r, dc1, dr1) <= 1) { u.dest = -1; res.front = db1.id; }
  }
  return res;
}

/* 출진 전에 목적지까지 걸릴 턴 수를 재 본다: 실제로 나가게 될 성 밖 칸에서 출발하는 임시 부대로 잰다.
   돌려주는 값은 '출진한 턴을 포함해 몇 턴째에 닿는가'. 길이 없으면 0 */
function deployEta(b, type, ids, destKey) {
  if (!ids.length) return 0;
  var dc = destKey % W, dr = (destKey - dc) / W, spot = deploySpot(b, { c: dc, r: dr });
  if (!spot) return 0;
  if (K(spot[0], spot[1]) === destKey) return 1;
  var tmp = { id: -1, f: b.owner, c: spot[0], r: spot[1], type: type, off: ids, troops: 1000, done: false, mp: 0 };
  tmp.mp = uMaxMp(tmp);
  var path = marchPath(tmp, destKey);
  return path ? marchTurns(tmp, path) : 0;
}

/* ---------- 자동 추천: 그 일을 가장 잘하는 무장 ---------- */
function statSum(o) { return o.ldr + o.war + o.int + o.pol + o.cha; }
function autoValue(kind, b, os, ctx) {
  var v = 0, i, t = 0;
  for (i = 0; i < os.length; i++) t += statSum(os[i]);
  if (kind === 'develop') { v = 60 + Math.round((bestOf(os, 'pol') + sumOthers(os, 'pol') * 0.5) * 0.6); if (anyHas(os, '축성')) v *= 2; }
  else if (kind === 'draft') v = draftAmount(b, os) + (anyHas(os, '인덕') ? 150 : 0);
  else if (kind === 'patrol') { v = Math.round(5 + Math.max(bestOf(os, 'war'), bestOf(os, 'ldr')) / 9 + (os.length - 1) * 2); if (anyHas(os, '위압')) v *= 2; v *= 10; }
  else if (kind === 'train') v = Math.round(8 + bestOf(os, 'ldr') / 8 + (os.length - 1) * 2) * 10;
  else if (kind === 'produce') { if (ctx && ctx.kind === 'ram') v = anyHas(os, '발명') ? 1000 : (os.length > 1 ? -1 : 0); else v = produceAmount(b, ctx.kind, os); }
  else if (kind === 'search') v = (25 + os[0].pol * 0.35 + os[0].cha * 0.15) * (os[0].sk === '안력' ? 2 : 1) * 10;
  else if (kind === 'recruit') v = recruitChance(b, os[0], ctx.target) * 10;
  else if (kind === 'merge') v = 0;
  return v - t * 0.001;                       // 같은 효과면 능력이 낮은 무장을 쓴다
}
function autoPick(kind, b, ctx, max, cands) {
  cands = cands || idleOfficers(b); max = max || 1;
  var chosen = [], cur = -1e9;
  while (chosen.length < max) {
    var bi = -1, bv = cur + (chosen.length ? 0.5 : 0);   // 덧붙여도 이득이 없으면 더 넣지 않는다
    for (var i = 0; i < cands.length; i++) {
      if (chosen.indexOf(cands[i]) >= 0) continue;
      var v = autoValue(kind, b, chosen.concat([cands[i]]), ctx);
      if (v > bv) { bv = v; bi = i; }
    }
    if (bi < 0) break;
    chosen.push(cands[bi]); cur = bv;
  }
  return chosen.map(function (o) { return o.id; });
}
/* 출진 편성 추천: 병과에 맞는 주장 + 도움이 되는 부장 */
function teamScore(type, os, want) {
  var L = os[0], idx = APT_IDX[type], apt = 'B', i;
  if (type === 'trans') return 300 - (L.war * 0.55 + L.ldr * 0.45) - (isLord(L) ? 100 : 0);
  if (idx !== undefined) { apt = 'C'; for (i = 0; i < os.length; i++) { var a = os[i].apt.charAt(idx); if (APT_RANK[a] > APT_RANK[apt]) apt = a; } }
  var atk = L.war * 0.7 + bestOf(os, 'war') * 0.3, def = L.ldr * 0.7 + bestOf(os, 'ldr') * 0.3;
  var v = (atk + def) * APT_MUL[apt] * (0.55 + 0.45 * Math.min(1, cmdCap(L) / Math.max(1000, want)));
  v += bestOf(os, 'int') * 0.12;
  for (i = 0; i < os.length; i++) if (os[i].sk && AUTO_WAR_SKILLS.indexOf(os[i].sk) >= 0) v += 6;
  if (isLord(L)) v *= 0.93;
  return v;
}
var AUTO_WAR_SKILLS = ['비장', '신장', '투신', '용장', '창장', '극장', '궁장', '기장', '기신', '궁신', '위풍', '연전', '금강', '철벽', '불굴', '급습', '공심', '강행', '답파', '포박', '공성', '신산', '허실', '귀모', '통찰', '명경', '간파', '반계', '연환', '심모', '화신', '화공', '둔전'];
function autoTeam(b, type, want) {
  var cands = idleOfficers(b), best = null, bv = -1e9, i;
  for (i = 0; i < cands.length; i++) { var v = teamScore(type, [cands[i]], want); if (v > bv) { bv = v; best = cands[i]; } }
  if (!best) return [];
  var team = [best];
  if (type === 'trans') return [best.id];
  while (team.length < 3) {
    var bi = null, gain = bv * 1.012;                 // 1.2% 넘게 좋아질 때만 부장을 붙인다
    for (i = 0; i < cands.length; i++) {
      if (team.indexOf(cands[i]) >= 0 || isLord(cands[i])) continue;
      var v2 = teamScore(type, team.concat([cands[i]]), want) - statSum(cands[i]) * 0.002;
      if (v2 > gain) { gain = v2; bi = cands[i]; }
    }
    if (!bi) break;
    team.push(bi); bv = teamScore(type, team, want);
  }
  return team.map(function (o) { return o.id; });
}

/* ---------- 전투 ---------- */
function dmgUU(a, d, mult, ranged) {
  var x = 620 * Math.pow(uAtk(a) / Math.max(10, uDef(d)), 0.9) * troopFactor(a.troops) * mult;
  var c = (COMPAT[a.type] && COMPAT[a.type][d.type]) || 1;
  if (VS_TYPE[d.type] && (!ranged || d.type === 'trans' || d.type === 'ram')) c *= VS_TYPE[d.type];
  x *= c;
  var td = terr(d.c, d.r), ta = terr(a.c, a.r);
  if (td === 'f' || td === 'h') x *= 0.85; else if (td === 'w') x *= 1.2;
  if (ta === 'w') x *= 0.7;
  if (d.status === 'confuse') x *= 1.25;
  x *= 0.92 + rnd() * 0.16;
  return Math.max(30, Math.round(x));
}
function baseDefVal(b) {
  var os = baseOfficers(b), l = Math.max(50, bestOf(os, 'ldr'));
  return l * (b.kind === 'gate' ? 1.6 : 1.3) * (0.7 + 0.3 * b.dur / b.maxDur);
}
function dmgUB(a, b) {
  var T = UNIT_TYPES[a.type];
  var x = 620 * Math.pow(uAtk(a) / baseDefVal(b), 0.9) * troopFactor(a.troops) * T.vsBase * (0.92 + rnd() * 0.16);
  if (terr(a.c, a.r) === 'w') x *= 0.7;
  return Math.max(20, Math.round(x));
}
function durDmgUB(a) {
  var T = UNIT_TYPES[a.type];
  var x = (uAtk(a) * 1.2 + 40) * troopFactor(a.troops) * T.siege;
  if (uHas(a, '공성')) x *= 1.5;
  return Math.round(x);
}
function dmgBU(b, u) {
  var os = baseOfficers(b), w = Math.max(50, bestOf(os, 'war'));
  var x = 620 * Math.pow(w / Math.max(10, uDef(u)), 0.9) * troopFactor(b.troops) * 0.5 * (0.92 + rnd() * 0.16);
  if (uHas(u, '금강')) x *= 0.65;
  return Math.max(20, Math.round(x));
}
function defMods(d, dmg, kind) {
  if (kind === 'normal' && uHas(d, '금강')) dmg *= 0.65;
  if (kind === 'tactic' && uHas(d, '철벽')) dmg *= 0.7;
  if (uHas(d, '불굴') && d.troops <= d.max * 0.3) dmg *= 0.5;
  return Math.round(dmg);
}
function afterHit(a, d, dealt) {
  addMerit(a, Math.round(dealt / 25));
  if (uHas(a, '공심') && UMAP[a.id]) a.troops = Math.min(a.max, a.troops + Math.round(dealt * 0.1));
  if (uHas(a, '위풍') && UMAP[d.id]) d.morale = Math.max(0, d.morale - 10);
}
function alive(u) { return !!UMAP[u.id]; }
function setStatus(u, st, turns) { if (!alive(u)) return; u.status = st; u.stT = turns; }
function setFire(c, r, turns) {
  var t = terr(c, r);
  if (t === 'w' || t === 's' || t === 'm' || t === 'x') return false;
  if (BAT[K(c, r)] !== undefined) return false;
  G.fires[K(c, r)] = Math.max(G.fires[K(c, r)] || 0, turns);
  return true;
}

/* 일기토: 근접전에서 낮은 확률로 벌어지는 자동 판정 */
function tryDuel(a, d, res) {
  if (!alive(a) || !alive(d) || hdist(a.c, a.r, d.c, d.r) !== 1) return;
  var la = uLeader(a), ld = uLeader(d);
  if (la.war < 75 || ld.war < 75 || !chance(7)) return;
  var win = chance(100 / (1 + Math.exp(-(la.war - ld.war) / 6)));
  var Wn = win ? a : d, Ls = win ? d : a, loss = Math.round(Ls.troops * 0.08);
  Ls.morale = Math.max(0, Ls.morale - 25); Wn.morale = Math.min(100, Wn.morale + 10);
  off(Wn.off[0]).merit += 100;
  res.duel = { a: la.n, d: ld.n, win: win, loss: loss };
  logMsg('일기토! ' + la.n + ' 대 ' + ld.n + ' — ' + (win ? la.n : ld.n) + ' 승', d.c, d.r, -1, (a.f === G.player || d.f === G.player) ? 2 : 0);
  if (hurtUnit(Ls, loss, Wn.f, Wn)) { if (Ls === a) res.selfDead = true; else res.killed = true; }
}

/* 공격 가능한 대상 (현재 위치 기준) */
function attackTargets(u) {
  var out = [], rg = uRange(u), c, r;
  for (c = Math.max(0, u.c - rg - 1); c <= Math.min(W - 1, u.c + rg + 1); c++) for (r = Math.max(0, u.r - rg); r <= Math.min(H - 1, u.r + rg); r++) {
    var d = hdist(u.c, u.r, c, r);
    if (d < 1 || d > rg) continue;
    var k = K(c, r), ou = UAT[k];
    if (ou && ou.f !== u.f) { out.push({ kind: 'unit', u: ou, c: c, r: r, d: d }); continue; }
    var bi = BAT[k];
    if (bi !== undefined) { var b = G.bases[bi]; if (b.owner >= 0 && b.owner !== u.f && u.zin !== b.id) out.push({ kind: 'base', b: b, c: c, r: r, d: d }); continue; }
    var pf = PAT[k];
    if (pf) { var pb = G.bases[pf.b], fc = pb.plots[pf.i].fac; if (fc && pb.owner >= 0 && pb.owner !== u.f && d === 1) out.push({ kind: 'fac', b: pb, i: pf.i, c: c, r: r, d: d }); }
  }
  return out;
}
function critNormal(a, d) { return (uHas(a, '비장') || uHas(a, '용장') || uHas(a, '신장')) && uWar(a) > uWar(d); }

function zoneFresh(u, b) { return !!b && u.zin !== undefined && u.zin >= 0 && u.zin === b.id; }   // 이번 턴에 이 성의 성역에 막 들어섰는가
function unitAttack0(u, t) {
  if (u.done) return ERR('이미 행동을 마쳤습니다');
  if (t.kind === 'base' && zoneFresh(u, t.b)) return ERR('성역에 막 들어선 부대는 이번 턴에 성을 칠 수 없습니다');
  var res = { ok: true, kind: t.kind, c: t.c, r: t.r, ac: u.c, ar: u.r };
  u.done = true; u.ox = -1;
  if (t.kind === 'unit') {
    var d = t.u, ranged = hdist(u.c, u.r, d.c, d.r) > 1;
    var crit = critNormal(u, d);
    var dmg = defMods(d, dmgUU(u, d, crit ? 1.3 : 1, ranged), 'normal');
    res.crit = crit; res.dmg = dmg; res.tname = uName(d);
    var dead = hurtUnit(d, dmg, u.f, u);
    afterHit(u, d, dmg);
    res.killed = dead;
    if (!dead && d.status !== 'confuse' && d.status !== 'rumor' && (!ranged || d.type === 'bow')) {
      if (!(uHas(u, '급습') && chance(50))) {
        var cm = ranged ? 0.4 : 0.55; if (d.type === 'bow' && !ranged) cm *= 0.6;
        var cd = defMods(u, dmgUU(d, u, cm, ranged), 'normal');
        res.counter = cd;
        if (hurtUnit(u, cd, d.f, d)) { res.selfDead = true; return res; }
      }
    }
    if (!dead && alive(u) && uHas(u, '연전') && chance(50)) {
      var d2 = defMods(d, dmgUU(u, d, 0.8, ranged), 'normal');
      res.dmg2 = d2; res.killed = hurtUnit(d, d2, u.f, u); afterHit(u, d, d2);
    }
    if (!ranged) tryDuel(u, d, res);
    return res;
  }
  if (t.kind === 'base') {
    var b = t.b, td = dmgUB(u, b), dd = durDmgUB(u), ranged2 = hdist(u.c, u.r, b.c, b.r) > 1;
    td = Math.min(td, b.troops);
    b.troops -= td; b.dur = Math.max(0, b.dur - dd);
    res.dmg = td; res.dur = dd; res.tname = b.n;
    addMerit(u, Math.round((td + dd) / 25));
    if (b.owner === G.player) logMsg(b.n + ' 피습! ' + facName(u.f) + ' ' + uName(u) + ' (병력 -' + nf(td) + ', 내구 -' + nf(dd) + ')', b.c, b.r, b.owner, 2);
    if (b.troops <= 0 || b.dur <= 0) {
      res.fell = true;
      if (!ranged2) captureBase(b, u);
      else {   // 원거리로 무너뜨리면 빈 거점이 된다
        var old = b.owner, os = baseOfficers(b), i;
        for (i = 0; i < os.length; i++) fleeOfficer(os[i], b.c, b.r, b.id);
        var held = b.captives.slice(); for (i = 0; i < held.length; i++) releaseCaptive(off(held[i]));
        logMsg(b.n + ' 수비대 궤멸 — 빈 거점이 되었다', b.c, b.r, old, 2);
        b.owner = -1; b.troops = 0; b.dur = Math.max(b.dur, Math.round(b.maxDur * 0.3));
        checkFaction(old);
      }
      return res;
    }
    if (!ranged2 && b.troops > 0) { var cb = dmgBU(b, u); res.counter = cb; if (hurtUnit(u, cb, b.owner, null)) res.selfDead = true; }
    return res;
  }
  if (t.kind === 'fac') {
    var fc = t.b.plots[t.i].fac;
    if (!fc) return res;
    var fd = Math.round(uAtk(u) * 2.2 * troopFactor(u.troops));
    fc.hp -= fd; res.dmg = fd; res.tname = FAC[fc.t].name;
    if (fc.hp <= 0) { t.b.plots[t.i].fac = null; res.destroyed = true; u.gold += 100; logMsg(t.b.n + '의 ' + FAC[fc.t].name + ' 파괴됨', t.c, t.r, t.b.owner, 1); }
    return res;
  }
  return res;
}

/* 전법 */
function tacticList(u) {
  var list = TACTICS[u.type] || [], apt = uApt(u), out = [];
  for (var i = 0; i < list.length; i++) if (APT_RANK[apt] >= APT_RANK[list[i].need]) out.push(list[i]);
  return out;
}
function tacticCrit(u, d) {
  if (uHas(u, '신장') && uWar(u) > uWar(d)) return 1.3;
  if (u.type === 'spear' && (uHas(u, '창장') || uHas(u, '투신'))) return 1.3;
  if (u.type === 'halberd' && (uHas(u, '극장') || uHas(u, '투신'))) return 1.3;
  if (u.type === 'bow') { if (uHas(u, '궁신')) return 1.43; if (uHas(u, '궁장')) return 1.3; }
  if (u.type === 'cav') { if (uHas(u, '기신')) return 1.43; if (uHas(u, '기장')) return 1.3; }
  return 0;
}
/* 전법의 성공·대성공 확률(%). 대성공은 성공 안에 들어 있다(성공 80·대성공 15 → 실패 20, 보통 성공 65, 대성공 15).
   - 성공: 병과 적성 + 무력
   - 대성공: 병과 적성 + 무력 + (내 무력 − 상대 통솔). 특기(창장·기장·신장…)가 맞으면 반드시 대성공 */
var TACTIC_CRIT = { C: 4, B: 8, A: 12, S: 18 };
function uLdr(u) { return bestOf(uMembers(u), 'ldr'); }
function tacticOdds(u, d) {
  var forced = tacticCrit(u, d);
  if (forced) return { ok: 100, crit: 100, mult: forced, sure: true };
  var w = uWar(u), ok = Math.round(TACTIC_RATE[uApt(u)] + clamp((w - 70) / 3, -5, 8));
  var cr = Math.round(clamp(TACTIC_CRIT[uApt(u)] + clamp((w - 70) * 0.5, -4, 15) + clamp((w - uLdr(d)) * 0.15, -6, 6), 2, 45));
  return { ok: ok, crit: Math.min(cr, ok), mult: 1.3, sure: false };
}
/* 대성공이면 전법의 효과 자체가 세진다(TACTICS 의 great 가 덮어쓴다) */
function tacticEff(tac, crit) {
  if (!crit || !tac.great) return tac;
  var o = {}, k;
  for (k in tac) o[k] = tac[k];
  for (k in tac.great) o[k] = tac.great[k];
  return o;
}
/* 전법으로 혼란시킬 확률(%): 맞는 쪽의 지력·통솔이 높을수록 낮다. confuse 가 1 이상이면 반드시 */
function confuseOdds(e, d) {
  if (!e.confuse) return 0;
  if (e.confuse >= 1) return 100;
  return Math.round(clamp(e.confuse * 100 + (70 - (uInt(d) + uLdr(d)) / 2) * 0.6, 5, 95));
}
function tacticTargets(u, tac) {
  if (u.morale < tac.cost) return [];
  if (tac.noForest && terr(u.c, u.r) === 'f') return [];
  return attackTargets(u).filter(function (t) { return t.kind === 'unit' && !(tac.noForest && terr(t.c, t.r) === 'f'); });
}
function canPushInto(c, r, u) {
  if (!inMap(c, r)) return false;
  var t = terr(c, r), k = K(c, r);
  if (t === 'm' || t === 's' || t === 'x') return false;
  if (UAT[k] || BAT[k] !== undefined) return false;
  var pf = PAT[k]; if (pf && G.bases[pf.b].plots[pf.i].fac) return false;
  return true;
}
function behindHex(u, d) {
  var dist = hdist(u.c, u.r, d.c, d.r), dx = cubeX(d.c, d.r) - cubeX(u.c, u.r), dz = d.r - u.r;
  if (dist === 1) return stepDir(d.c, d.r, dx, dz);
  if (dx % 2 === 0 && dz % 2 === 0) return stepDir(d.c, d.r, dx / 2, dz / 2);
  var ns = nbrs(d.c, d.r), best = null, bd = 0;
  for (var i = 0; i < ns.length; i++) { var q = hdist(u.c, u.r, ns[i][0], ns[i][1]); if (q > bd) { bd = q; best = ns[i]; } }
  return best;
}
function unitTactic0(u, tac, t) {
  if (u.done) return ERR('이미 행동을 마쳤습니다');
  if (u.morale < tac.cost) return ERR('기력이 부족합니다');
  var d = t.u, ranged = hdist(u.c, u.r, d.c, d.r) > 1;
  var res = { ok: true, kind: 'tactic', tac: tac.name, c: d.c, r: d.r, ac: u.c, ar: u.r, tname: uName(d), extra: [] };
  u.done = true; u.ox = -1; u.morale -= tac.cost;
  var od = tacticOdds(u, d), roll = od.sure ? 0 : rnd() * 100;
  res.pOk = od.ok; res.pCrit = od.crit;
  if (roll >= od.ok) {
    res.fail = true;
    var fdm = defMods(d, dmgUU(u, d, 0.6, ranged), 'normal');
    res.dmg = fdm; res.killed = hurtUnit(d, fdm, u.f, u);
    if (!res.killed && !ranged && d.status !== 'confuse') { var fc = defMods(u, dmgUU(d, u, 0.4, false), 'normal'); res.counter = fc; if (hurtUnit(u, fc, d.f, d)) res.selfDead = true; }
    return res;
  }
  var crit = roll < od.crit ? od.mult : 0, e = tacticEff(tac, !!crit);      // e = 이번에 적용되는 효과(대성공이면 강화된 것)
  res.crit = !!crit;
  var cm = crit || 1;
  var dmg = defMods(d, dmgUU(u, d, tac.mult * cm, ranged), 'tactic');
  var oc = d.c, or_ = d.r, dx = cubeX(d.c, d.r) - cubeX(u.c, u.r), dz = d.r - u.r;
  // 부가 대상(먼저 목록을 잡아 둔다)
  var others = [], i, ns, q;
  if (tac.arc) { ns = nbrs(u.c, u.r); for (i = 0; i < ns.length; i++) { q = UAT[K(ns[i][0], ns[i][1])]; if (q && q.f !== u.f && q !== d && hdist(q.c, q.r, d.c, d.r) === 1) others.push([q, e.side || 0.8]); } }
  if (tac.all) { ns = nbrs(u.c, u.r); for (i = 0; i < ns.length; i++) { q = UAT[K(ns[i][0], ns[i][1])]; if (q && q.f !== u.f && q !== d) others.push([q, 1.0]); } }
  if (tac.pierce) { var bh = behindHex(u, d); if (bh && inMap(bh[0], bh[1])) { q = UAT[K(bh[0], bh[1])]; if (q && q.f !== u.f) others.push([q, e.side || 0.8]); } }
  if (tac.splash) { ns = nbrs(d.c, d.r); for (i = 0; i < ns.length; i++) { q = UAT[K(ns[i][0], ns[i][1])]; if (q && q.f !== u.f && q !== d) others.push([q, e.side || 0.6]); } }
  // 밀어내기
  if (e.push && !ranged) {
    for (i = 0; i < e.push; i++) {
      var nx = stepDir(d.c, d.r, dx, dz);
      if (canPushInto(nx[0], nx[1], d)) {
        var pc = d.c, pr = d.r;
        placeUnit(d, nx[0], nx[1]);
        res.pushed = (res.pushed || 0) + 1;
        if (tac.follow) placeUnit(u, pc, pr);
      } else { dmg = Math.round(dmg * 1.2); res.slam = true; break; }
    }
  }
  res.dmg = dmg; res.tc = d.c; res.tr = d.r;
  var dead = hurtUnit(d, dmg, u.f, u);
  afterHit(u, d, dmg);
  res.killed = dead;
  if (!dead) {
    if (e.moraleDmg) { var md = Math.min(d.morale, e.moraleDmg); d.morale -= md; if (md) res.mor = md; }
    var cp = confuseOdds(e, d);
    if (cp) { res.pConf = cp; if (cp >= 100 || chance(cp)) { setStatus(d, 'confuse', 1); res.confused = true; } }
  }
  if (tac.fire) { if (setFire(oc, or_, e.fireT || 2)) res.fire = true; }
  if (tac.pass && !ranged) {
    var bx = stepDir(oc, or_, dx, dz);
    if (canPushInto(bx[0], bx[1], u) && moveCost(u, bx[0], bx[1]) < 99) { placeUnit(u, bx[0], bx[1]); res.passed = true; }
  }
  for (i = 0; i < others.length; i++) {
    var o2 = others[i][0];
    if (!alive(o2) || !alive(u)) continue;
    var od = defMods(o2, dmgUU(u, o2, tac.mult * cm * others[i][1], hdist(u.c, u.r, o2.c, o2.r) > 1), 'tactic');
    var ex = { c: o2.c, r: o2.r, dmg: od };
    res.extra.push(ex);
    var dead2 = hurtUnit(o2, od, u.f, u); afterHit(u, o2, od);
    if (!dead2 && e.moraleAll) { var m2 = Math.min(o2.morale, e.moraleAll); o2.morale -= m2; if (m2) ex.mor = m2; }
  }
  res.uc = u.c; res.ur = u.r;
  if (!dead && !tac.noCounter && !ranged && alive(u) && d.status !== 'confuse' && d.status !== 'rumor' && hdist(u.c, u.r, d.c, d.r) === 1) {
    var cd = defMods(u, dmgUU(d, u, 0.25, false), 'normal');
    res.counter = cd;
    if (hurtUnit(u, cd, d.f, d)) res.selfDead = true;
  }
  if (!ranged) tryDuel(u, d, res);
  return res;
}

/* 계략 */
function ployRange(u) { return uHas(u, '귀모') ? 2 : 1; }
function ployTargets(u, ploy) {
  var out = [], rg = ployRange(u), c, r;
  if (u.morale < ploy.cost) return out;
  if (ploy.id === 'douse') { var any = G.fires[K(u.c, u.r)]; nbrs(u.c, u.r).forEach(function (n) { if (G.fires[K(n[0], n[1])]) any = true; }); return any ? [{ c: u.c, r: u.r }] : []; }
  for (c = Math.max(0, u.c - rg - 1); c <= Math.min(W - 1, u.c + rg + 1); c++) for (r = Math.max(0, u.r - rg); r <= Math.min(H - 1, u.r + rg); r++) {
    var d = hdist(u.c, u.r, c, r);
    if (d < 1 || d > rg) continue;
    var k = K(c, r), ou = UAT[k];
    if (ploy.id === 'fire') {
      var t = terr(c, r);
      if (t === 'w' || t === 's' || t === 'm' || t === 'x' || BAT[k] !== undefined) continue;
      if (ou && ou.f === u.f) continue;
      var pf = PAT[k], enemyFac = pf && G.bases[pf.b].plots[pf.i].fac && G.bases[pf.b].owner !== u.f;
      if (!ou && !enemyFac) continue;
      out.push({ c: c, r: r, u: ou || null });
    } else if (ou && ou.f !== u.f && !ou.status) out.push({ c: c, r: r, u: ou });
  }
  return out;
}
function ployChance(u, ploy, tu) {
  var it = uInt(u), base = ploy.id === 'fire' ? 55 : (ploy.id === 'confuse' ? 45 : 40), mul = ploy.id === 'fire' ? 1.2 : 1.5;
  if (ploy.id === 'douse') return 100;
  if (!tu) return 92;
  var ti = uInt(tu);
  if (uHas(tu, '통찰')) return 0;
  if (ploy.id !== 'fire' && uHas(tu, '명경')) return 0;
  if (uHas(tu, '간파') && ti > it) return 0;
  if (ploy.id === 'fire' && uHas(u, '화신')) return 100;
  if (uHas(u, '신산') && it > ti) return 100;
  var p = base + (it - ti) * mul;
  if (uHas(u, '허실')) p += 20;
  if (ploy.id === 'fire' && uHas(u, '화공')) p += 20;
  return clamp(Math.round(p), 5, 95);
}
function fireDamage(u) {
  var v = 250 + uInt(u) * 3;
  if (uHas(u, '화공')) v *= 1.5;
  if (uHas(u, '화신')) v *= 2;
  if (uHas(u, '심모')) v *= 1.5;
  return Math.round(v * (0.9 + rnd() * 0.2));
}
function applyPloy(u, ploy, c, r, tu, res) {
  var ext = uHas(u, '심모') ? 1 : 0;
  if (ploy.id === 'fire') {
    setFire(c, r, 3 + ext);
    if (tu && alive(tu) && !uHas(tu, '화신')) { var fd = fireDamage(u); res.dmg = (res.dmg || 0) + fd; tu.morale = Math.max(0, tu.morale - 5); res.killed = hurtUnit(tu, fd, u.f, u) || res.killed; }
    var pf = PAT[K(c, r)]; if (pf) { var fc = G.bases[pf.b].plots[pf.i].fac; if (fc) { fc.hp -= 300; if (fc.hp <= 0) G.bases[pf.b].plots[pf.i].fac = null; } }
  } else if (tu && alive(tu)) setStatus(tu, ploy.id, 2 + ext);
}
function unitPloy0(u, ploy, t) {
  if (u.done) return ERR('이미 행동을 마쳤습니다');
  if (u.morale < ploy.cost) return ERR('기력이 부족합니다');
  u.done = true; u.ox = -1; u.morale -= ploy.cost;
  var res = { ok: true, kind: 'ploy', ploy: ploy.name, c: t.c, r: t.r, ac: u.c, ar: u.r };
  if (ploy.id === 'douse') { delete G.fires[K(u.c, u.r)]; nbrs(u.c, u.r).forEach(function (n) { delete G.fires[K(n[0], n[1])]; }); res.success = true; return res; }
  var tu = t.u || null, p = ployChance(u, ploy, tu);
  res.p = p;
  if (chance(p)) {
    res.success = true;
    applyPloy(u, ploy, t.c, t.r, tu, res);
    addMerit(u, 30);
    if (uHas(u, '연환') && alive(u)) {
      var ns = nbrs(t.c, t.r);
      for (var i = 0; i < ns.length; i++) { var q = UAT[K(ns[i][0], ns[i][1])]; if (q && q.f !== u.f && !q.status) { applyPloy(u, ploy, q.c, q.r, q, res); res.chain = true; break; } }
    }
  } else {
    res.success = false;
    if (tu && uHas(tu, '반계') && alive(u)) {
      res.reflected = true;
      if (ploy.id === 'fire') { setFire(u.c, u.r, 2); if (!uHas(u, '화신')) { var rd = fireDamage(tu); res.selfDmg = rd; if (hurtUnit(u, rd, tu.f, tu)) res.selfDead = true; } }
      else if (!uHas(u, '통찰') && !uHas(u, '명경')) setStatus(u, ploy.id, 2);
    }
  }
  return res;
}

/* ---------- 저장 ---------- */
/* 행동 + 연출용 사건 기록. tf = 맞은 쪽 세력(행동 전 기준) */
function actTargetF(t) { return t && t.u ? t.u.f : (t && t.b ? t.b.owner : -1); }
function actEv(u, t, res, f, name, ac, ar, tf) {
  if (res && res.ok) {
    // 화면 연출에 쓰는 정보: 공격한 부대·맞은 부대의 번호와 병과, 행동 뒤의 자리
    res.aid = u.id; res.atype = u.type; res.af = f; res.ac0 = ac; res.ar0 = ar;
    res.ac1 = u.c; res.ar1 = u.r; res.aAlive = !!UMAP[u.id];
    res.tk = t ? t.kind : ''; res.tid = t && t.u ? t.u.id : -1; res.tbase = t && t.b ? t.b.id : -1; res.tf = tf;
    if (t && t.u) { res.tc1 = t.u.c; res.tr1 = t.u.r; res.tAlive = !!UMAP[t.u.id]; res.ttype = t.u.type; }
    ev({ t: 'act', f: f, name: name, ac: ac, ar: ar, tc: t && t.c, tr: t && t.r, tf: tf, res: res });
  }
  return res;
}
function unitAttack(u, t) { var f = u.f, n = uName(u), ac = u.c, ar = u.r, tf = actTargetF(t); return actEv(u, t, unitAttack0(u, t), f, n, ac, ar, tf); }
function unitTactic(u, tac, t) { var f = u.f, n = uName(u), ac = u.c, ar = u.r, tf = actTargetF(t); return actEv(u, t, unitTactic0(u, tac, t), f, n, ac, ar, tf); }
function unitPloy(u, ploy, t) { var f = u.f, n = uName(u), ac = u.c, ar = u.r, tf = actTargetF(t); return actEv(u, t, unitPloy0(u, ploy, t), f, n, ac, ar, tf); }

function serialize() { return JSON.stringify(G); }
function deserialize(s) {
  var g = JSON.parse(s);
  if (!g || g.ver !== 2) return false;
  // 초상은 저장본이 아니라 지금 판의 명부를 따른다(예전 저장을 열어도 새로 그린 초상이 나오게)
  for (var i = 0; i < g.officers.length; i++) { var so = SCENARIO.officers[i]; if (so && so.n === g.officers[i].n) g.officers[i].por = so.por; }
  // 세력 색도 지금 판의 색(석채 빛)을 따른다
  for (var j = 0; j < g.factions.length; j++) { var sf = SCENARIO.factions[j]; if (sf && sf.n === g.factions[j].name) g.factions[j].color = sf.color; }
  G = g; reindex(); REPORT = []; EVQ = [];
  return true;
}

=====8<===== src/ai.js
/* ===== 천하도 — AI ===== */
function estUU(a, d, mult, ranged) {
  var x = 620 * Math.pow(uAtk(a) / Math.max(10, uDef(d)), 0.9) * troopFactor(a.troops) * mult;
  var c = (COMPAT[a.type] && COMPAT[a.type][d.type]) || 1;
  if (VS_TYPE[d.type] && (!ranged || d.type === 'trans' || d.type === 'ram')) c *= VS_TYPE[d.type];
  x *= c;
  var td = terr(d.c, d.r);
  if (td === 'f' || td === 'h') x *= 0.85; else if (td === 'w') x *= 1.2;
  if (d.status === 'confuse') x *= 1.25;
  return x;
}
function enemyUnitsNear(f, c, r, rad) {
  var out = [];
  for (var i = 0; i < G.units.length; i++) { var u = G.units[i]; if (u.f !== f && hdist(c, r, u.c, u.r) <= rad) out.push(u); }
  return out;
}
function aiMilScore(o) { return o.war * 0.55 + o.ldr * 0.45; }

function aiChooseType(b, leader, want) {
  var best = 'sword', bs = -1, types = ['spear', 'halberd', 'bow', 'cav'];
  for (var i = 0; i < types.length; i++) {
    var t = types[i], wp = UNIT_TYPES[t].weapon, stock = b.w[wp];
    if (stock < Math.min(want, 1500)) continue;
    var s = APT_RANK[leader.apt.charAt(APT_IDX[t])] * 10 + (t === 'cav' ? 3 : t === 'spear' ? 2 : 1) + Math.min(1, stock / want) * 6;
    if (s > bs) { bs = s; best = t; }
  }
  return best;
}
function aiDeploy(b, leader, subs, troops, turns, ai, toward, forceType) {
  var type = forceType || aiChooseType(b, leader, troops);
  var wp = UNIT_TYPES[type].weapon;
  troops = Math.min(troops, cmdCap(leader), b.troops);
  if (wp && wp !== 'ram') troops = Math.min(troops, b.w[wp]);
  troops = Math.floor(troops / 100) * 100;
  if (troops < 500) return null;
  var food = Math.min(Math.ceil(troops / 20) * turns, Math.floor(b.food * 0.6));
  var res = cmdDeploy(b, { type: type, ids: [leader.id].concat(subs.map(function (o) { return o.id; })), troops: troops, food: food, toward: toward });
  if (!res.ok) return null;
  res.unit.ai = ai;
  return res.unit;
}

/* ---------- 부대 행동 ---------- */
function aiUnitAct(u) {
  if (!alive(u) || u.done) return;
  var f = u.f, ai = u.ai || (u.ai = { mode: 'return' });
  var T = (ai.target !== undefined && ai.target >= 0) ? G.bases[ai.target] : null;
  var mode = ai.mode;
  if (u.type === 'trans') mode = 'supply';
  if (mode === 'attack') { if (!T) mode = 'return'; else if (T.owner === f) mode = 'garrison'; }
  if (mode === 'defend') {
    var home = G.bases[ai.home];
    if (!home || home.owner !== f || enemyUnitsNear(f, home.c, home.r, 5).length === 0) mode = 'return';
  }
  var nbH = nearestOwnBase(f, u.c, u.r), tHome = nbH ? Math.ceil(hdist(u.c, u.r, nbH.c, nbH.r) / 4.5) + 1 : 2;
  var lowFood = u.food < Math.ceil(u.troops / 20) * tHome, weak = u.troops < Math.max(600, u.max * 0.2);
  if ((lowFood || weak) && mode !== 'supply' && mode !== 'garrison') mode = 'return';

  var rc = unitReach(u), k, c, r, i;
  var stops = [K(u.c, u.r)];
  for (k in rc.stop) { k = +k; if (BAT[k] === undefined && !G.fires[k]) stops.push(k); }

  // 1) 공격 기회
  var best = null, bv = 0, rg = uRange(u);
  if (mode !== 'supply') {
    var en = enemyUnitsNear(f, u.c, u.r, 9);
    var onlyHere = (mode === 'return');
    for (i = 0; i < en.length; i++) {
      var e = en[i];
      for (var s = 0; s < stops.length; s++) {
        if (onlyHere && s > 0) break;
        k = stops[s]; c = k % W; r = (k - c) / W;
        var d = hdist(c, r, e.c, e.r);
        if (d < 1 || d > rg) continue;
        var ranged = d > 1;
        var dmg = estUU(u, e, 1.2, ranged);
        var v = dmg + (dmg >= e.troops ? 600 : 0) + (e.type === 'trans' ? 300 : 0);
        if (!ranged && e.status !== 'confuse') v -= estUU(e, u, 0.55, false) * 0.6;
        if (rg > 1 && ranged) v += 80;
        if (terr(c, r) === 'w') v -= 200;
        if (T && mode === 'attack') v -= hdist(c, r, T.c, T.r) * 4;
        if (v > bv) { bv = v; best = { kind: 'unit', u: e, k: k }; }
      }
    }
    if (mode === 'attack' && T && T.owner >= 0 && T.owner !== f) {
      for (s = 0; s < stops.length; s++) {
        k = stops[s]; c = k % W; r = (k - c) / W;
        var db = hdist(c, r, T.c, T.r);
        if (db < 1 || db > rg) continue;
        if (ZONE[k] === T.id && (ZONE[K(u.c, u.r)] !== T.id || u.zin === T.id)) continue;   // 성역에 막 들어선 턴에는 그 성을 칠 수 없다
        var vb = 260 + durDmgUB(u) * 0.5 + (terr(c, r) === 'w' ? -150 : 0) + (db > 1 ? 60 : 0);
        if (T.troops < 800) vb += 500;
        if (vb > bv) { bv = vb; best = { kind: 'base', b: T, k: k }; }
      }
    }
    // 길을 막는 적 시설
    if (!best && mode === 'attack') {
      var ats = attackTargets(u);
      for (i = 0; i < ats.length; i++) if (ats[i].kind === 'fac' && chance(15)) { best = { kind: 'fac', t: ats[i], k: K(u.c, u.r) }; bv = 1; break; }
    }
  }
  if (best && bv > 0) {
    if (best.k !== K(u.c, u.r)) { c = best.k % W; r = (best.k - c) / W; var mv = unitMove(u, c, r, rc); if (!mv.ok || !alive(u)) return; }
    if (best.kind === 'base') { unitAttack(u, { kind: 'base', b: best.b, c: best.b.c, r: best.b.r }); return; }
    if (best.kind === 'fac') { unitAttack(u, best.t); return; }
    var tg = best.u;
    if (!alive(tg)) { unitWait(u); return; }
    var tgt = { kind: 'unit', u: tg, c: tg.c, r: tg.r };
    // 계략
    if (uInt(u) >= 78 && u.morale >= 30 && hdist(u.c, u.r, tg.c, tg.r) <= ployRange(u) && chance(30)) {
      var pl = (!tg.status && chance(50)) ? PLOYS[1] : PLOYS[0];
      var pts = ployTargets(u, pl), pt = null;
      for (i = 0; i < pts.length; i++) if (pts[i].u === tg) pt = pts[i];
      if (pt && ployChance(u, pl, tg) >= 55) { aiReport(u, unitPloy(u, pl, pt), tg); return; }
    }
    // 전법
    var tl = tacticList(u), bt = null, bm = 0;
    for (i = 0; i < tl.length; i++) {
      if (u.morale < tl[i].cost) continue;
      var tt = tacticTargets(u, tl[i]), okT = false;
      for (var q = 0; q < tt.length; q++) if (tt[q].u === tg) okT = true;
      if (!okT) continue;
      var m = tl[i].mult + (tl[i].all || tl[i].splash || tl[i].arc ? 0.2 : 0);
      if (m > bm) { bm = m; bt = tl[i]; }
    }
    if (bt && chance(75)) { aiReport(u, unitTactic(u, bt, tgt), tg); return; }
    aiReport(u, unitAttack(u, tgt), tg);
    return;
  }

  // 2) 목표로 이동
  var goalFn = null, gb = null, hint = null;
  if (mode === 'attack') {
    if (T.owner === -1) { gb = T; }
    else { var tc = T.c, tr = T.r; goalFn = function (kk) { var cc = kk % W, rr = (kk - cc) / W; return BAT[kk] === undefined && hdist(cc, rr, tc, tr) <= rg; }; hint = [tc, tr, rg]; }
  } else if (mode === 'defend') {
    var hm = G.bases[ai.home], es = enemyUnitsNear(f, hm.c, hm.r, 5);
    if (es.length) { var ex = es[0]; goalFn = function (kk) { var cc = kk % W, rr = (kk - cc) / W; return BAT[kk] === undefined && hdist(cc, rr, ex.c, ex.r) <= 1; }; hint = [ex.c, ex.r, 1]; }
  }
  if (!goalFn && !gb) {
    if ((mode === 'garrison' || mode === 'supply') && T && T.owner === f) gb = T;
    else gb = nearestOwnBase(f, u.c, u.r);
    if (!gb) { unitWait(u); return; }
  }
  if (gb) { var gk = K(gb.c, gb.r); goalFn = function (kk) { return kk === gk; }; hint = [gb.c, gb.r, 0]; }
  var path = longPath(u, goalFn, 0, hint);
  if (!path) { ai.stuck = (ai.stuck || 0) + 1; if (ai.stuck > 3 && mode === 'attack') ai.mode = 'return'; unitWait(u); return; }
  var dest = -1;
  for (i = 0; i < path.length; i++) { k = path[i]; if (rc.stop[k] && (!G.fires[k] || BAT[k] !== undefined)) dest = k; }
  if (dest >= 0) {
    c = dest % W; r = (dest - c) / W;
    var res = unitMove(u, c, r, rc);
    if (!res.ok) { unitWait(u); return; }
    if (res.captured !== undefined || res.entered !== undefined) return;
    ai.stuck = 0;
  } else ai.stuck = (ai.stuck || 0) + 1;
  if (ai.stuck > 5 && mode === 'attack') ai.mode = 'return';
  if (alive(u)) unitWait(u);
}
function aiReport(u, res, tg) {
  if (!res || !res.ok || G.aiOnly) return;
  if (tg && tg.f === G.player) {
    var txt = facName(u.f) + ' ' + uLeader(u).n + '대';
    if (res.kind === 'ploy') txt += '의 ' + res.ploy + (res.success ? ' 성공' : ' 실패') + ' → ' + (uLeaderSafe(tg)) + (res.dmg ? ' (-' + nf(res.dmg) + ')' : '');
    else if (res.kind === 'tactic') txt += '의 ' + res.tac + (res.fail ? ' 실패' : (res.crit ? ' 대성공' : '')) + ' → ' + uLeaderSafe(tg) + ' (-' + nf(res.dmg) + ')';
    else txt += '의 공격 → ' + uLeaderSafe(tg) + ' (-' + nf(res.dmg) + ')';
    if (res.duel) txt += ' · 일기토 ' + (res.duel.win ? res.duel.a : res.duel.d) + ' 승';
    if (res.killed) txt += ' 궤멸';
    logMsg(txt, res.c, res.r, G.player, 1);
  }
}
function uLeaderSafe(u) { return (u && u.off && u.off.length) ? off(u.off[0]).n + '대' : '아군'; }
function aiUnits(f) {
  var us = facUnits(f);
  us.sort(function (a, b) { return a.id - b.id; });
  for (var i = 0; i < us.length; i++) { if (G.over) return; aiUnitAct(us[i]); }
}

/* ---------- 내정 ---------- */
function aiCountFac(b) {
  var n = { market: 0, farm: 0, barracks: 0, smithy: 0, stable: 0, workshop: 0, mint: 0, granary: 0 };
  for (var i = 0; i < b.plots.length; i++) if (b.plots[i].fac) n[b.plots[i].fac.t]++;
  return n;
}
function aiPickFacility(b) {
  var n = aiCountFac(b);
  if (!n.barracks) return 'barracks';
  if (!n.market) return 'market';
  if (!n.farm) return 'farm';
  if (!n.smithy) return 'smithy';
  if (n.market < 2) return 'market';
  if (n.farm < 2) return 'farm';
  if (!n.stable && b.plots.length >= 8) return 'stable';
  if (n.market < 3) return 'market';
  if (n.farm < 3) return 'farm';
  if (!n.mint && b.plots.length >= 8) return 'mint';
  if (!n.granary && b.plots.length >= 8) return 'granary';
  if (!n.workshop && b.plots.length >= 10) return 'workshop';
  return n.market <= n.farm ? 'market' : 'farm';
}
function aiPickPlot(b, type) {
  var free = [], i;
  for (i = 0; i < b.plots.length; i++) if (!b.plots[i].fac && !UAT[K(b.plots[i].c, b.plots[i].r)]) free.push(i);
  if (!free.length) return -1;
  if (type === 'mint' || type === 'granary') {
    var want = type === 'mint' ? 'market' : 'farm', bi = free[0], bn = -1;
    for (i = 0; i < free.length; i++) {
      var p = b.plots[free[i]], ns = nbrs(p.c, p.r), cnt = 0;
      for (var j = 0; j < ns.length; j++) { var q = PAT[K(ns[j][0], ns[j][1])]; if (q && q.b === b.id) { var fc = b.plots[q.i].fac; if (fc && fc.t === want) cnt++; } }
      if (cnt > bn) { bn = cnt; bi = free[i]; }
    }
    return bi;
  }
  return free[0];
}
function aiCaptives(f) {
  var F = G.factions[f], bs = facBases(f);
  for (var bi = 0; bi < bs.length; bi++) {
    var b = bs[bi], caps = b.captives.slice();
    for (var i = 0; i < caps.length; i++) {
      var cp = off(caps[i]), os = idleOfficers(b);
      b.ai.capTry = b.ai.capTry || {};
      var n = b.ai.capTry[cp.id] = (b.ai.capTry[cp.id] || 0) + 1;
      if (os.length && F.ap >= AP.recruit) {
        os.sort(function (a, c2) { return c2.cha - a.cha; });
        var rr = cmdRecruit(b, os[0].id, cp.id);
        if (rr.ok && rr.joined) { delete b.ai.capTry[cp.id]; continue; }
      }
      if (n >= 3) { cmdCaptive(b, cp.id, 'release'); delete b.ai.capTry[cp.id]; }
    }
  }
}
function aiInternal(f) {
  var F = G.factions[f], cities = facCities(f), ci, i;
  for (ci = 0; ci < cities.length; ci++) {
    var b = cities[ci];
    var threatened = enemyUnitsNear(f, b.c, b.r, 4).length > 0;
    var idle = function () { return idleOfficers(b); };
    var os, o;
    // 포상
    os = baseOfficers(b);
    for (i = 0; i < os.length; i++) if (os[i].loy < 72 && !isLord(os[i]) && b.gold >= 250 && F.ap >= AP.reward) cmdReward(b, os[i].id);
    // 재야 등용
    var fr = freeOfficers(b);
    for (i = 0; i < fr.length; i++) { os = idle(); if (!os.length || F.ap < AP.recruit) break; os.sort(function (a, c2) { return c2.cha - a.cha; }); cmdRecruit(b, os[0].id, fr[i].id); }
    // 순찰
    if (b.order < 60 && b.gold >= 150 && F.ap >= AP.patrol) { os = idle(); if (os.length) { os.sort(function (a, c2) { return Math.max(c2.war, c2.ldr) - Math.max(a.war, a.ldr); }); cmdPatrol(b, [os[0].id]); } }
    // 개발
    for (var rep = 0; rep < 2; rep++) {
      os = idle(); if (!os.length || F.ap < AP.develop + 10) break;
      var type = aiPickFacility(b), pi = aiPickPlot(b, type);
      if (pi < 0 || b.gold < FAC[type].cost + (threatened ? 400 : 100)) break;
      os.sort(function (a, c2) { return c2.pol - a.pol; });
      cmdDevelop(b, pi, type, os.slice(0, Math.min(2, os.length)).map(function (x) { return x.id; }));
    }
    // 흡수합병: 부지가 다 찼으면 붙어 있는 같은 시설을 합친다
    if (F.ap >= AP.merge + 10 && b.gold >= 600 && !b.plots.some(function (p) { return !p.fac; })) {
      for (var mp = 0; mp < b.plots.length; mp++) {
        var mc = mergeCandidates(b, mp);
        if (!mc.length) continue;
        os = idle(); if (os.length) { os.sort(function (a, c2) { return c2.pol - a.pol; }); cmdMerge(b, mp, mc[0], [os[0].id]); }
        break;
      }
    }
    // 징병
    var cap = Math.min(45000, 10000 + baseOfficers(b).length * 3500);
    if (!threatened && !aiIsFront(f, b)) cap = Math.min(cap, 8000);
    if (hasFac(b, 'barracks') && b.gold >= 420 && b.order >= 55 && b.troops < cap && b.food >= Math.ceil((b.troops + 2500) / 60) * 10 && F.ap >= AP.draft) {
      os = idle(); if (os.length) { os.sort(function (a, c2) { return c2.cha - a.cha; }); cmdDraft(b, os.slice(0, Math.min(2, os.length)).map(function (x) { return x.id; })); }
    }
    // 생산
    if (b.gold >= 380 && F.ap >= AP.produce) {
      os = idle();
      if (os.length) {
        os.sort(function (a, c2) { return c2.int - a.int; });
        var tot = b.w.spear + b.w.halberd + b.w.bow;
        if (hasFac(b, 'smithy') && tot < b.troops * 0.9) {
          var lead = baseOfficers(b).sort(function (a, c2) { return aiMilScore(c2) - aiMilScore(a); })[0];
          var kinds = ['spear', 'halberd', 'bow'], bk = 'spear', bsc = -1;
          for (i = 0; i < 3; i++) { var sc = APT_RANK[lead.apt.charAt(i)] * 3 - b.w[kinds[i]] / 4000 + (i === 0 ? 0.5 : 0); if (sc > bsc) { bsc = sc; bk = kinds[i]; } }
          cmdProduce(b, bk, [os[0].id]);
        } else if (hasFac(b, 'stable') && b.w.horse < b.troops * 0.35) cmdProduce(b, 'horse', [os[0].id]);
        else if (hasFac(b, 'workshop') && b.w.ram < 1 && b.gold >= 900) cmdProduce(b, 'ram', [os[0].id]);
      }
    }
    // 훈련
    if (b.morale < 75 && b.troops >= 2000 && F.ap >= AP.train) { os = idle(); if (os.length) { os.sort(function (a, c2) { return c2.ldr - a.ldr; }); cmdTrain(b, [os[0].id]); } }
    // 탐색
    if (F.ap >= 60 && chance(35)) { os = idle(); if (os.length >= 2) { os.sort(function (a, c2) { return c2.pol - a.pol; }); cmdSearch(b, os[0].id); } }
    // 거래
    if (hasFac(b, 'market') && F.ap >= AP.trade) {
      if (b.food < b.troops * 0.35 && b.gold >= 600) cmdTrade(b, 'buy', Math.min(400, b.gold - 300));
      else if (b.gold < 200 && b.food > b.troops * 1.2 + 6000) cmdTrade(b, 'sell', 200);
    }
  }
}

/* ---------- 방어·공격·보급 ---------- */
function aiDefense(f) {
  var F = G.factions[f], bs = facBases(f);
  for (var bi = 0; bi < bs.length; bi++) {
    var b = bs[bi];
    var en = enemyUnitsNear(f, b.c, b.r, 4);
    if (!en.length || b.troops < 4000) continue;
    var et = 0; en.forEach(function (e) { et += e.troops; });
    var mine = 0; G.units.forEach(function (u) { if (u.f === f && hdist(u.c, u.r, b.c, b.r) <= 4) mine += u.troops; });
    var n = 0;
    while (n < 3 && F.ap >= AP.deploy && b.troops - 2000 >= 1500 && mine < et * 1.3) {
      var os = idleOfficers(b).filter(function (o) { return !isLord(o) || b.troops > 12000; });
      if (!os.length) break;
      os.sort(function (a, c) { return aiMilScore(c) - aiMilScore(a); });
      var u = aiDeploy(b, os[0], [], b.troops - 2000, 6, { mode: 'defend', home: b.id }, en[0]);
      if (!u) break;
      mine += u.troops; n++;
    }
  }
}
function aiTargets(f, b) {
  var out = [], seen = {};
  seen[b.id] = 1;
  function add(id, len) {
    if (seen[id]) return; seen[id] = 1;
    var T = G.bases[id];
    if (T.owner === f) { if (T.kind === 'gate') ADJ[id].forEach(function (e) { add(e.to, len + e.len); }); return; }
    out.push({ T: T, len: len });
  }
  ADJ[b.id].forEach(function (e) { add(e.to, e.len); });
  return out;
}
function aiOffense(f) {
  var F = G.factions[f], cities = facCities(f);
  for (var ci = 0; ci < cities.length; ci++) {
    var b = cities[ci];
    if (F.ap < AP.deploy * 2) return;
    if (enemyUnitsNear(f, b.c, b.r, 5).length) continue;
    var idle = idleOfficers(b);
    if (!idle.length) continue;
    var tg = aiTargets(f, b), best = null, bs = -1e9, i;
    var hasLord = baseOfficers(b).some(isLord);
    var reserve = Math.max(hasLord ? 4000 : 2500, Math.round(b.troops * 0.25));
    var avail = b.troops - reserve;
    for (i = 0; i < tg.length; i++) {
      var T = tg[i].T, len = tg[i].len, sc, need;
      if (T.owner === -1) { need = 600; sc = 90 - len * 0.5 + (T.kind === 'city' ? 10 : 0); }
      else {
        var def = T.troops;
        enemyUnitsNear(f, T.c, T.r, 3).forEach(function (e) { if (e.f === T.owner) def += e.troops; });
        need = def * 1.25 + 2500 + (T.kind === 'gate' ? 1500 : 0);
        if (avail >= 12000 && avail < need && aiFacTroops(f) >= def * 4 && chance(50)) need = avail;   // 국력이 압도적이면 파상 공세
        sc = 50 - def / 400 - len * 0.5 + (T.kind === 'city' ? 15 : 0) + (T.sz === 'L' ? 8 : 0) + (T.owner === G.player ? 3 : 0);
        if (b.morale < 55 || (b.ai.cool || 0) > G.turn) continue;
      }
      // 이미 그 거점으로 향하는 아군이 충분하면 건너뛴다
      var going = 0; G.units.forEach(function (u) { if (u.f === f && u.ai && u.ai.mode === 'attack' && u.ai.target === T.id) going += u.troops; });
      if (T.owner === -1 && going > 0) continue;
      if (avail + going < need) continue;
      if (going >= need) continue;
      if (sc > bs) { bs = sc; best = tg[i]; best.need = need; }
    }
    if (!best) continue;
    var Tb = best.T, turns = Math.ceil(best.len / 4) + 8;
    if (Tb.owner === -1) {
      if (avail < 800) continue;
      idle.sort(function (a, c) { return (c.pol + c.ldr) - (a.pol + a.ldr); });
      var gov = idle[0];
      if (isLord(gov) && idle.length > 1) gov = idle[1];
      aiDeploy(b, gov, [], Math.min(3000, Math.max(800, avail)), turns, { mode: 'attack', target: Tb.id, home: b.id }, Tb);
      continue;
    }
    idle = idle.filter(function (o) { return !isLord(o) || facCities(f).length === 1 && b.troops > 15000; });
    idle.sort(function (a, c) { return aiMilScore(c) - aiMilScore(a); });
    var nUnits = Math.min(idle.length, 5, Math.ceil(best.need / 5000) + 1), sent = 0, cnt = 0;
    if (b.w.ram >= 1 && idle.length > nUnits && avail > 6000) {
      var ro = idle.pop();
      if (aiDeploy(b, ro, [], 1500, turns, { mode: 'attack', target: Tb.id, home: b.id }, Tb, 'ram')) avail -= 1500;
    }
    for (i = 0; i < nUnits && F.ap >= AP.deploy; i++) {
      var left = b.troops - reserve;
      if (left < 1500) break;
      if (!idle[i]) break;
      var subs = (i === 0 && idle.length > nUnits + 1) ? [idle.pop()] : [];
      var u = aiDeploy(b, idle[i], subs, Math.floor(left / (nUnits - i)), turns, { mode: 'attack', target: Tb.id, home: b.id }, Tb);
      if (u) { sent += u.troops; cnt++; }
    }
    if (cnt) {
      b.ai.cool = G.turn + 8;
      if (Tb.owner === G.player) {
        logMsg(facName(f) + '군이 ' + b.n + '에서 ' + Tb.n + ' 방면으로 출진 (' + cnt + '부대)', b.c, b.r, G.player, 2);
        ev({ t: 'news', f: f, c: b.c, r: b.r, warn: 1, text: '<b>' + facName(f) + '군이 ' + b.n + '에서 우리 ' + Tb.n + ' 방면으로 출진</b> (' + cnt + '부대)' });
      } else logMsg(facName(f) + '군이 ' + b.n + '에서 ' + Tb.n + (Tb.owner >= 0 ? '(' + facName(Tb.owner) + '군)' : '') + ' 방면으로 출진', b.c, b.r, f, 1);
    }
  }
}
function aiFacTroops(f) { var t = 0; G.bases.forEach(function (b) { if (b.owner === f) t += b.troops; }); return t; }
function aiIsFront(f, b) {
  if (enemyUnitsNear(f, b.c, b.r, 6).length) return true;
  return aiTargets(f, b).length > 0;
}
function aiLogistics(f) {
  var F = G.factions[f];
  var cities = facCities(f);
  if (cities.length < 2) return;
  var ci, b;
  // 1) 태수 없는 도시에 한 명 보낸다
  var moved = 0;
  for (ci = 0; ci < cities.length && moved < 2; ci++) {
    b = cities[ci];
    if (baseOfficers(b).length > 0 || F.ap < AP.move) continue;
    var coming = G.officers.some(function (o) { return o.st === 'travel' && o.f === f && o.dest === b.id; });
    if (coming) continue;
    var src = null, sd = 1e9;
    cities.forEach(function (c2) { if (c2 === b) return; var n = idleOfficers(c2).filter(function (o) { return !isLord(o); }).length; if (n >= 1 && baseOfficers(c2).length >= 3) { var d = hdist(b.c, b.r, c2.c, c2.r); if (d < sd) { sd = d; src = c2; } } });
    if (!src) continue;
    var cand = idleOfficers(src).filter(function (o) { return !isLord(o); });
    cand.sort(function (a, c) { return (c.pol - aiMilScore(c) * 0.5) - (a.pol - aiMilScore(a) * 0.5); });
    if (cmdMoveOfficers(src, [cand[0].id], b.id).ok) moved++;
  }
  if ((G.turn + f) % 3 !== 0) return;
  var front = cities.filter(function (c3) { return aiIsFront(f, c3); });
  if (!front.length) return;
  for (ci = 0; ci < cities.length; ci++) {
    b = cities[ci];
    if (front.indexOf(b) >= 0 || F.ap < AP.deploy + 10) continue;
    var dest = null, bd = 1e9;
    front.forEach(function (fb) { var d = hdist(b.c, b.r, fb.c, fb.r) + fb.troops / 3000; if (d < bd) { bd = d; dest = fb; } });
    if (!dest) continue;
    var here = baseOfficers(b).length;
    var idle = idleOfficers(b).filter(function (o) { return !isLord(o); });
    // 2) 병력·물자 수송 (태수 한 명은 남긴다)
    if (b.troops >= 6000 && idle.length && here >= 2) {
      idle.sort(function (a, c) { return aiMilScore(a) - aiMilScore(c); });
      var keep = 3000, troops = b.troops - keep;
      var gold = Math.max(0, Math.floor((b.gold - 800) * 0.6)), food = Math.max(0, Math.floor((b.food - Math.ceil(keep / 60) * 30) * 0.5));
      var cargo = { spear: Math.floor(b.w.spear * 0.6), halberd: Math.floor(b.w.halberd * 0.6), bow: Math.floor(b.w.bow * 0.6), horse: Math.floor(b.w.horse * 0.6), ram: b.w.ram };
      var res = cmdDeploy(b, { type: 'trans', ids: [idle[0].id], troops: troops, food: food, gold: gold, cargo: cargo, toward: dest });
      if (res.ok) { res.unit.ai = { mode: 'supply', target: dest.id, home: b.id }; idle.shift(); here--; }
    } else if (F.ap >= 10 && (b.gold > 2500 || b.food > 30000)) {
      // 3) 후방의 남는 금·병량은 전선으로 보낸다 (AI 전용 간이 수송)
      var g2 = Math.max(0, Math.floor((b.gold - 1200) * 0.7)), f2 = Math.max(0, Math.floor((b.food - 15000) * 0.6));
      b.gold -= g2; b.food -= f2; dest.gold += g2; dest.food += f2; F.ap -= 10;
    }
    if (idle.length > 1 && here > 3 && F.ap >= AP.move) {
      idle.sort(function (a, c) { return aiMilScore(c) - aiMilScore(a); });
      cmdMoveOfficers(b, idle.slice(0, Math.min(2, here - 3, idle.length)).map(function (o) { return o.id; }), dest.id);
    }
  }
}

/* 한 세력의 차례를 '한 행동' 단위로 쪼갠 실행기. 부를 때마다 한 단계 하고, 더 할 일이 없으면 false */
function aiPhaseStepper(f) {
  var stage = 0, list = null, i = 0;
  function oneUnit() { while (i < list.length) { var u = list[i++]; if (alive(u) && !u.done) { aiUnitAct(u); return true; } } return false; }
  return function () {
    if (!G.factions[f].alive || G.over) return false;
    if (stage === 0) { list = facUnits(f); list.sort(function (a, b) { return a.id - b.id; }); i = 0; stage = 1; }
    if (stage === 1) { if (oneUnit()) return true; stage = 2; }
    if (stage === 2) {
      aiDefense(f); aiCaptives(f); aiInternal(f); aiOffense(f); aiLogistics(f);
      list = facUnits(f).filter(function (u) { return !u.done; }); i = 0; stage = 3;
      return true;
    }
    if (stage === 3) { if (oneUnit()) return true; stage = 4; }
    return false;
  };
}
function aiPhase(f) { var next = aiPhaseStepper(f); while (next()) { /* */ } }

/* ===== 천하도 — 3D 지도(three.js)·입력·연출·소리 ===== */
var SQ3 = Math.sqrt(3);
var FONT_D = '"Noto Serif KR","Nanum Myeongjo","AppleMyungjo","Batang",serif';
var FONT_B = '"Apple SD Gothic Neo","Noto Sans KR","Malgun Gothic",system-ui,sans-serif';
var FOV = 32, K3 = 2 * Math.tan(FOV / 2 * Math.PI / 180), Z_MIN = 0.14, Z_MAX = 2.3, FOGC = 0xefe9db;
var INK = '#1b1a18', PAPER = '#f4efe3', SEAL = '#b5332a', NEUTRAL = '#8d877b';
var cv, DPR = 1, CW = 0, CH = 0;
var cam = { x: 200, y: 160, z: 1 };                 // x·y = 바라보는 지면(월드 x·z), z = 확대
var UI = { sel: null, rc: null, atk: null, target: null, march: null, busy: false, anims: [], floats: [], dirty: true, render: true, sound: true, music: true, hide: -1, play: null, pspeed: 1 };
var POR_URL = {}, POR_IMG = {}, SCENE_URL = {}, HMAP = null, R3 = null, TEX = {}, TEXF = {};

function $(id) { return document.getElementById(id); }
function fmt(n) { return Math.round(n).toLocaleString('en-US'); }
function requestDraw() { UI.dirty = true; }
function b64blob(b64, type) {
  var bin = atob(b64), n = bin.length, a = new Uint8Array(n);
  for (var i = 0; i < n; i++) a[i] = bin.charCodeAt(i);
  return new Blob([a], { type: type });
}
function rgbOf(hex) { var v = parseInt(hex.slice(1), 16); return [(v >> 16) & 255, (v >> 8) & 255, v & 255]; }

/* ---------- 좌표·높이 ---------- */
function hexCenter(c, r) { return [SQ3 * (c + 0.5 * (r & 1)) + SQ3 / 2, 1.5 * r + 1]; }
function worldToHex(wx, wz) {
  var r0 = Math.round((wz - 1) / 1.5), best = null, bd = 1e9;
  for (var r = r0 - 1; r <= r0 + 1; r++) {
    if (r < 0 || r >= H) continue;
    var c0 = Math.round((wx - SQ3 / 2) / SQ3 - 0.5 * (r & 1));
    for (var c = c0 - 1; c <= c0 + 1; c++) {
      if (c < 0 || c >= W) continue;
      var p = hexCenter(c, r), d = (p[0] - wx) * (p[0] - wx) + (p[1] - wz) * (p[1] - wz);
      if (d < bd) { bd = d; best = [c, r]; }
    }
  }
  return best;
}
function decodeHeights() {
  var bin = atob(MAP3D.hm), n = MAP3D.GX * MAP3D.GZ, k = (MAP3D.hmax - MAP3D.hmin) / 65535;
  HMAP = new Float32Array(n);
  for (var i = 0; i < n; i++) HMAP[i] = MAP3D.hmin + (bin.charCodeAt(2 * i) | (bin.charCodeAt(2 * i + 1) << 8)) * k;
}
function groundY(x, z) {
  var GX = MAP3D.GX, gx = clamp(x / MAP3D.WX * (GX - 1), 0, GX - 1.001), gz = clamp(z / MAP3D.WZ * (MAP3D.GZ - 1), 0, MAP3D.GZ - 1.001);
  var ix = Math.floor(gx), iz = Math.floor(gz), fx = gx - ix, fz = gz - iz, i = iz * GX + ix;
  return (HMAP[i] * (1 - fx) + HMAP[i + 1] * fx) * (1 - fz) + (HMAP[i + GX] * (1 - fx) + HMAP[i + GX + 1] * fx) * fz;
}
function hexPos(c, r) { var p = hexCenter(c, r); return [p[0], groundY(p[0], p[1]), p[1]]; }

/* ---------- 카메라 ---------- */
function camDist() { return 62 / cam.z; }
function camPitch() { var t = clamp(Math.log(cam.z / Z_MIN) / Math.log(Z_MAX / Z_MIN), 0, 1); return (68 - 27 * t) * Math.PI / 180; }
function clampCam() {
  cam.z = clamp(cam.z, Z_MIN, Z_MAX);
  cam.x = clamp(cam.x, 6, MAP3D.WX - 6); cam.y = clamp(cam.y, 8, MAP3D.WZ - 3);
  UI.render = true;
}
function applyCam() {
  var D = camDist(), p = camPitch(), ty = groundY(cam.x, cam.y), c = R3.cam;
  c.position.set(cam.x, ty + D * Math.sin(p), cam.y + D * Math.cos(p));
  c.lookAt(cam.x, ty, cam.y);
  c.near = Math.max(0.5, D * 0.08); c.far = D * 5 + 120; c.updateProjectionMatrix(); c.updateMatrixWorld();
  var fn = D * 1.15, ff = D * 3.3;
  R3.scene.fog.near = fn; R3.scene.fog.far = ff;
  R3.tmat.uniforms.fogNear.value = fn; R3.tmat.uniforms.fogFar.value = ff;
  if (R3.pitch !== p) { R3.pitch = p; R3.billboard = true; }
}
var _v = null;
function w2s(x, y, z) {
  _v.set(x, y, z).applyMatrix4(R3.cam.matrixWorldInverse);
  var depth = -_v.z; if (depth <= 0.1) return null;
  _v.applyMatrix4(R3.cam.projectionMatrix);
  return [(_v.x * 0.5 + 0.5) * CW, (0.5 - _v.y * 0.5) * CH, depth];
}
function screenRay(sx, sy) {
  var o = R3.cam.position, d = new THREE.Vector3(sx / CW * 2 - 1, 1 - sy / CH * 2, 0.5).unproject(R3.cam).sub(o).normalize();
  return { o: o, d: d };
}
function planeHit(sx, sy, y0) {
  var ry = screenRay(sx, sy); if (ry.d.y > -0.02) return null;
  var t = (y0 - ry.o.y) / ry.d.y; return [ry.o.x + ry.d.x * t, ry.o.z + ry.d.z * t];
}
function pickGround(sx, sy) {
  var ry = screenRay(sx, sy), o = ry.o, d = ry.d; if (d.y > -0.02) return null;
  var t = Math.max(0, (o.y - MAP3D.hmax - 0.5) / -d.y), step = 0.35, prev = t;
  for (var i = 0; i < 900; i++) {
    var x = o.x + d.x * t, y = o.y + d.y * t, z = o.z + d.z * t;
    if (y <= groundY(x, z)) {
      var a = prev, b = t;
      for (var j = 0; j < 12; j++) { var m = (a + b) / 2; if (o.y + d.y * m <= groundY(o.x + d.x * m, o.z + d.z * m)) b = m; else a = m; }
      return [o.x + d.x * b, o.z + d.z * b];
    }
    if (y < MAP3D.hmin - 1) break;
    prev = t; t += step;
  }
  return null;
}
function pxPerUnit(depth) { return CH / (K3 * depth); }
function resize() {
  if (!R3) return;
  DPR = Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio || 1, 2);
  CW = cv.clientWidth; CH = cv.clientHeight;
  R3.renderer.setPixelRatio(DPR); R3.renderer.setSize(CW, CH, false);
  R3.cam.aspect = CW / Math.max(1, CH);
  R3.labelK = 0; clampCam(); requestDraw();
}
/* 상단 HUD와 아래 창(STATUS 창·버튼 줄·재생 줄)에 가려지지 않는 지도 영역 */
function safeArea() {
  var hud = $('hud'), top = (hud ? hud.offsetHeight : 64) + 6, cover = 0, sh = $('sheet'), bar = $('bar'), pl = $('play');
  if (pl && pl.offsetHeight) cover = pl.offsetHeight;
  else cover = (bar ? bar.offsetHeight : 0) + (sh ? sh.offsetHeight : 0);
  var bottom = Math.max(top + 90, CH - cover - 6);
  return { top: top, bottom: bottom, fy: (top + bottom) / 2 / Math.max(1, CH) };
}
function jumpTo(c, r, z, fy, dur) {
  var p = hexCenter(c, r), z1 = clamp(z || cam.z, Z_MIN, Z_MAX), x0 = cam.x, y0 = cam.y, z0 = cam.z;
  var D = 62 / z1, pt = (68 - 27 * clamp(Math.log(z1 / Z_MIN) / Math.log(Z_MAX / Z_MIN), 0, 1)) * Math.PI / 180;
  if (!fy) fy = safeArea().fy;
  var ty = p[1] + (0.5 - fy) * D * K3 / Math.sin(pt);
  anim(dur || 260, function (t) { var e = t * (2 - t); cam.x = x0 + (p[0] - x0) * e; cam.y = y0 + (ty - y0) * e; cam.z = z0 + (z1 - z0) * e; clampCam(); });
}
function onScreen(c, r, pad) {
  var sa = safeArea(), p = hexPos(c, r), s = w2s(p[0], p[1], p[2]); pad = pad || 0;
  return !!s && s[0] >= 44 + pad && s[0] <= CW - 44 - pad && s[1] >= sa.top + 44 + pad && s[1] <= sa.bottom - 14 - pad;
}
function ensureVisible(c, r) { if (!onScreen(c, r, 0)) jumpTo(c, r, Math.max(cam.z, 0.8)); }

/* ---------- 장면 만들기 ---------- */
var TERRAIN_VS = 'varying vec2 vUv; varying vec2 vW; varying float vD;\nvoid main(){ vUv = uv; vW = position.xz; vec4 mv = modelViewMatrix * vec4(position, 1.0); vD = -mv.z; gl_Position = projectionMatrix * mv; }';
var TERRAIN_FS = [
  'uniform sampler2D map; uniform sampler2D detail; uniform sampler2D hexOv; uniform sampler2D hexSt;',
  'uniform vec2 hexN; uniform vec3 fogColor; uniform float fogNear; uniform float fogFar; uniform float gridK; uniform vec3 tint;',
  'varying vec2 vUv; varying vec2 vW; varying float vD;',
  'const float SQ3 = 1.7320508;',
  'void main(){',
  '  vec3 col = texture2D(map, vUv).rgb;',
  '  float dn = texture2D(detail, vW * 0.21).r, dn2 = texture2D(detail, vW * 1.17 + 0.37).g;',
  '  float nearK = 1.0 - smoothstep(25.0, 120.0, vD);',
  '  col *= 0.955 + 0.09 * dn + nearK * 0.10 * (dn2 - 0.5);',
  '  col *= tint;',
  '  vec2 p = vec2(vW.x - SQ3 * 0.5, vW.y - 1.0);',
  '  float rf = p.y / 1.5, qf = p.x / SQ3 - rf * 0.5;',
  '  float xr = floor(qf + 0.5), zr = floor(rf + 0.5), yr = floor(-qf - rf + 0.5);',
  '  float dx = abs(xr - qf), dz = abs(zr - rf), dy = abs(yr + qf + rf);',
  '  if (dx > dy && dx > dz) xr = -yr - zr; else if (dy <= dz) zr = -xr - yr;',
  '  vec2 dd = abs(p - vec2(SQ3 * (xr + zr * 0.5), 1.5 * zr));',
  '  float e = SQ3 * 0.5 - max(dd.x, dd.x * 0.5 + dd.y * 0.8660254);',
  '  vec2 huv = (vec2(xr + (zr - mod(zr, 2.0)) * 0.5, zr) + 0.5) / hexN;',
  '  vec4 ov = texture2D(hexOv, huv); float pass = texture2D(hexSt, huv).r;',
  '  float aa = fwidth(e) + 0.0006;',
  '  float line = 1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, aa * 1.5, e - 0.008);',
  '  float gk = gridK * pass * (1.0 - smoothstep(0.03, 0.10, aa));',
  '  col = mix(col, vec3(0.105, 0.10, 0.094), line * gk * 0.21);',
  '  if (ov.a > 0.004) {',
  '    if (ov.a < 0.30) {',                                      // 담채: 세력권·성역. 칸 테두리 없이 번지듯
  '      col = mix(col, col * mix(vec3(1.0), ov.rgb, 0.85), ov.a * 2.6);',
  '    } else {',                                                // 이동·공격 범위: 칸 가장자리를 짙게
  '      float edge = 1.0 - smoothstep(0.03, 0.06 + aa * 2.0, e);',
  '      col = mix(col, ov.rgb, ov.a * (0.34 + 0.66 * edge));',
  '    }',
  '  }',
  '  col = mix(col, fogColor, smoothstep(fogNear, fogFar, vD));',
  '  gl_FragColor = vec4(col, 1.0);',
  '}'].join('\n');

function noiseTexture() {
  var n = 256, c = document.createElement('canvas'); c.width = c.height = n;
  var x = c.getContext('2d'), im = x.createImageData(n, n), d = im.data, s = 12345;
  function rnd() { s = (s * 1664525 + 1013904223) | 0; return ((s >>> 8) & 0xffff) / 65535; }
  function layer(cell) {                             // 이음매 없는 값 잡음
    var g = n / cell, a = new Float32Array(g * g), out = new Float32Array(n * n), i, j;
    for (i = 0; i < g * g; i++) a[i] = rnd();
    for (j = 0; j < n; j++) for (i = 0; i < n; i++) {
      var fx = i / cell, fy = j / cell, x0 = Math.floor(fx), y0 = Math.floor(fy), tx = fx - x0, ty = fy - y0;
      tx = tx * tx * (3 - 2 * tx); ty = ty * ty * (3 - 2 * ty);
      var x1 = (x0 + 1) % g, y1 = (y0 + 1) % g;
      out[j * n + i] = (a[y0 * g + x0] * (1 - tx) + a[y0 * g + x1] * tx) * (1 - ty) + (a[y1 * g + x0] * (1 - tx) + a[y1 * g + x1] * tx) * ty;
    }
    return out;
  }
  var A = [layer(32), layer(16), layer(8), layer(4)], B = [layer(16), layer(8), layer(4), layer(2)];
  for (var k = 0; k < n * n; k++) {
    d[k * 4] = 255 * (A[0][k] * 0.45 + A[1][k] * 0.28 + A[2][k] * 0.17 + A[3][k] * 0.10);
    d[k * 4 + 1] = 255 * (B[0][k] * 0.35 + B[1][k] * 0.30 + B[2][k] * 0.20 + B[3][k] * 0.15);
    d[k * 4 + 2] = 128; d[k * 4 + 3] = 255;
  }
  x.putImageData(im, 0, 0);
  var t = new THREE.CanvasTexture(c); t.wrapS = t.wrapT = THREE.RepeatWrapping; return t;
}
function init3d() {
  cv = $('map'); decodeHeights(); _v = new THREE.Vector3();
  var renderer = new THREE.WebGLRenderer({ canvas: cv, antialias: true, alpha: false, powerPreference: 'high-performance' });
  renderer.setClearColor(FOGC, 1);
  var scene = new THREE.Scene(); scene.fog = new THREE.Fog(FOGC, 60, 200);
  var camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(FOV, 1, 1, 800);
  R3 = { renderer: renderer, scene: scene, cam: camera, bases: [], units: {}, facs: {}, fires: {}, labelK: 0, pitch: -1, billboard: true, ready: false, loading: 0, labelCache: {} };
  // 헥스 자료 텍스처
  var st = new Uint8Array(NHEX * 4), i;
  for (i = 0; i < NHEX; i++) { var t = TCH[i]; st[i * 4] = (t === 120 || t === 115 || t === 109) ? 0 : 255; st[i * 4 + 3] = 255; }
  R3.stTex = new THREE.DataTexture(st, W, H); R3.stTex.magFilter = R3.stTex.minFilter = THREE.NearestFilter; R3.stTex.needsUpdate = true;
  R3.ovData = new Uint8Array(NHEX * 4);
  R3.ovTex = new THREE.DataTexture(R3.ovData, W, H); R3.ovTex.magFilter = R3.ovTex.minFilter = THREE.NearestFilter; R3.ovTex.needsUpdate = true;
  var blank = new THREE.DataTexture(new Uint8Array([120, 130, 100, 255]), 1, 1); blank.needsUpdate = true;
  R3.tmat = new THREE.ShaderMaterial({
    uniforms: { map: { value: blank }, detail: { value: noiseTexture() }, hexOv: { value: R3.ovTex }, hexSt: { value: R3.stTex }, hexN: { value: new THREE.Vector2(W, H) },
      fogColor: { value: new THREE.Color(FOGC) }, fogNear: { value: 60 }, fogFar: { value: 200 }, gridK: { value: 1 }, tint: { value: new THREE.Vector3(1, 1, 1) } },
    vertexShader: TERRAIN_VS, fragmentShader: TERRAIN_FS, extensions: { derivatives: true }
  });
  buildTerrain(); buildRoads(); buildTrees();
  R3.objs = new THREE.Group(); scene.add(R3.objs);
  R3.flame = flameTexture();
}
function buildTerrain() {
  var GX = MAP3D.GX, GZ = MAP3D.GZ, NX = 8, NZ = 8, sx = MAP3D.WX / (GX - 1), sz = MAP3D.WZ / (GZ - 1);
  for (var cz = 0; cz < NZ; cz++) for (var cx = 0; cx < NX; cx++) {
    var x0 = Math.floor(cx * (GX - 1) / NX), x1 = Math.floor((cx + 1) * (GX - 1) / NX), z0 = Math.floor(cz * (GZ - 1) / NZ), z1 = Math.floor((cz + 1) * (GZ - 1) / NZ);
    var nx = x1 - x0 + 1, nz = z1 - z0 + 1, pos = new Float32Array(nx * nz * 3), uv = new Float32Array(nx * nz * 2), idx = new Uint16Array((nx - 1) * (nz - 1) * 6), p = 0, q = 0, k = 0, i, j;
    for (j = 0; j < nz; j++) for (i = 0; i < nx; i++) {
      var gx = x0 + i, gz = z0 + j;
      pos[p++] = gx * sx; pos[p++] = HMAP[gz * GX + gx]; pos[p++] = gz * sz;
      uv[q++] = gx / (GX - 1); uv[q++] = gz / (GZ - 1);
    }
    for (j = 0; j < nz - 1; j++) for (i = 0; i < nx - 1; i++) { var a = j * nx + i, b = a + 1, c = a + nx, d = c + 1; idx[k++] = a; idx[k++] = c; idx[k++] = b; idx[k++] = b; idx[k++] = c; idx[k++] = d; }
    var g = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
    g.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(pos, 3)); g.setAttribute('uv', new THREE.BufferAttribute(uv, 2)); g.setIndex(new THREE.BufferAttribute(idx, 1));
    g.computeBoundingSphere();
    var m = new THREE.Mesh(g, R3.tmat); m.matrixAutoUpdate = false; R3.scene.add(m);
  }
  var skirt = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(MAP3D.WX + 900, MAP3D.WZ + 900), new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0xefe9db }));
  skirt.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2; skirt.position.set(MAP3D.WX / 2, MAP3D.hmin - 0.08, MAP3D.WZ / 2); R3.scene.add(skirt);
}
function buildRoads() {
  var pos = [], idx = [], n = 0, w = 0.062;
  MAP3D.roads.forEach(function (f) {
    var m = f.length / 2, base = n;
    for (var i = 0; i < m; i++) {
      var x = f[2 * i], z = f[2 * i + 1], a = Math.max(0, i - 1), b = Math.min(m - 1, i + 1);
      var tx = f[2 * b] - f[2 * a], tz = f[2 * b + 1] - f[2 * a + 1], L = Math.sqrt(tx * tx + tz * tz) || 1, px = -tz / L * w, pz = tx / L * w;
      var y = Math.max(groundY(x + px, z + pz), groundY(x - px, z - pz), groundY(x, z)) + 0.03;
      pos.push(x + px, y, z + pz, x - px, y, z - pz); n += 2;
      if (i) idx.push(base + 2 * i - 2, base + 2 * i - 1, base + 2 * i, base + 2 * i - 1, base + 2 * i + 1, base + 2 * i);
    }
  });
  var g = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
  g.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(new Float32Array(pos), 3)); g.setIndex(n > 65535 ? new THREE.BufferAttribute(new Uint32Array(idx), 1) : new THREE.BufferAttribute(new Uint16Array(idx), 1));
  var mat = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0x2b2722, transparent: true, opacity: 0.46, depthWrite: false, side: THREE.DoubleSide, polygonOffset: true, polygonOffsetFactor: -3, polygonOffsetUnits: -3 });
  var mesh = new THREE.Mesh(g, mat); mesh.frustumCulled = false; mesh.renderOrder = 0; R3.scene.add(mesh);
}
function hash2(a, b) { var n = (a * 374761393 + b * 668265263) | 0; n = Math.imul(n ^ (n >>> 13), 1274126177); return ((n ^ (n >>> 16)) >>> 0) / 4294967296; }
function buildTrees() {
  var NB = 8, buckets = [], i, j, k;
  for (i = 0; i < NB * NB; i++) buckets.push([]);
  for (k = 0; k < NHEX; k++) {
    if (TCH[k] !== 102 || RDB[k] || PAT[k]) continue;
    var c = k % W, r = (k - c) / W, p = hexCenter(c, r);
    for (j = 0; j < 4; j++) {
      var a = hash2(k, j * 7 + 1) * 6.283, d = 0.18 + 0.56 * Math.sqrt(hash2(k, j * 7 + 2)), x = p[0] + Math.cos(a) * d, z = p[1] + Math.sin(a) * d;
      buckets[Math.min(NB - 1, Math.floor(z / MAP3D.WZ * NB)) * NB + Math.min(NB - 1, Math.floor(x / MAP3D.WX * NB))].push([x, groundY(x, z), z, 0.75 + 0.6 * hash2(k, j * 7 + 3), hash2(k, j * 7 + 4)]);
    }
  }
  var cone = new THREE.ConeGeometry(0.21, 0.66, 5, 1, true); cone.translate(0, 0.30, 0);
  var cp = cone.attributes.position, cols = new Float32Array(cp.count * 3);
  for (i = 0; i < cp.count; i++) { var t = clamp(cp.getY(i) / 0.63, 0, 1); cols[i * 3] = 0.12 + 0.20 * t; cols[i * 3 + 1] = 0.15 + 0.23 * t; cols[i * 3 + 2] = 0.13 + 0.19 * t; }
  cone.setAttribute('color', new THREE.BufferAttribute(cols, 3));
  var mat = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ vertexColors: true }), m4 = new THREE.Matrix4(), q = new THREE.Quaternion(), s = new THREE.Vector3(), v = new THREE.Vector3(), col = new THREE.Color(), up = new THREE.Vector3(0, 1, 0);
  R3.trees = [];
  for (i = 0; i < buckets.length; i++) {
    var B = buckets[i]; if (!B.length) continue;
    var g = cone.clone(), mesh = new THREE.InstancedMesh(g, mat, B.length), minx = 1e9, maxx = -1e9, minz = 1e9, maxz = -1e9;
    for (j = 0; j < B.length; j++) {
      var e = B[j]; q.setFromAxisAngle(up, e[4] * 6.283); s.set(e[3], e[3] * (0.85 + 0.4 * e[4]), e[3]); v.set(e[0], e[1] - 0.03, e[2]);
      m4.compose(v, q, s); mesh.setMatrixAt(j, m4);
      var tg = 0.72 + 0.5 * e[4]; col.setRGB(tg, tg * (0.98 + 0.08 * hash2(j, i)), tg); mesh.setColorAt(j, col);
      minx = Math.min(minx, e[0]); maxx = Math.max(maxx, e[0]); minz = Math.min(minz, e[2]); maxz = Math.max(maxz, e[2]);
    }
    g.boundingSphere = new THREE.Sphere(new THREE.Vector3((minx + maxx) / 2, 1, (minz + maxz) / 2), Math.sqrt((maxx - minx) * (maxx - minx) + (maxz - minz) * (maxz - minz)) / 2 + 3);
    mesh.instanceMatrix.needsUpdate = true; mesh.matrixAutoUpdate = false; R3.scene.add(mesh); R3.trees.push(mesh);
  }
}
function flameTexture() {
  var c = document.createElement('canvas'); c.width = 64; c.height = 96;
  var x = c.getContext('2d'), g = x.createRadialGradient(32, 66, 2, 32, 60, 34);
  g.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(255,226,150,0.98)'); g.addColorStop(0.32, 'rgba(236,120,44,0.95)'); g.addColorStop(0.72, 'rgba(190,52,36,0.78)'); g.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(120,24,18,0)');
  x.fillStyle = g; x.beginPath(); x.moveTo(32, 2); x.bezierCurveTo(60, 44, 58, 92, 32, 94); x.bezierCurveTo(6, 92, 4, 44, 32, 2); x.fill();
  return new THREE.CanvasTexture(c);
}
function loadAssets() {
  var k;
  for (k in PORTRAITS) POR_URL[k] = URL.createObjectURL(b64blob(PORTRAITS[k], 'image/webp'));
  $('title').style.backgroundImage = 'url(' + TITLE_IMG + ')';
  if (typeof HANJI_IMG === 'string') document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--hanji', 'url(' + HANJI_IMG + ')');
  function img(src, done) { var im = new Image(); R3.loading++; im.onload = function () { done(im); R3.loading--; if (!R3.loading) { R3.ready = true; requestDraw(); } }; im.onerror = function () { R3.loading--; }; im.src = src; }
  img(URL.createObjectURL(b64blob(TERRAIN_B64, 'image/jpeg')), function (im) {
    var t = new THREE.Texture(im); t.flipY = false; t.anisotropy = Math.min(8, R3.renderer.capabilities.getMaxAnisotropy()); t.needsUpdate = true;
    R3.tmat.uniforms.map.value = t;
  });
  Object.keys(SPRITES).forEach(function (key) {
    img(SPRITES[key].u, function (im) {
      var t = new THREE.Texture(im); t.needsUpdate = true; TEX[key] = t;
      var f = t.clone(); f.repeat.x = -1; f.offset.x = 1; f.needsUpdate = true; TEXF[key] = f;
    });
  });
  if (document.fonts && document.fonts.ready) document.fonts.ready.then(function () { R3.labelCache = {}; R3.bases.forEach(function (o) { o.sig = ''; }); for (var id in R3.units) R3.units[id].sig = ''; requestDraw(); });
}

/* 장면 초상(3:4): 처음 볼 때만 주소를 만든다. 장면이 없는 키는 '' */
function sceneUrl(key) {
  if (SCENE_URL[key] === undefined) SCENE_URL[key] = (typeof SCENES !== 'undefined' && SCENES[key]) ? URL.createObjectURL(b64blob(SCENES[key], 'image/webp')) : '';
  return SCENE_URL[key];
}
/* 초상 그림 객체: 처음 쓸 때 불러오고, 다 불러오면 부대 이름표를 다시 그린다 */
function porImg(key) {
  var im = POR_IMG[key];
  if (im === undefined) {
    if (!POR_URL[key]) { POR_IMG[key] = null; return null; }
    im = POR_IMG[key] = new Image();
    im.onload = function () { im.ok = true; if (R3) { for (var id in R3.units) R3.units[id].sig = ''; } requestDraw(); };
    im.src = POR_URL[key];
  }
  return im && im.ok ? im : null;
}

/* ---------- 이름표 ---------- */
function rr(x, X, Y, w, h, r) { x.beginPath(); x.moveTo(X + r, Y); x.arcTo(X + w, Y, X + w, Y + h, r); x.arcTo(X + w, Y + h, X, Y + h, r); x.arcTo(X, Y + h, X, Y, r); x.arcTo(X, Y, X + w, Y, r); x.closePath(); }
function mkLabel() {
  var c = document.createElement('canvas'); c.width = 8; c.height = 8;
  var t = new THREE.CanvasTexture(c); t.minFilter = THREE.LinearFilter; t.generateMipmaps = false;
  var sp = new THREE.Sprite(new THREE.SpriteMaterial({ map: t, transparent: true, depthTest: false, depthWrite: false, sizeAttenuation: false }));
  sp.center.set(0.5, 0); sp.renderOrder = 6;
  return { sp: sp, cv: c, tex: t, w: 8, h: 8 };
}
function labelSize(L, k) { L.sp.scale.set(L.w / 2 / CH * K3 * k, L.h / 2 / CH * K3 * k, 1); }
/* lines: [{t, px, font, color, bold}], 판 색 bg, 테두리 색 */
function drawLabel(L, lines, bg, border, badge) {
  var x = L.cv.getContext('2d'), i, w = 0, h = 10, S = 2;
  for (i = 0; i < lines.length; i++) { x.font = (lines[i].bold || 700) + ' ' + lines[i].px * S + 'px ' + (lines[i].font || FONT_B); lines[i].w = x.measureText(lines[i].t).width; w = Math.max(w, lines[i].w); h += lines[i].px * S * 1.18; }
  w = Math.ceil(w + 22 * S / 2 + (badge ? 30 : 8)); h = Math.ceil(h);
  L.cv.width = w; L.cv.height = h; L.w = w; L.h = h;
  x = L.cv.getContext('2d');
  var bw = w - (badge ? 22 : 0), sel = border === SEAL;
  // 석채(石彩) 빛 판에 종이 빛 글씨, 가는 먹선. 고른 것은 인주색 겹테
  rr(x, 3, 3, bw - 6, h - 6, 3); x.fillStyle = bg; x.fill();
  x.lineWidth = sel ? 4 : 1.6; x.strokeStyle = sel ? SEAL : border; x.stroke();
  if (sel) { rr(x, 6.5, 6.5, bw - 13, h - 13, 1.5); x.lineWidth = 1.2; x.strokeStyle = 'rgba(251,246,234,0.75)'; x.stroke(); }
  var y = 5;
  x.textAlign = 'center'; x.textBaseline = 'top';
  for (i = 0; i < lines.length; i++) {
    var ln = lines[i]; x.font = (ln.bold || 700) + ' ' + ln.px * S + 'px ' + (ln.font || FONT_B);
    x.fillStyle = 'rgba(20,18,16,0.38)'; x.fillText(ln.t, bw / 2 + 1, y + 1.5);
    x.fillStyle = ln.color || '#fbf6ea'; x.fillText(ln.t, bw / 2, y); y += ln.px * S * 1.18;
  }
  if (badge) { rr(x, w - 31, 3, 28, 28, 4); x.fillStyle = SEAL; x.fill(); x.lineWidth = 1.5; x.strokeStyle = '#fbf6ea'; x.stroke(); x.fillStyle = '#fbf6ea'; x.font = '800 19px ' + FONT_B; x.textBaseline = 'middle'; x.fillText(badge, w - 17, 18); }
  L.tex.dispose(); L.tex.needsUpdate = true;
}
/* 부대 왼쪽 위에 붙는 주장 얼굴 딱지(둥근 초상). 테두리는 세력 색 */
function drawFace(L, img, ring) {
  var S = 60, x; L.cv.width = L.cv.height = S; L.w = L.h = S; x = L.cv.getContext('2d');
  x.save(); x.beginPath(); x.arc(S / 2, S / 2, S / 2 - 4, 0, 6.3); x.clip(); x.fillStyle = '#f6f1e6'; x.fillRect(0, 0, S, S);
  x.globalCompositeOperation = 'multiply';
  try { var iw = img.naturalWidth, ih = img.naturalHeight; x.drawImage(img, iw * 0.25, ih * 0.13, iw * 0.50, ih * 0.50, 3, 3, S - 6, S - 6);    /* v0.5 흉상: 얼굴 중심이 가로 50%·세로 41% */ } catch (e) { /* 바탕만 */ }
  x.restore();
  x.beginPath(); x.arc(S / 2, S / 2, S / 2 - 4, 0, 6.3); x.lineWidth = 5; x.strokeStyle = ring; x.stroke();
  x.beginPath(); x.arc(S / 2, S / 2, S / 2 - 1.3, 0, 6.3); x.lineWidth = 1.4; x.strokeStyle = 'rgba(27,26,24,0.9)'; x.stroke();
  L.tex.dispose(); L.tex.needsUpdate = true;
}
function mixHex(a, b, t) { var x = rgbOf(a), y = rgbOf(b); return 'rgb(' + Math.round(x[0] + (y[0] - x[0]) * t) + ',' + Math.round(x[1] + (y[1] - x[1]) * t) + ',' + Math.round(x[2] + (y[2] - x[2]) * t) + ')'; }
/* 붓으로 한 번에 두른 고리(성역 표시): 굵기가 변하고 한쪽이 트여 있다 */
function ensoTexture() {
  var n = 256, c = document.createElement('canvas'); c.width = c.height = n;
  var x = c.getContext('2d'), i, s = 91;
  function rnd() { s = (s * 1664525 + 1013904223) | 0; return ((s >>> 8) & 0xffff) / 65535; }
  x.translate(n / 2, n / 2);
  for (var pass = 0; pass < 3; pass++) {
    var a0 = 0.5 + rnd() * 0.3, a1 = a0 + 5.45 + rnd() * 0.4, steps = 220;
    for (i = 0; i < steps; i++) {
      var t = i / (steps - 1), a = a0 + (a1 - a0) * t, r = 112 + Math.sin(a * 3.1 + pass) * 1.6 + (rnd() - 0.5) * 1.4;
      var wd = (2.2 + 4.6 * Math.sin(Math.PI * Math.pow(t, 0.8))) * (pass ? 0.55 : 1), al = (0.34 + 0.4 * Math.sin(Math.PI * t)) * (pass ? 0.5 : 1) * (0.6 + 0.4 * rnd());
      x.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,' + al.toFixed(3) + ')';
      x.beginPath(); x.arc(Math.cos(a) * (r - pass * 3.5), Math.sin(a) * (r - pass * 3.5), wd, 0, 6.3); x.fill();
    }
  }
  var t2 = new THREE.CanvasTexture(c); t2.minFilter = THREE.LinearMipmapLinearFilter; return t2;
}
/* 부대 발밑의 먹점: 가운데가 짙고 가장자리가 번진 점 */
function inkDotTexture() {
  var n = 96, c = document.createElement('canvas'); c.width = c.height = n;
  var x = c.getContext('2d'), g = x.createRadialGradient(n / 2, n / 2, 4, n / 2, n / 2, n / 2);
  g.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(255,255,255,0.95)'); g.addColorStop(0.62, 'rgba(255,255,255,0.86)'); g.addColorStop(0.84, 'rgba(255,255,255,0.42)'); g.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(255,255,255,0)');
  x.fillStyle = g; x.fillRect(0, 0, n, n);
  return new THREE.CanvasTexture(c);
}
function shade(hex, k) { var c = rgbOf(hex); return 'rgb(' + Math.round(c[0] * k) + ',' + Math.round(c[1] * k) + ',' + Math.round(c[2] * k) + ')'; }

/* ---------- 물체 ---------- */
var BASE_W = { L: 3.5, M: 3.1, S: 2.7, G: 3.0 }, FAC_W = 1.62, UNIT_W = 2.0;
function spriteObj(key, wu, order) {
  var S = SPRITES[key], sp = new THREE.Sprite(new THREE.SpriteMaterial({ map: TEX[key], transparent: true, depthTest: false, depthWrite: false }));
  sp.center.set(0.5, S.ay); sp.scale.set(wu, wu * S.h / S.w, 1); sp.renderOrder = order;
  sp.userData = { key: key, top: wu * S.h / S.w * (1 - S.ay), w: wu, h: wu * S.h / S.w, ay: S.ay };
  return sp;
}
function setSprite(sp, key, wu, flip) {
  var S = SPRITES[key]; sp.material.map = flip ? TEXF[key] : TEX[key]; sp.material.needsUpdate = true;
  sp.center.set(0.5, S.ay); sp.scale.set(wu, wu * S.h / S.w, 1);
  sp.userData.key = key; sp.userData.top = wu * S.h / S.w * (1 - S.ay); sp.userData.w = wu; sp.userData.h = wu * S.h / S.w; sp.userData.ay = S.ay;
}
function syncScene() {
  if (!G || !R3.ready) return;
  var i, j, b, o, p, sig, idleN = {};
  for (i = 0; i < G.officers.length; i++) { var of = G.officers[i]; if (of.st === 'active' && of.f === G.player && of.unit === null && !of.acted) idleN[of.loc] = (idleN[of.loc] || 0) + 1; }
  // 거점
  for (i = 0; i < G.bases.length; i++) {
    b = G.bases[i]; o = R3.bases[i];
    if (!o) {
      p = hexPos(b.c, b.r); o = R3.bases[i] = { g: new THREE.Group(), sig: '' };
      o.sp = spriteObj(b.kind === 'gate' ? 'gate' : 'city_' + b.sz, BASE_W[b.sz], 2); o.lab = mkLabel();
      o.g.position.set(p[0], p[1], p[2]); o.g.add(o.sp); o.g.add(o.lab.sp); o.lab.sp.position.set(0, o.sp.userData.top * 0.74, 0); R3.objs.add(o.g);
    }
    var mineB = b.owner === G.player, col = b.owner >= 0 ? G.factions[b.owner].color : NEUTRAL;
    if (b.kind === 'city' && !o.zone) {                                 // 성역(성 둘레 ZONE_R 칸)을 붓으로 두른 고리
      var zr = (ZONE_R + 0.5) * SQ3 * 0.94;
      o.zone = new THREE.Mesh(R3.zoneGeo || (R3.zoneGeo = new THREE.PlaneGeometry(2, 2).rotateX(-Math.PI / 2)), new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ map: R3.zoneTex || (R3.zoneTex = ensoTexture()), transparent: true, opacity: 0.6, depthTest: false, depthWrite: false }));
      o.zone.scale.set(zr, 1, zr * 0.93); o.zone.rotation.y = hash2(i, 77) * 6.283; o.zone.position.set(o.g.position.x, o.g.position.y + 0.06, o.g.position.z); o.zone.renderOrder = 0.5; R3.objs.add(o.zone);
    }
    if (o.zone && o.zcol !== col) { o.zcol = col; o.zone.material.color.set(shade(col, 0.82)); }
    sig = b.owner + '|' + (b.owner >= 0 ? Math.round(b.troops / 100) : '') + '|' + (mineB ? (idleN[b.id] || 0) : '') + '|' + (UI.sel && UI.sel.kind === 'base' && UI.sel.id === b.id);
    if (sig !== o.sig) {
      o.sig = sig;
      var ls = [{ t: b.n, px: 15, font: FONT_D, bold: 900 }];
      if (b.owner >= 0) ls.push({ t: fmt(b.troops), px: 10.5, color: '#f6efdf' });
      drawLabel(o.lab, ls, shade(col, 0.86), (UI.sel && UI.sel.kind === 'base' && UI.sel.id === b.id) ? SEAL : INK, mineB && idleN[b.id] ? idleN[b.id] : 0);
      R3.labelK = 0;
    }
    // 시설
    for (j = 0; j < b.plots.length; j++) {
      var pl = b.plots[j], fk = i + ':' + j, fo = R3.facs[fk], fc = pl.fac;
      if (!fc) { if (fo) { R3.objs.remove(fo.sp); fo.sp.material.dispose(); delete R3.facs[fk]; } continue;      }
      var fs = fc.t + fc.lv + (fc.done ? 'd' : 'c');
      if (!fo) { p = hexPos(pl.c, pl.r); fo = R3.facs[fk] = { sp: spriteObj(fc.t, FAC_W, 2), sig: '' }; fo.sp.position.set(p[0], p[1], p[2]); R3.objs.add(fo.sp); }
      if (fo.sig !== fs) { fo.sig = fs; setSprite(fo.sp, fc.t, FAC_W * (0.94 + 0.10 * (fc.lv - 1))); fo.sp.material.opacity = fc.done ? 1 : 0.5; }
    }
  }
  // 부대
  var seen = {};
