  for (i = 0; i < G.units.length; i++) {
    var u = G.units[i]; seen[u.id] = 1; o = R3.units[u.id];
    var fcol = G.factions[u.f].color, key = 'u_' + u.type, L = uLeader(u), isSel = UI.sel && UI.sel.kind === 'unit' && UI.sel.id === u.id;
    if (!o) {
      o = R3.units[u.id] = { g: new THREE.Group(), sig: '', anim: false, face: 1, c: -1, r: -1 };
      o.sp = spriteObj(key, UNIT_W, 3); o.lab = mkLabel(); o.g.add(o.sp); o.g.add(o.lab.sp);
      o.fc = mkLabel(); o.fc.sp.center.set(0.5, 0.5); o.fc.sp.renderOrder = 7; o.fc.sp.visible = false; o.fcOn = false; o.g.add(o.fc.sp);
      o.ring = new THREE.Mesh(R3.ringGeo || (R3.ringGeo = new THREE.CircleGeometry(1.0, 24).rotateX(-Math.PI / 2)), new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: fcol, map: R3.dotTex || (R3.dotTex = inkDotTexture()), transparent: true, opacity: 0.78, depthTest: false, depthWrite: false }));
      o.ring.renderOrder = 1; R3.objs.add(o.ring); R3.objs.add(o.g);
    }
    if (!o.anim && (o.c !== u.c || o.r !== u.r)) {
      p = hexPos(u.c, u.r);
      if (o.c >= 0) { var px0 = hexCenter(o.c, o.r)[0]; if (Math.abs(p[0] - px0) > 0.2) o.face = p[0] < px0 ? -1 : 1; }
      o.c = u.c; o.r = u.r; o.g.position.set(p[0], p[1], p[2]); o.ring.position.set(p[0], p[1] + 0.04, p[2]);
    }
    var showName = isSel || R3.nameZoom, fim = (isSel || R3.faceZoom) ? porImg(L.por) : null;
    sig = u.f + '|' + u.type + '|' + Math.round(u.troops / 10) + '|' + (u.f === G.player && u.done) + '|' + (u.status || '') + '|' + isSel + '|' + o.face + '|' + L.n + '|' + (u.dest >= 0) + '|' + showName + '|' + (fim ? 1 : 0);
    if (sig !== o.sig) {
      o.sig = sig; setSprite(o.sp, key, UNIT_W, o.face < 0);
      var dim = u.f === G.player && u.done;
      o.sp.material.color.setHex(0xffffff); o.sp.material.opacity = dim ? 0.52 : 1; o.ring.material.color.set(fcol); o.ring.material.opacity = dim ? 0.4 : 0.78;
      o.lab.sp.center.set(0.5, 1); o.lab.sp.position.set(0, -0.16, 0);
      var mark = u.status ? (u.status === 'confuse' ? '혼란 ' : '위보 ') : (u.dest >= 0 ? '▸ ' : ''), lns = [{ t: mark + fmt(u.troops), px: 12, bold: 800, color: '#fbf6ea' }];
      if (showName) lns.unshift({ t: L.n, px: 10.5, font: FONT_D, bold: 900, color: '#fbf6ea' });
      drawLabel(o.lab, lns, dim ? mixHex(fcol, '#8d877b', 0.6) : shade(fcol, 0.84), isSel ? SEAL : INK);
      o.fcOn = !!fim;
      if (fim) { drawFace(o.fc, fim, isSel ? SEAL : fcol); o.fc.sp.position.set(-0.74, o.sp.userData.top * 0.80, 0); o.fc.sp.material.opacity = dim ? 0.7 : 1; }
      R3.labelK = 0;
    }
    o.g.visible = o.ring.visible = (u.id !== UI.hide) || o.anim;
  }
  for (var id in R3.units) {
    if (seen[id] || R3.units[id].anim) continue;
    o = R3.units[id]; R3.objs.remove(o.g); R3.objs.remove(o.ring); o.sp.material.dispose(); o.lab.tex.dispose(); o.lab.sp.material.dispose(); o.fc.tex.dispose(); o.fc.sp.material.dispose(); o.ring.material.dispose(); delete R3.units[id];
  }
  // 불
  for (var fk2 in G.fires) {
    if (R3.fires[fk2]) continue;
    var kk = +fk2, fc2 = kk % W; p = hexPos(fc2, (kk - fc2) / W);
    var fg = new THREE.Group(); fg.position.set(p[0], p[1], p[2]);
    for (j = 0; j < 3; j++) { var fsprite = new THREE.Sprite(new THREE.SpriteMaterial({ map: R3.flame, transparent: true, depthTest: false, depthWrite: false })); fsprite.center.set(0.5, 0.08); fsprite.position.set((j - 1) * 0.42, 0, (j % 2) * 0.2); fsprite.renderOrder = 4; fg.add(fsprite); }
    R3.objs.add(fg); R3.fires[fk2] = fg;
  }
  for (fk2 in R3.fires) if (!G.fires[fk2]) { R3.objs.remove(R3.fires[fk2]); delete R3.fires[fk2]; }
  // 철
  var m = G.month, tn = R3.tmat.uniforms.tint.value;
  if (m >= 3 && m <= 5) tn.set(0.995, 1.0, 0.985); else if (m >= 6 && m <= 8) tn.set(0.985, 1.0, 0.975); else if (m >= 9 && m <= 11) tn.set(1.0, 0.985, 0.95); else tn.set(0.99, 0.995, 1.01);
  rebuildOverlay(); R3.billboard = true; drawMini();
}
function ovPut(k, c, a) { var d = R3.ovData, i = k * 4; d[i] = c[0]; d[i + 1] = c[1]; d[i + 2] = c[2]; d[i + 3] = a; }
function rebuildOverlay() {
  var d = R3.ovData, i, j, k; d.fill(0);
  for (i = 0; i < G.bases.length; i++) {                       // 세력권
    var b = G.bases[i]; if (b.owner < 0) continue;
    var col = rgbOf(G.factions[b.owner].color), rad = b.kind === 'gate' ? 1 : ZONE_R;
    for (var r = Math.max(0, b.r - rad); r <= Math.min(H - 1, b.r + rad); r++) for (var c = Math.max(0, b.c - rad - 1); c <= Math.min(W - 1, b.c + rad + 1); c++) {
      if (hdist(c, r, b.c, b.r) > rad) continue; k = r * W + c; var t = TCH[k];
      if (t !== 120 && t !== 115 && t !== 109) ovPut(k, col, 44);
    }
    if (b.owner === G.player && b.kind === 'city') for (j = 0; j < b.plots.length; j++) if (!b.plots[j].fac) ovPut(K(b.plots[j].c, b.plots[j].r), [176, 124, 34], 88);
  }
  if (UI.march && UI.march.path) for (i = 0; i < UI.march.path.length; i++) ovPut(UI.march.path[i], [47, 79, 111], 96);
  var su = selUnit();
  if (su && UI.rc && !UI.busy) {
    for (var ks in UI.rc.stop) { k = +ks; if (BAT[k] !== undefined) ovPut(k, [176, 124, 34], 170); else ovPut(k, ZONE[k] !== ZONE[K(su.c, su.r)] ? [96, 118, 140] : [47, 79, 111], 122); }
    var ats = UI.atk || [];
    for (i = 0; i < ats.length; i++) ovPut(K(ats[i].c, ats[i].r), [181, 51, 42], 172);
  }
  if (UI.sel && UI.sel.kind === 'unit' && UMAP[UI.sel.id]) ovPut(K(UMAP[UI.sel.id].c, UMAP[UI.sel.id].r), [27, 26, 24], 96);
  if (UI.sel && UI.sel.kind === 'base') ovPut(K(G.bases[UI.sel.id].c, G.bases[UI.sel.id].r), [176, 124, 34], 150);
  if (UI.target) ovPut(K(UI.target.c, UI.target.r), [181, 51, 42], 225);
  R3.ovTex.needsUpdate = true;
}

/* ---------- 연출 ---------- */
function anim(dur, step, done) { UI.anims.push({ t0: performance.now(), dur: dur, step: step, done: done }); UI.render = true; }
function floatText(c, r, text, color, delay, big, dy) {
  var el = document.createElement('div'); el.className = 'flt' + (big ? ' big' : ''); el.textContent = text; el.style.color = color || INK; el.style.opacity = 0;
  $('fx').appendChild(el);
  UI.floats.push({ p: hexPos(c, r), el: el, t0: performance.now() + (delay || 0), dur: 1350, dy: dy || 0 }); UI.render = true;
}
function flashHex(c, r, color) {
  var el = document.createElement('div'); el.className = 'ring'; el.style.borderColor = color || INK; el.style.opacity = 0;
  $('fx').appendChild(el);
  UI.floats.push({ p: hexPos(c, r), el: el, t0: performance.now(), dur: 360, ring: true }); UI.render = true;
}
function unitVis(u) { var L = uLeader(u); return { id: u.id, color: G.factions[u.f].color, por: L.por, type: u.type, ratio: u.troops / Math.max(1, u.max), name: L.n }; }
/* 부대 이동 연출 */
function animMove(vis, from, path, done, slow) {
  var pts = [hexPos(from[0], from[1])], i, o = R3.units[vis.id];
  for (i = 0; i < path.length; i++) { var k = path[i], c = k % W; pts.push(hexPos(c, (k - c) / W)); }
  if (!o || !path.length) { if (o) { o.anim = false; o.sig = ''; } UI.hide = -1; if (done) done(); return; }
  UI.busy = true; o.anim = true; o.g.visible = o.ring.visible = true;
  var dur = Math.min(1500, 150 * path.length + 120) * (slow || 1), last = -1, hop = vis.type === 'cav' ? 0.16 : (vis.type === 'ram' || vis.type === 'trans' ? 0.04 : 0.10);
  var mtype = vis.type;
  if (!vis.type) { var uu = UMAP[vis.id]; if (uu) { mtype = uu.type; hop = uu.type === 'cav' ? 0.16 : (uu.type === 'ram' || uu.type === 'trans' ? 0.04 : 0.10); } }
  sfxMove(mtype, dur);
  anim(dur, function (t) {
    var f = t * (pts.length - 1), i2 = Math.min(pts.length - 2, Math.floor(f)), w = f - i2, a = pts[i2], b = pts[i2 + 1];
    var x = a[0] + (b[0] - a[0]) * w, z = a[2] + (b[2] - a[2]) * w, y = groundY(x, z);
    o.g.position.set(x, y + Math.abs(Math.sin(w * Math.PI * 2)) * hop, z); o.ring.position.set(x, y + 0.04, z);
    if (i2 !== last) { last = i2; if (typeof fxDust === 'function') fxDust(a[0], a[1], a[2], 0.8); var nf_ = Math.abs(b[0] - a[0]) > 0.2 ? (b[0] < a[0] ? -1 : 1) : o.face; if (nf_ !== o.face) { o.face = nf_; setSprite(o.sp, o.sp.userData.key, UNIT_W, nf_ < 0); } }
  }, function () {
    var lastK = path[path.length - 1], lc = lastK % W; o.c = lc; o.r = (lastK - lc) / W;
    o.anim = false; o.sig = ''; UI.hide = -1; UI.busy = !!UI.play; requestDraw(); if (done) done();
  });
}

/* ---------- 미니맵 ---------- */
var MINI = { img: null, t: 0 };
function drawMini() {
  var m = $('mini'); if (!m || !G) return;
  var w = m.width, h = m.height, x = m.getContext('2d'), i;
  if (!MINI.img) { MINI.img = new Image(); MINI.img.onload = drawMini; MINI.img.src = MAP3D.mini; }
  if (MINI.img.complete && MINI.img.naturalWidth) x.drawImage(MINI.img, 0, 0, w, h); else { x.fillStyle = '#efe9db'; x.fillRect(0, 0, w, h); }
  var sx = w / MAP3D.WX, sz = h / MAP3D.WZ;
  for (i = 0; i < G.bases.length; i++) {
    var b = G.bases[i], p = hexCenter(b.c, b.r), s = b.kind === 'gate' ? 3 : 5;
    x.fillStyle = b.owner >= 0 ? G.factions[b.owner].color : '#a8a295'; x.fillRect(p[0] * sx - s / 2, p[1] * sz - s / 2, s, s);
    x.strokeStyle = b.owner === G.player ? SEAL : 'rgba(27,26,24,0.8)'; x.lineWidth = b.owner === G.player ? 1.6 : 1; x.strokeRect(p[0] * sx - s / 2, p[1] * sz - s / 2, s, s);
  }
  if (!R3 || !CW) return;
  var ty = groundY(cam.x, cam.y), cs = [planeHit(0, CH * 0.12, ty), planeHit(CW, CH * 0.12, ty), planeHit(CW, CH, ty), planeHit(0, CH, ty)];
  if (cs[0] && cs[1] && cs[2] && cs[3]) {
    x.beginPath(); for (i = 0; i < 4; i++) { if (i) x.lineTo(cs[i][0] * sx, cs[i][1] * sz); else x.moveTo(cs[i][0] * sx, cs[i][1] * sz); }
    x.closePath(); x.lineWidth = 1.6; x.strokeStyle = SEAL; x.stroke();
  }
}
function miniGo(e) {
  var m = $('mini'), rc = m.getBoundingClientRect();
  cam.x = (e.clientX - rc.left) / rc.width * MAP3D.WX; cam.y = (e.clientY - rc.top) / rc.height * MAP3D.WZ + camDist() * 0.12; clampCam();
}

/* ---------- 매 프레임 ---------- */
function tick(now) {
  var i, hot = false;
  for (i = UI.anims.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
    var a = UI.anims[i], t = clamp((now - a.t0) / a.dur, 0, 1);
    a.step(t); hot = true;
    if (t >= 1) { UI.anims.splice(i, 1); if (a.done) a.done(); }
  }
  if (R3 && R3.ready) {
    var nzm = cam.z >= 1.15; if (nzm !== R3.nameZoom) { R3.nameZoom = nzm; UI.dirty = true; }
    var fzm = cam.z >= 0.5; if (fzm !== R3.faceZoom) { R3.faceZoom = fzm; UI.dirty = true; }
    if (UI.dirty) { UI.dirty = false; syncScene(); UI.render = true; }
    var nf = 0; for (var fk in R3.fires) { nf++; var fg = R3.fires[fk]; for (i = 0; i < fg.children.length; i++) { var s = 0.9 + 0.22 * Math.sin(now / 90 + i * 2.1 + (+fk)); fg.children[i].scale.set(0.8 * s, 1.25 * s + 0.15 * Math.sin(now / 57 + i), 1); } }
    if (hot || UI.floats.length || (nf && now - (R3.fireT || 0) > 50)) { UI.render = true; R3.fireT = now; }   // 불은 초당 20번만 다시 그린다
    if (UI.render) {
      UI.render = false; applyCam();
      var lk = clamp(0.72 + 0.26 * cam.z, 0.66, 1.12);
      if (R3.labelK !== lk || R3.billboard) {
        R3.labelK = lk; R3.billboard = false;
        var q = R3.cam.quaternion, showU = cam.z >= 0.26, showF = cam.z >= 0.3;
        for (i = 0; i < R3.bases.length; i++) { var bo = R3.bases[i]; if (!bo) continue; bo.g.quaternion.copy(q); labelSize(bo.lab, lk * (cam.z < 0.3 ? 0.82 : 1)); }
        for (var id in R3.units) { var uo = R3.units[id]; uo.g.quaternion.copy(q); labelSize(uo.lab, lk * 0.82); uo.lab.sp.visible = showU; labelSize(uo.fc, lk * 0.92); uo.fc.sp.visible = showU && uo.fcOn; }
        for (var k2 in R3.facs) R3.facs[k2].sp.visible = showF;
        for (var f2 in R3.fires) R3.fires[f2].quaternion.copy(q);
      }
      R3.renderer.render(R3.scene, R3.cam);
      if (now - MINI.t > 120) { MINI.t = now; drawMini(); }
    }
    for (i = UI.floats.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
      var f = UI.floats[i], ft = (now - f.t0) / f.dur;
      if (ft < 0) continue;
      if (ft >= 1) { f.el.remove(); UI.floats.splice(i, 1); continue; }
      var sp = w2s(f.p[0], f.p[1] + (f.ring ? 0 : 0.9), f.p[2]);
      if (!sp) { f.el.style.opacity = 0; continue; }
      if (f.ring) { var rs = pxPerUnit(sp[2]) * (0.6 + ft * 1.3); f.el.style.cssText += ';width:' + rs + 'px;height:' + rs * 0.62 + 'px;opacity:' + (1 - ft) + ';transform:translate(' + (sp[0] - rs / 2) + 'px,' + (sp[1] - rs * 0.31) + 'px)'; }
      else if (f.stamp) { var ss = ft < 0.12 ? 2.5 - 1.5 * (ft / 0.12) : 1; f.el.style.opacity = ft < 0.05 ? ft / 0.05 : (ft < 0.78 ? 1 : (1 - ft) / 0.22); f.el.style.transform = 'translate(-50%,-50%) translate(' + sp[0] + 'px,' + (sp[1] - 22) + 'px) rotate(-5deg) scale(' + ss.toFixed(3) + ')'; }
      else { var pop = ft < 0.1 ? 0.6 + 4 * ft : 1; f.el.style.opacity = ft < 0.72 ? 1 : (1 - ft) / 0.28; f.el.style.transform = 'translate(-50%,-100%) translate(' + sp[0] + 'px,' + (sp[1] - ft * 34 + (f.dy || 0)) + 'px) scale(' + pop.toFixed(3) + ')'; }
    }
  }
  requestAnimationFrame(tick);
}

/* ---------- 입력 ---------- */
var PT = {}, pinch = null, tapOK = false;
function ptList() { var a = []; for (var k in PT) a.push(PT[k]); return a; }
function pickAt(sx, sy) {
  var g = pickGround(sx, sy), gh = g ? worldToHex(g[0], g[1]) : null, su = selUnit();
  if (gh && su) { var gk = K(gh[0], gh[1]); if ((UI.rc && UI.rc.stop[gk]) || (UI.atk || []).some(function (t) { return K(t.c, t.r) === gk; })) return gh; }
  var best = null, bd = 1e9;
  function test(c, r, sp, pri) {
    var p = hexPos(c, r), s = w2s(p[0], p[1], p[2]); if (!s) return;
    var ppu = pxPerUnit(s[2]), ud = sp.userData, hw = ud.w * ppu * 0.40, top = s[1] - ud.top * ppu * 0.86, bot = s[1] + ud.h * ud.ay * ppu * 0.8;
    if (sx < s[0] - hw || sx > s[0] + hw || sy < top || sy > bot) return;
    var d = Math.abs(sx - s[0]) + Math.abs(sy - (top + bot) / 2) - pri * 40;
    if (d < bd) { bd = d; best = [c, r]; }
  }
  for (var id in R3.units) { var o = R3.units[id], u = UMAP[id]; if (u && o.g.visible) test(u.c, u.r, o.sp, 1); }
  for (var i = 0; i < G.bases.length; i++) if (R3.bases[i]) test(G.bases[i].c, G.bases[i].r, R3.bases[i].sp, 0.5);
  return best || gh;
}
function initInput() {
  cv.addEventListener('pointerdown', function (e) {
    audioInit(); bgmKick();
    try { cv.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId); } catch (er) { /* */ }
    PT[e.pointerId] = { x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY, sx: e.clientX, sy: e.clientY, t: performance.now(), moved: false };
    var a = ptList();
    if (a.length === 2) { pinch = { d: Math.hypot(a[0].x - a[1].x, a[0].y - a[1].y) || 1, z: cam.z }; a[0].moved = a[1].moved = true; }
  });
  cv.addEventListener('pointermove', function (e) {
    var p = PT[e.pointerId]; if (!p || !R3) return;
    var rect = cv.getBoundingClientRect(), ox = p.x - rect.left, oy = p.y - rect.top, nx = e.clientX - rect.left, ny = e.clientY - rect.top;
    p.x = e.clientX; p.y = e.clientY;
    var a = ptList();
    if (a.length >= 2 && pinch) {
      var d = Math.hypot(a[0].x - a[1].x, a[0].y - a[1].y) || 1, mx = (a[0].x + a[1].x) / 2 - rect.left, my = (a[0].y + a[1].y) / 2 - rect.top, ty = groundY(cam.x, cam.y);
      var before = planeHit(mx, my, ty);
      cam.z = clamp(pinch.z * d / pinch.d, Z_MIN, Z_MAX); applyCam();
      var after = planeHit(mx, my, ty);
      if (before && after) { cam.x += before[0] - after[0]; cam.y += before[1] - after[1]; }
      clampCam(); return;
    }
    if (!p.moved && Math.hypot(e.clientX - p.sx, e.clientY - p.sy) > 8) p.moved = true;
    if (p.moved) {
      var ty2 = groundY(cam.x, cam.y), h0 = planeHit(ox, oy, ty2), h1 = planeHit(nx, ny, ty2);
      if (h0 && h1) { cam.x += h0[0] - h1[0]; cam.y += h0[1] - h1[1]; clampCam(); }
    }
  });
  function up(e) {
    var p = PT[e.pointerId]; if (!p) return;
    delete PT[e.pointerId];
    if (ptList().length < 2) pinch = null;
    tapOK = (e.type === 'pointerup' && !p.moved && performance.now() - p.t < 700 && ptList().length === 0);
  }
  cv.addEventListener('pointerup', up); cv.addEventListener('pointercancel', up);
  cv.addEventListener('click', function (e) {             // 탭은 click 에서(유령 클릭이 새 창을 누르지 않게)
    bgmKick();
    if (!tapOK || !R3 || !R3.ready || !G) return;
    tapOK = false;
    if (UI.play) { playNudge(); return; }                 // 재생 중에는 화면을 누르면 다음 장면으로
    var rect = cv.getBoundingClientRect(), h = pickAt(e.clientX - rect.left, e.clientY - rect.top);
    if (h) onTap(h[0], h[1]);
  });
  cv.addEventListener('wheel', function (e) {
    e.preventDefault(); if (!R3) return;
    var rect = cv.getBoundingClientRect(), mx = e.clientX - rect.left, my = e.clientY - rect.top, ty = groundY(cam.x, cam.y), before = planeHit(mx, my, ty);
    cam.z = clamp(cam.z * (e.deltaY < 0 ? 1.15 : 1 / 1.15), Z_MIN, Z_MAX); applyCam();
    var after = planeHit(mx, my, ty);
    if (before && after) { cam.x += before[0] - after[0]; cam.y += before[1] - after[1]; }
    clampCam();
  }, { passive: false });
  var mini = $('mini'), md = false;
  mini.addEventListener('pointerdown', function (e) { md = true; try { mini.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId); } catch (er) { /* */ } miniGo(e); });
  mini.addEventListener('pointermove', function (e) { if (md) miniGo(e); });
  mini.addEventListener('pointerup', function () { md = false; }); mini.addEventListener('pointercancel', function () { md = false; });
  window.addEventListener('resize', resize);
}

/* ---------- 소리 ----------
   효과음은 녹음된 소리다(src/sfxdata.js 의 SFX_DATA — 짧은 mp3 를 base64 로 담은 것). 처음 누를 때 풀어 둔다.
   아직 풀리지 않았거나 풀지 못한 소리만 예전의 만든 소리(잡음 + 사인파)로 대신한다. */
var AC = null, NOISE = null;
var SND = { buf: {}, off: {}, last: {}, bus: null, loaded: false, mv: null };
/* 장면 → 낼 소리들 [표본, 크기, 음높이 흔들림, 늦춤(초)]. gap = 같은 장면 소리를 다시 낼 수 있는 가장 짧은 간격(ms). syn = 표본이 없을 때 대신할 만든 소리 */
var CUES = {
  ui:       { s: [['tick', 0.5, 0.06]], gap: 50, syn: 'ui' },
  page:     { s: [['page', 0.55, 0.05]], gap: 150, syn: 'ui' },
  step:     { s: [['stomp', 0.5, 0.08]], gap: 60, syn: 'step' },
  hit:      { s: [['slash', 0.8, 0.06]], gap: 70, syn: 'hit' },
  counter:  { s: [['slash', 0.5, 0.05]], gap: 70, syn: 'hit' },
  arrowhit: { s: [['thud', 0.6, 0.1]], gap: 70, syn: 'hit' },
  siege:    { s: [['crush', 0.6, 0.05], ['thud', 0.7, 0.05, 0.03]], gap: 120, syn: 'hit' },
  big:      { s: [['crush', 0.95, 0.04], ['clang', 0.55, 0.05, 0.03]], gap: 120, syn: 'big' },
  great:    { s: [['crush', 1.0, 0.03], ['clang', 0.75, 0.04, 0.02], ['gong', 0.3, 0, 0.06]], gap: 200, syn: 'big' },
  fail:     { s: [['clang', 0.5, 0.05], ['thud', 0.55, 0.05, 0.03]], gap: 120, syn: 'fail' },
  miss:     { s: [['thud', 0.6, 0.05]], gap: 120, syn: 'fail' },
  fire:     { s: [['fire', 0.8, 0.04]], gap: 300, syn: 'fire' },
  douse:    { s: [['douse', 0.8, 0.03]], gap: 300, syn: 'fire' },
  wind:     { s: [['wind', 0.7, 0.04]], gap: 300, syn: 'fail' },
  drum:     { s: [['drum', 0.85, 0.03]], gap: 150, syn: 'drum' },
  drums:    { s: [['drums', 0.9, 0.02]], gap: 400, syn: 'drum' },
  horn:     { s: [['horn', 0.7, 0.01]], gap: 800, syn: 'drum' },
  deploy:   { s: [['drums', 0.85, 0.02], ['horn', 0.5, 0.01, 0.3]], gap: 600, syn: 'drum' },
  gong:     { s: [['gong', 0.8, 0.01]], gap: 500, syn: 'drum' },
  fall:     { s: [['fall', 0.95, 0]], gap: 3500, syn: 'fall' },          // 함락 장면이 곧바로 한 번 더 불러도 겹치지 않게
  cheerlate: { s: [['cheer', 0.6, 0.02, 1.0]], gap: 3500, syn: '' },
  cheer:    { s: [['cheer', 0.7, 0.02]], gap: 800, syn: 'drum' },
  rout:     { s: [['rout', 0.7, 0.03]], gap: 500, syn: 'big' },
  coin:     { s: [['coin', 0.7, 0.03]], gap: 200, syn: 'ui' },
  hammer:   { s: [['hammer', 0.8, 0.03]], gap: 300, syn: 'drum' },
  duel:     { s: [['duel', 0.95, 0.02]], gap: 800, syn: 'big' },
  shout:    { s: [['shout', 0.6, 0.05]], gap: 250, syn: '' },
  bow:      { s: [['bow', 0.7, 0.08]], gap: 60, syn: 'ui' },
  arrows:   { s: [['arrows', 0.8, 0.04]], gap: 200, syn: 'step' },
  charge:   { s: [['cavalry', 0.85, 0.03]], gap: 500, syn: 'step' }
};
function acMake() {
  if (AC) return AC;
  try {
    AC = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)();
    var n = AC.sampleRate, b = AC.createBuffer(1, n, AC.sampleRate), d = b.getChannelData(0);
    for (var i = 0; i < n; i++) d[i] = Math.random() * 2 - 1;
    NOISE = b;
  } catch (e) { AC = null; }
  return AC;
}
function audioInit() { if (!UI.sound || !acMake()) return; sndLoad(); }
function sndBus() {                                           // 여러 소리가 겹쳐도 찢어지지 않게 한데 모아 누른다
  if (!SND.bus) {
    var g = AC.createGain(); g.gain.value = 0.9;
    try {
      var c = AC.createDynamicsCompressor();
      c.threshold.value = -10; c.knee.value = 8; c.ratio.value = 6; c.attack.value = 0.003; c.release.value = 0.2;
      g.connect(c); c.connect(AC.destination);
    } catch (e) { g.connect(AC.destination); }
    SND.bus = g;
  }
  return SND.bus;
}
function sndLoad() {
  if (SND.loaded || typeof SFX_DATA === 'undefined' || !AC) return;
  SND.loaded = true;
  Object.keys(SFX_DATA).forEach(function (k) {
    try {
      var bin = atob(SFX_DATA[k]), u8 = new Uint8Array(bin.length), i;
      for (i = 0; i < bin.length; i++) u8[i] = bin.charCodeAt(i);
      var ok = function (b) {
        // mp3 를 풀면 앞에 짧은 무음이 붙는다 → 소리가 시작되는 곳부터 틀어 누른 순간과 맞춘다(최대 60ms)
        var d = b.getChannelData(0), lim = Math.min(d.length, Math.floor(b.sampleRate * 0.06)), j = 0;
        while (j < lim && Math.abs(d[j]) < 0.02) j++;
        SND.off[k] = j >= lim ? 0 : Math.max(0, j / b.sampleRate - 0.002);
        SND.buf[k] = b;
      }, bad = function () { /* 이 소리는 만든 소리로 대신한다 */ };
      var pr = AC.decodeAudioData(u8.buffer, ok, bad);
      if (pr && pr.catch) pr.catch(bad);
    } catch (e) { /* */ }
  });
}
function sndPlay(name, vol, jit, delay) {
  var b = SND.buf[name]; if (!b) return null;
  var s = AC.createBufferSource(); s.buffer = b;
  if (jit) s.playbackRate.value = 1 + (Math.random() * 2 - 1) * jit;
  var g = AC.createGain(); g.gain.value = vol;
  s.connect(g); g.connect(sndBus());
  s.start(AC.currentTime + (delay || 0), SND.off[name] || 0);
  return { s: s, g: g };
}
function nz(t, dur, type, freq, q, vol) {
  var s = AC.createBufferSource(); s.buffer = NOISE; s.loop = true;
  var f = AC.createBiquadFilter(); f.type = type; f.frequency.value = freq; f.Q.value = q;
  var g = AC.createGain(); g.gain.setValueAtTime(vol, t); g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, t + dur);
  s.connect(f); f.connect(g); g.connect(AC.destination); s.start(t, Math.random() * 0.5); s.stop(t + dur + 0.02);
}
function thump(t, f0, f1, dur, vol) {
  var o = AC.createOscillator(); o.type = 'sine'; o.frequency.setValueAtTime(f0, t); o.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(f1, t + dur);
  var g = AC.createGain(); g.gain.setValueAtTime(vol, t); g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, t + dur);
  o.connect(g); g.connect(AC.destination); o.start(t); o.stop(t + dur + 0.02);
}
function sfxSyn(kind) {                                       // 만든 소리(대신 쓰는 것)
  var t = AC.currentTime;
  if (kind === 'step') nz(t, 0.06, 'lowpass', 420, 0.7, 0.10);
  else if (kind === 'ui') nz(t, 0.035, 'bandpass', 1900, 1.2, 0.06);
  else if (kind === 'hit') { nz(t, 0.16, 'bandpass', 1100, 0.9, 0.34); nz(t + 0.03, 0.22, 'lowpass', 300, 0.7, 0.3); thump(t, 120, 48, 0.18, 0.5); }
  else if (kind === 'big') { nz(t, 0.3, 'bandpass', 800, 0.7, 0.45); thump(t, 110, 38, 0.4, 0.8); nz(t + 0.08, 0.5, 'lowpass', 220, 0.6, 0.4); }
  else if (kind === 'fire') { nz(t, 0.7, 'bandpass', 2600, 0.6, 0.16); nz(t, 0.9, 'lowpass', 500, 0.5, 0.18); }
  else if (kind === 'drum') { thump(t, 95, 42, 0.5, 0.8); nz(t, 0.12, 'lowpass', 260, 0.7, 0.25); }
  else if (kind === 'fall') { thump(t, 95, 40, 0.5, 0.8); thump(t + 0.28, 95, 40, 0.6, 0.8); nz(t, 0.9, 'lowpass', 350, 0.6, 0.3); }
  else if (kind === 'fail') nz(t, 0.2, 'lowpass', 240, 0.8, 0.2);
}
function sfx(kind) {
  if (!AC || !UI.sound) return;
  try {
    if (AC.state === 'suspended') AC.resume();
    var q = CUES[kind];
    if (!q) { sfxSyn(kind); return; }
    var now = performance.now();
    if (now - (SND.last[kind] || 0) < q.gap) return;
    SND.last[kind] = now;
    var n = 0;
    for (var i = 0; i < q.s.length; i++) if (sndPlay(q.s[i][0], q.s[i][1], q.s[i][2], q.s[i][3])) n++;
    if (!n && q.syn) sfxSyn(q.syn);
  } catch (e) { /* 소리는 없어도 된다 */ }
}
/* 부대가 움직이는 동안의 소리: 보병은 발소리, 기병은 말발굽, 충차·수송대는 수레. ms 뒤에 잦아든다 */
function sfxMoveStop(quick) {
  var m = SND.mv; SND.mv = null;
  if (!m || !AC) return;
  try { var t = AC.currentTime; m.g.gain.cancelScheduledValues(t); m.g.gain.setValueAtTime(Math.max(0.0001, m.g.gain.value), t); m.g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, t + (quick ? 0.05 : 0.14)); m.s.stop(t + 0.2); } catch (e) { /* */ }
}
function sfxMove(type, ms) {
  if (!AC || !UI.sound) return;
  try {
    if (AC.state === 'suspended') AC.resume();
    sfxMoveStop(true);
    var name = type === 'cav' ? 'gallop' : (type === 'ram' || type === 'trans' ? 'cart' : 'march'), b = SND.buf[name];
    if (!b) { sfxSyn('step'); return; }
    var s = AC.createBufferSource(), g = AC.createGain(), t = AC.currentTime, dur = Math.max(0.25, ms / 1000), off = SND.off[name] || 0, vol = name === 'cart' ? 0.55 : 0.5;
    s.buffer = b; s.playbackRate.value = 1 + (Math.random() * 2 - 1) * 0.04;
    if (dur > b.duration - off - 0.3) { s.loop = true; s.loopStart = off + 0.05; s.loopEnd = Math.max(off + 0.4, b.duration - 0.45); }   // 길게 가면 가운데 토막을 되풀이
    g.gain.setValueAtTime(vol, t); g.gain.setValueAtTime(vol, t + Math.max(0.05, dur - 0.16)); g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, t + dur + 0.06);
    s.connect(g); g.connect(sndBus()); s.start(t, off); s.stop(t + dur + 0.1);
    var m = { s: s, g: g }; SND.mv = m;
    s.onended = function () { if (SND.mv === m) SND.mv = null; };
  } catch (e) { /* */ }
}

/* ---------- 배경 음악 ----------
   1) 같이 놓인 bgm/<곡>.mp3 를 튼다. 장면(제목·평시·긴장·전투·승리·패배)마다 곡이 정해져 있고, 여러 곡이면 차례로 돌아간다.
   2) 파일이 없거나 틀 수 없으면 코드로 만든 음악을 튼다: 뜯는 현(Karplus–Strong), 피리, 북, 낮게 깔리는 현. 오음 음계. */
var BGM_TRACKS = { title: ['title'], field: ['field1', 'field2'], tense: ['tense'], battle: ['battle1', 'battle2'], victory: ['victory'], defeat: ['defeat'] };
var BGM = { el: null, name: '', want: '', vol: 0.5, dead: {}, tok: 0, mode: '', idx: {}, file: '' };
var SYN = { g: null, timer: 0, t: 0, step: 0, style: null, name: '', bufs: {}, pad: null, last: 5, hold: 0 };
var SCALE_MAJ = [0, 2, 4, 7, 9], SCALE_MIN = [0, 3, 5, 7, 10];
var SYN_STYLE = {
  title:   { bpm: 56,  base: 293.66, pad: 73.42, sc: SCALE_MAJ, gain: 2.8 },
  field:   { bpm: 66,  base: 329.63, pad: 82.41, sc: SCALE_MAJ, gain: 3.0 },
  battle:  { bpm: 112, base: 293.66, pad: 73.42, sc: SCALE_MIN, gain: 1.5 },
  victory: { bpm: 80,  base: 392.00, pad: 98.00, sc: SCALE_MAJ, gain: 2.6 },
  defeat:  { bpm: 46,  base: 261.63, pad: 65.41, sc: SCALE_MIN, gain: 3.0 }
};
function synBus(ctx) {                      // 음악 버스: 음량 → 컴프레서 → 출력
  var g = ctx.createGain(), c = ctx.createDynamicsCompressor();
  c.threshold.value = -15; c.knee.value = 8; c.ratio.value = 4; c.attack.value = 0.008; c.release.value = 0.25;
  g.connect(c); c.connect(ctx.destination);
  return g;
}
function noteHz(st, deg) { var o = Math.floor(deg / 5), d = ((deg % 5) + 5) % 5; return st.base * Math.pow(2, o + st.sc[d] / 12); }
function ksBuf(hz) {
  var key = Math.round(hz * 4); if (SYN.bufs[key]) return SYN.bufs[key];
  var sr = AC.sampleRate, n = Math.floor(sr * 2.4), b = AC.createBuffer(1, n, sr), d = b.getChannelData(0), N = Math.max(2, Math.round(sr / hz)), ring = new Float32Array(N), i, k = 0, lp = 0;
  var damp = hz > 600 ? 0.9925 : (hz > 250 ? 0.995 : 0.9968);
  for (i = 0; i < N; i++) { lp += ((Math.random() * 2 - 1) - lp) * 0.5; ring[i] = lp; }           // 둥글린 초기 잡음 → 부드러운 음색
  for (i = 0; i < n; i++) { var j = k + 1 === N ? 0 : k + 1, y = ring[k]; d[i] = y; ring[k] = (y + ring[j]) * 0.5 * damp; k = j; }
  for (i = 0; i < 3000 && i < n; i++) d[n - 1 - i] *= i / 3000;
  return (SYN.bufs[key] = b);
}
function synOut(node, pan) {
  if (pan && AC.createStereoPanner) { var p = AC.createStereoPanner(); p.pan.value = pan; node.connect(p); p.connect(SYN.g); } else node.connect(SYN.g);
}
function pluck(t, hz, vol, pan) {
  var s = AC.createBufferSource(); s.buffer = ksBuf(hz);
  var g = AC.createGain(); g.gain.value = vol; s.connect(g); synOut(g, pan); s.start(t);
}
function flute(t, hz, dur, vol) {
  var o = AC.createOscillator(); o.type = 'triangle'; o.frequency.setValueAtTime(hz, t);
  var l = AC.createOscillator(); l.frequency.value = 5.1;
  var lg = AC.createGain(); lg.gain.setValueAtTime(0, t); lg.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(hz * 0.007, t + dur * 0.6); l.connect(lg); lg.connect(o.frequency);
  var f = AC.createBiquadFilter(); f.type = 'lowpass'; f.frequency.value = hz * 2.4;
  var g = AC.createGain(); g.gain.setValueAtTime(0.0001, t); g.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(vol, t + 0.14); g.gain.setValueAtTime(vol, t + dur * 0.72); g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, t + dur);
  o.connect(f); f.connect(g); synOut(g, 0.15); o.start(t); l.start(t); o.stop(t + dur + 0.05); l.stop(t + dur + 0.05);
  var s = AC.createBufferSource(); s.buffer = NOISE; s.loop = true;                               // 숨소리
  var bf = AC.createBiquadFilter(); bf.type = 'bandpass'; bf.frequency.value = hz * 2; bf.Q.value = 2;
  var bg = AC.createGain(); bg.gain.setValueAtTime(0.0001, t); bg.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(vol * 0.10, t + 0.1); bg.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, t + dur);
  s.connect(bf); bf.connect(bg); synOut(bg, 0.15); s.start(t); s.stop(t + dur + 0.05);
}
function drum(t, vol, low) {
  var o = AC.createOscillator(); o.type = 'sine'; o.frequency.setValueAtTime(low ? 88 : 135, t); o.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(low ? 38 : 62, t + 0.26);
  var g = AC.createGain(); g.gain.setValueAtTime(vol, t); g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, t + (low ? 0.55 : 0.28));
  o.connect(g); g.connect(SYN.g); o.start(t); o.stop(t + 0.62);
  var s = AC.createBufferSource(); s.buffer = NOISE; var f = AC.createBiquadFilter(); f.type = 'lowpass'; f.frequency.value = low ? 320 : 1100;
  var g2 = AC.createGain(); g2.gain.setValueAtTime(vol * 0.45, t); g2.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, t + 0.11);
  s.connect(f); f.connect(g2); g2.connect(SYN.g); s.start(t, Math.random() * 0.5); s.stop(t + 0.14);
}
function padOn(hz, level) {
  var out = AC.createGain(); out.gain.value = 0.0001;
  var f = AC.createBiquadFilter(); f.type = 'lowpass'; f.frequency.value = 380; f.Q.value = 0.4;
  var os = [hz, hz * 1.004, hz * 1.5, hz * 2.006].map(function (h, i) { var o = AC.createOscillator(); o.type = 'sawtooth'; o.frequency.value = h; var g = AC.createGain(); g.gain.value = i < 2 ? 0.5 : 0.2; o.connect(g); g.connect(f); o.start(); return o; });
  f.connect(out); out.connect(SYN.g); out.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(level, AC.currentTime + 3.5);
  return { out: out, os: os };
}
function padOff(p) {
  var t = AC.currentTime;
  try { p.out.gain.cancelScheduledValues(t); p.out.gain.setValueAtTime(Math.max(0.0001, p.out.gain.value), t); p.out.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, t + 1.2); p.os.forEach(function (o) { o.stop(t + 1.3); }); } catch (e) { /* */ }
}
function rint(n) { return Math.floor(Math.random() * n); }
function walk(lo, hi) { SYN.last = clamp(SYN.last + [-2, -1, -1, 0, 1, 1, 2][rint(7)], lo, hi); return SYN.last; }
function synStep(name, s, t, st) {
  var b16 = s % 16, b32 = s % 32;
  if (name === 'battle') {
    var pat = [1, 0, 2, 2, 1, 0, 2, 1][s % 8];
    if (pat === 1) drum(t, 0.62, true); else if (pat === 2) drum(t, 0.26, false);
    pluck(t, noteHz(st, (s % 4 === 2 ? -3 : -5)) / 2, 0.20, -0.2);                                   // 낮은 현의 되풀이
    if (b32 === 30 || b32 === 31) { pluck(t, noteHz(st, 5 + (b32 - 30) * 2), 0.20, 0.3); pluck(t + 60 / st.bpm / 4, noteHz(st, 6 + (b32 - 30) * 2), 0.18, 0.3); }
    else if (Math.random() < (s % 2 ? 0.22 : 0.42)) pluck(t, noteHz(st, walk(2, 11)), 0.24, 0.25);
    if (b32 === 0) flute(t, noteHz(st, [5, 7, 4, 8][Math.floor(s / 32) % 4]), 60 / st.bpm * 6, 0.07);
    return;
  }
  if (name === 'title' || name === 'defeat') {
    if (b16 === 0) pluck(t, noteHz(st, -5) / 2, 0.30, -0.15);
    if (b16 === 8 && name === 'title') pluck(t, noteHz(st, -2) / 2, 0.22, -0.15);
    if (s >= SYN.hold && s % 2 === 0 && Math.random() < 0.72) { var beats = [2, 3, 3, 4, 6][rint(5)], dg = name === 'defeat' ? walk(-2, 5) : walk(0, 8); flute(t, noteHz(st, dg), 60 / st.bpm * beats * 0.95, 0.085); SYN.hold = s + beats * 2; }
    if (s % 2 === 1 && Math.random() < 0.22) pluck(t, noteHz(st, [0, 2, 4, 5, 7][rint(5)]), 0.14, 0.3);
    if (b32 === 0 && name === 'title') drum(t, 0.22, true);
    return;
  }
  // 평시·승리: 뜯는 현의 가락
  if (name === 'victory' && s < 10) { pluck(t, noteHz(st, s), 0.26, 0.2); if (s === 0) drum(t, 0.5, true); return; }
  if (b16 === 0) pluck(t, noteHz(st, -5) / 2, 0.30, -0.2);
  else if (b16 === 8) pluck(t, noteHz(st, -2) / 2, 0.24, -0.2);
  else if (b16 === 12 && Math.random() < 0.5) pluck(t, noteHz(st, -3) / 2, 0.18, -0.2);
  if (b32 === 28) { SYN.last = [0, 5, 2][rint(3)]; pluck(t, noteHz(st, SYN.last), 0.26, 0.25); }      // 구절 끝은 으뜸음 쪽으로
  else if (b32 < 28 && Math.random() < (s % 2 ? 0.34 : 0.64)) {
    var d2 = walk(0, 10); pluck(t, noteHz(st, d2), 0.24 - d2 * 0.006, 0.25);
    if (Math.random() < 0.16) pluck(t + 0.02, noteHz(st, d2 - 2), 0.12, 0.35);
  }
  if (b32 === 16 && Math.random() < 0.6) flute(t, noteHz(st, [5, 4, 7, 2][rint(4)]), 60 / st.bpm * 5, 0.05);
  if (b32 === 0) drum(t, 0.16, true);
}
function synTick() {
  if (!AC || !SYN.style || AC.state !== 'running') return;
  var sp = 60 / SYN.style.bpm / 2;
  if (SYN.t < AC.currentTime) SYN.t = AC.currentTime + 0.05;
  while (SYN.t < AC.currentTime + 0.4) { try { synStep(SYN.name, SYN.step, SYN.t, SYN.style); } catch (e) { /* */ } SYN.step++; SYN.t += sp; }
}
function synStart(name) {
  if (!acMake()) return;
  try { if (AC.state === 'suspended') AC.resume(); } catch (e) { /* */ }
  synStop();
  if (!SYN.g) SYN.g = synBus(AC);
  SYN.style = SYN_STYLE[name] || SYN_STYLE.field;
  var t = AC.currentTime; SYN.g.gain.cancelScheduledValues(t); SYN.g.gain.setValueAtTime(0.0001, t + 0.05); SYN.g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(BGM.vol * SYN.style.gain, t + 1.6); SYN.name = name; SYN.step = 0; SYN.hold = 0; SYN.t = t + 0.2; SYN.last = 5;
  SYN.pad = padOn(SYN.style.pad, name === 'battle' ? 0.07 : 0.05);
  SYN.timer = setInterval(synTick, 110);
  BGM.mode = 'syn';
}
function synStop() {
  if (SYN.timer) { clearInterval(SYN.timer); SYN.timer = 0; }
  if (SYN.pad) { padOff(SYN.pad); SYN.pad = null; }
  SYN.style = null;
}
function bgmEl() {
  if (!BGM.el) { BGM.el = new Audio(); BGM.el.preload = 'auto'; }
  return BGM.el;
}
function bgmFade(a, to, ms, end, tok) {
  var from = a.volume, t0 = performance.now();
  (function step() {
    if (tok !== BGM.tok) return;
    var t = clamp((performance.now() - t0) / ms, 0, 1);
    try { a.volume = from + (to - from) * t; } catch (e) { /* */ }
    if (t < 1) setTimeout(step, 40); else if (end) end();
  })();
}
function playBgm(name, once) {
  BGM.want = name;
  if (!UI.music) { stopBgm(); return; }
  if (BGM.name === name) return;
  var a0 = BGM.el, was = BGM.mode === 'file' && a0 && !a0.paused;
  stopBgm(was);                                            // 틀던 곡이 있으면 끊지 않고 잦아들게 한 뒤 바꾼다
  var tok = BGM.tok; BGM.name = name;
  if (was) bgmFade(a0, 0, 320, function () { bgmTrack(name, once, true, tok); }, tok);
  else bgmTrack(name, once, true, tok);
}
/* 장면의 다음 곡을 튼다. 못 트는 곡은 건너뛰고, 다 못 틀면 만든 음악 */
function bgmTrack(name, once, fadeIn, tok) {
  if (tok !== BGM.tok) return;
  var list = (BGM_TRACKS[name] || [name]).filter(function (f) { return !BGM.dead[f]; });
  if (!list.length) { synStart(SYN_STYLE[name] ? name : 'field'); return; }
  var i = (BGM.idx[name] || 0) % list.length, file = list[i], a = bgmEl();
  BGM.idx[name] = i + 1; BGM.file = file;
  var next = function () { if (tok === BGM.tok && !BGM.dead[file]) { BGM.dead[file] = 1; bgmTrack(name, once, fadeIn, tok); } };
  a.loop = !once && list.length === 1;
  a.onerror = next;                                         // 파일이 없다 → 다음 곡, 없으면 만든 음악
  a.onended = function () { if (tok === BGM.tok && !once) bgmTrack(name, once, false, tok); };
  a.src = 'bgm/' + file + '.mp3';
  try { a.volume = fadeIn ? 0 : BGM.vol; } catch (e) { /* */ }
  var p = a.play();
  if (p && p.then) p.then(function () { if (tok === BGM.tok) { BGM.mode = 'file'; if (fadeIn) bgmFade(a, BGM.vol, 900, null, tok); } })
    .catch(function (e) { if (tok !== BGM.tok) return; if (e && e.name === 'NotAllowedError') { BGM.name = ''; BGM.idx[name] = i; } else if (!e || e.name !== 'AbortError') next(); });
}
function stopBgm(keep) {
  BGM.tok++; BGM.name = ''; BGM.mode = '';
  var a = BGM.el; if (a) { a.onerror = null; a.onended = null; if (!keep) { try { a.pause(); } catch (e) { /* */ } } }
  if (SYN.g && AC) { var t = AC.currentTime; try { SYN.g.gain.cancelScheduledValues(t); SYN.g.gain.setValueAtTime(Math.max(0.0001, SYN.g.gain.value), t); SYN.g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, t + 0.5); } catch (e) { /* */ } }
  synStop();
}
function bgmKick() { if (UI.music && BGM.want && !BGM.name) playBgm(BGM.want); }   // 첫 조작 뒤에야 소리를 낼 수 있다
document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', function () {
  try { if (document.hidden) { if (AC && AC.state === 'running') AC.suspend(); if (BGM.el && BGM.mode === 'file') BGM.el.pause(); } else { if (AC && (UI.sound || UI.music)) AC.resume(); if (BGM.el && BGM.mode === 'file' && UI.music) BGM.el.play(); } } catch (e) { /* */ }
});

=====8<===== src/fx.js
/* ===== 천하도 — 먹 붓질 연출: 이동·공격·전법·계략·함락 =====
   그림은 모두 코드로 그린 흰 붓 자국(투명 바탕)이고, 쓸 때 먹빛·인주빛으로 물들인다.
   한 번의 행동은 하나의 anim 위에 올린 시간표(fxTimeline)로 돌아가므로, 재생 중 화면을 누르면 한꺼번에 끝까지 넘어간다. */
var FX = { tex: {}, live: [] };
var FXK = 1.32;                                          // 붓 자국의 크기(부대 그림 크기에 맞춤)
var FX_INK = 0x1b1a18, FX_SEAL = 0xb5332a, FX_WASH = 0x6f6a60, FX_INDIGO = 0x2f4f6f, FX_FIRE = 0xd8572a;

function fxRng(seed) { var s = seed | 0; return function () { s = (s * 1664525 + 1013904223) | 0; return ((s >>> 8) & 0xffff) / 65535; }; }
function fxCanvas(w, h) { var c = document.createElement('canvas'); c.width = w; c.height = h; return c; }
function fxTexOf(c) { var t = new THREE.CanvasTexture(c); t.minFilter = THREE.LinearFilter; t.generateMipmaps = false; return t; }

/* 붓 자국 그림들 */
function fxTex(name) {
  if (FX.tex[name]) return FX.tex[name];
  var c, x, i, j, R = fxRng(name.length * 7919 + name.charCodeAt(0) * 131), g;
  if (name === 'stroke') {                                   // 곧게 그은 한 획: 갈라진 붓털, 시작은 짙고 끝은 마른 붓
    c = fxCanvas(512, 96); x = c.getContext('2d');
    for (i = 0; i < 46; i++) {
      var y = 10 + 76 * (i / 45) + (R() - 0.5) * 3, x0 = 6 + R() * 26, x1 = 512 - R() * (40 + 150 * Math.abs(i / 45 - 0.5));
      x.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,' + (0.35 + 0.65 * R()).toFixed(2) + ')'; x.lineWidth = 1.2 + R() * 2.6;
      x.beginPath(); x.moveTo(x0, y); x.bezierCurveTo(170, y + (R() - 0.5) * 5, 340, y + (R() - 0.5) * 5, x1, y + (R() - 0.5) * 4); x.stroke();
    }
    x.globalCompositeOperation = 'destination-out';          // 마른 붓: 군데군데 비어 있다
    for (i = 0; i < 90; i++) { x.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,' + (0.3 + 0.6 * R()).toFixed(2) + ')'; var gx = 150 + R() * 360; x.fillRect(gx, R() * 96, 8 + R() * 46 * (gx / 512), 1 + R() * 2.2); }
    x.globalCompositeOperation = 'destination-in';           // 위아래로 볼록한 모양
    g = x.createRadialGradient(256, 48, 10, 256, 48, 262); g.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(0,0,0,1)'); g.addColorStop(0.9, 'rgba(0,0,0,1)'); g.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(0,0,0,0)');
    x.save(); x.translate(256, 48); x.scale(1, 0.19); x.translate(-256, -48); x.fillStyle = g; x.fillRect(-10, -400, 540, 900); x.restore();
  } else if (name === 'arc') {                               // 휘둘러 벤 초승달 획
    c = fxCanvas(256, 256); x = c.getContext('2d'); x.translate(128, 150);
    for (i = 0; i < 40; i++) {
      var rr2 = 78 + 34 * (i / 39) + (R() - 0.5) * 2, a0 = Math.PI * (1.12 + R() * 0.07), a1 = Math.PI * (1.9 - R() * 0.12 - 0.1 * Math.abs(i / 39 - 0.45));
      x.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,' + (0.3 + 0.7 * R()).toFixed(2) + ')'; x.lineWidth = 1 + R() * 2.4;
      x.beginPath(); x.arc(0, 0, rr2, a0, a1); x.stroke();
    }
    x.globalCompositeOperation = 'destination-out';
    for (i = 0; i < 60; i++) { var aa = Math.PI * (1.45 + R() * 0.45), r3 = 78 + R() * 34; x.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,' + (0.4 + 0.5 * R()).toFixed(2) + ')'; x.beginPath(); x.arc(Math.cos(aa) * r3, Math.sin(aa) * r3, 0.8 + R() * 1.6, 0, 6.3); x.fill(); }
    x.setTransform(1, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0); x.globalCompositeOperation = 'destination-in';
    g = x.createLinearGradient(20, 0, 236, 0); g.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(0,0,0,0)'); g.addColorStop(0.12, 'rgba(0,0,0,1)'); g.addColorStop(0.7, 'rgba(0,0,0,0.95)'); g.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(0,0,0,0)');
    x.fillStyle = g; x.fillRect(0, 0, 256, 256);
  } else if (name === 'splat') {                             // 튄 먹물
    c = fxCanvas(256, 256); x = c.getContext('2d'); x.fillStyle = '#fff';
    x.beginPath();
    for (i = 0; i <= 28; i++) { var a = i / 28 * 6.2832, rd = 46 + 26 * R() + (i % 4 === 0 ? 20 * R() : 0); if (i) x.lineTo(128 + Math.cos(a) * rd, 128 + Math.sin(a) * rd); else x.moveTo(128 + rd, 128); }
    x.closePath(); x.fill();
    for (i = 0; i < 26; i++) { var a2 = R() * 6.2832, d2 = 62 + R() * 58, s2 = 1.5 + R() * 7 * (1 - (d2 - 62) / 70); x.globalAlpha = 0.5 + 0.5 * R(); x.beginPath(); x.ellipse(128 + Math.cos(a2) * d2, 128 + Math.sin(a2) * d2, s2 * (1 + R()), s2, a2, 0, 6.3); x.fill(); }
    x.globalAlpha = 1; x.globalCompositeOperation = 'destination-out';
    g = x.createRadialGradient(118, 118, 2, 128, 128, 60); g.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(0,0,0,0.42)'); g.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(0,0,0,0)'); x.fillStyle = g; x.fillRect(0, 0, 256, 256);
  } else if (name === 'puff') {                              // 담묵 먼지
    c = fxCanvas(128, 128); x = c.getContext('2d');
    for (i = 0; i < 7; i++) { var px = 64 + (R() - 0.5) * 52, py = 66 + (R() - 0.5) * 40, pr = 20 + R() * 22; g = x.createRadialGradient(px, py, 1, px, py, pr); g.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(255,255,255,0.5)'); g.addColorStop(0.6, 'rgba(255,255,255,0.22)'); g.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(255,255,255,0)'); x.fillStyle = g; x.fillRect(0, 0, 128, 128); }
  } else if (name === 'arrow') {                             // 날아가는 화살 한 줄기
    c = fxCanvas(128, 24); x = c.getContext('2d');
    g = x.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 128, 0); g.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(255,255,255,0)'); g.addColorStop(0.35, 'rgba(255,255,255,0.55)'); g.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(255,255,255,1)');
    x.strokeStyle = g; x.lineWidth = 2.4; x.lineCap = 'round'; x.beginPath(); x.moveTo(4, 12); x.lineTo(118, 12); x.stroke();
    x.fillStyle = '#fff'; x.beginPath(); x.moveTo(126, 12); x.lineTo(108, 6); x.lineTo(112, 12); x.lineTo(108, 18); x.closePath(); x.fill();
  } else if (name === 'spark') {                             // 부딪힌 자리의 짧은 획들
    c = fxCanvas(128, 128); x = c.getContext('2d'); x.translate(64, 64); x.strokeStyle = '#fff'; x.lineCap = 'round';
    for (i = 0; i < 11; i++) { var a3 = i / 11 * 6.2832 + R() * 0.4, r0 = 12 + R() * 10, r1 = 34 + R() * 26; x.lineWidth = 1.6 + R() * 3.4; x.globalAlpha = 0.55 + 0.45 * R(); x.beginPath(); x.moveTo(Math.cos(a3) * r0, Math.sin(a3) * r0); x.lineTo(Math.cos(a3) * r1, Math.sin(a3) * r1); x.stroke(); }
  } else if (name === 'swirl') {                             // 소용돌이(혼란·위보)
    c = fxCanvas(256, 256); x = c.getContext('2d'); x.translate(128, 128); x.fillStyle = '#fff';
    for (j = 0; j < 2; j++) for (i = 0; i < 240; i++) { var tt = i / 239, a4 = j * Math.PI + tt * 7.6, r4 = 14 + tt * 100; x.globalAlpha = (0.25 + 0.6 * (1 - tt)) * (0.6 + 0.4 * R()); x.beginPath(); x.arc(Math.cos(a4) * r4, Math.sin(a4) * r4, 1.6 + 5.2 * Math.sin(Math.PI * tt), 0, 6.3); x.fill(); }
  } else return null;
  return (FX.tex[name] = fxTexOf(c));
}

/* 지도 위에 띄우는 붓 자국 하나 */
function fxSprite(name, color, order) {
  var tex = name === 'enso' ? (R3.zoneTex || (R3.zoneTex = ensoTexture())) : (name === 'flame' ? R3.flame : fxTex(name));
  var sp = new THREE.Sprite(new THREE.SpriteMaterial({ map: tex, color: color === undefined ? FX_INK : color, transparent: true, opacity: 0, depthTest: false, depthWrite: false }));
  sp.renderOrder = order || 9; R3.objs.add(sp); FX.live.push(sp);
  return sp;
}
function fxKill(sp) { var i = FX.live.indexOf(sp); if (i >= 0) FX.live.splice(i, 1); R3.objs.remove(sp); sp.material.dispose(); }
function fxClear() { while (FX.live.length) fxKill(FX.live[0]); }
function easeOut(t) { return 1 - (1 - t) * (1 - t); }
function easeIO(t) { return t < 0.5 ? 2 * t * t : 1 - 2 * (1 - t) * (1 - t); }
/* 화면에서 본 두 점 사이의 각도(스프라이트 회전에 쓴다) */
function fxAngle(p, q) {
  var a = w2s(p[0], p[1], p[2]), b = w2s(q[0], q[1], q[2]);
  if (!a || !b) return 0;
  return Math.atan2(-(b[1] - a[1]), b[0] - a[0]);
}

/* 시간표: tracks = [{a, b, on, step(0..1), off}] (밀리초). 하나의 anim 으로 돌린다 */
function fxTimeline(total, tracks, done) {
  var i;
  for (i = 0; i < tracks.length; i++) tracks[i].st = 0;
  anim(Math.max(30, total), function (t) {
    var ms = t * total;
    for (var k = 0; k < tracks.length; k++) {
      var tr = tracks[k];
      if (tr.st === 2 || (ms < tr.a && t < 1)) continue;
      if (tr.st === 0) { tr.st = 1; if (tr.on) tr.on(); }
      var u = (t >= 1 || tr.b <= tr.a) ? 1 : clamp((ms - tr.a) / (tr.b - tr.a), 0, 1);
      if (tr.step) tr.step(u);
      if (u >= 1) { tr.st = 2; if (tr.off) tr.off(); }
    }
  }, done);
  return total;
}
/* 붓 자국 하나를 나타냈다 지우는 길: 크기 s0→s1, 진하기는 금세 올랐다가 서서히 빠진다 */
function fxMark(tracks, a, dur, name, color, p, s0, s1, rot, opt) {
  var sp = null; opt = opt || {};
  tracks.push({ a: a, b: a + dur,
    on: function () { sp = fxSprite(name, color, opt.order); sp.position.set(p[0], p[1] + (opt.dy === undefined ? 0.7 : opt.dy), p[2]); sp.material.rotation = rot || 0; },
    step: function (u) {
      var s = (s0 + (s1 - s0) * easeOut(u)) * FXK, k = opt.aspect || 1;
      sp.scale.set(s, s * k, 1);
      sp.material.opacity = (opt.alpha || 1) * (u < 0.15 ? u / 0.15 : Math.pow(1 - (u - 0.15) / 0.85, opt.fade || 1.3));
      if (opt.spin) sp.material.rotation = (rot || 0) + opt.spin * u;
      if (opt.rise) sp.position.y = p[1] + (opt.dy === undefined ? 0.7 : opt.dy) + opt.rise * u;
    },
    off: function () { if (sp) fxKill(sp); } });
}
/* 걸음마다 이는 먼지 */
function fxDust(x, y, z, k) {
  if (!R3 || !R3.ready || cam.z < 0.34) return;
  var sp = fxSprite('puff', FX_WASH, 2.5), ox = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.3;
  sp.position.set(x + ox, y + 0.12, z + 0.1);
  anim(520, function (t) { var s = (0.5 + 0.9 * t) * (k || 1); sp.scale.set(s, s * 0.7, 1); sp.material.opacity = 0.55 * (1 - t); sp.position.y = y + 0.12 + 0.25 * t; }, function () { fxKill(sp); });
}
/* 화면 흔들기(큰 타격) */
function fxShake(px, ms) {
  if (!cv) return;
  anim(ms || 220, function (t) { var k = (1 - t) * px; cv.style.transform = t >= 1 ? '' : 'translate(' + (Math.sin(t * 40) * k).toFixed(1) + 'px,' + (Math.cos(t * 33) * k * 0.7).toFixed(1) + 'px)'; });
}
/* 낙관처럼 찍히는 글씨 */
function fxStamp(c, r, text, delay) {
  var el = document.createElement('div'); el.className = 'stamp'; el.textContent = text; el.style.opacity = 0;
  $('fx').appendChild(el);
  UI.floats.push({ p: hexPos(c, r), el: el, t0: performance.now() + (delay || 0), dur: 1700, stamp: true }); UI.render = true;
}

/* 부대·거점 그림을 다루는 작은 도우미 */
function fxPut(o, p) { o.g.position.set(p[0], p[1], p[2]); if (o.ring) o.ring.position.set(p[0], p[1] + 0.04, p[2]); }
function fxFace(o, fromP, toP) {
  if (!o.sp || Math.abs(toP[0] - fromP[0]) < 0.2) return;
  var f = toP[0] < fromP[0] ? -1 : 1;
  if (f !== o.face) { o.face = f; setSprite(o.sp, o.sp.userData.key, UNIT_W, f < 0); }
}
function fxLerp(p, q, t) { var x = p[0] + (q[0] - p[0]) * t, z = p[2] + (q[2] - p[2]) * t; return [x, groundY(x, z), z]; }
/* 맞아서 떨리고 붉게 물드는 길 */
function fxHurt(tracks, a, o, home, amp) {
  if (!o) return;
  tracks.push({ a: a, b: a + 330,
    step: function (u) {
      var k = 1 - u, dx = Math.sin(u * 20) * k * (amp || 0.13);
      o.g.position.set(home[0] + dx, home[1], home[2]);
      if (o.sp) o.sp.material.color.setRGB(1, 1 - 0.55 * k, 1 - 0.62 * k);
    },
    off: function () { o.g.position.set(home[0], home[1], home[2]); if (o.sp) o.sp.material.color.setRGB(1, 1, 1); } });
}
/* 흩어지며 사라지는 길(궤멸) */
function fxVanish(tracks, a, o, p) {
  if (!o) return;
  fxMark(tracks, a, 900, 'splat', FX_INK, p, 0.7, 2.3, Math.random() * 6.28, { dy: 0.3, alpha: 0.8, fade: 1.0, order: 2.6 });
  tracks.push({ a: a, b: a + 560,
    step: function (u) {
      var k = 1 - u;
      o.sp.material.opacity = k; o.lab.sp.material.opacity = k; if (o.fc) o.fc.sp.material.opacity = k; if (o.ring) o.ring.material.opacity = 0.6 * k;
      o.g.position.y = p[1] - 0.25 * u;
    },
    off: function () { o.g.visible = false; if (o.ring) o.ring.visible = false; o.gone = true; } });
}
/* 화살 무리: n 발이 포물선으로 날아간다 */
function fxVolley(tracks, a, from, to, n, color, spread) {
  var dist = Math.sqrt((to[0] - from[0]) * (to[0] - from[0]) + (to[2] - from[2]) * (to[2] - from[2])), h = 0.7 + 0.16 * dist, fly = 250 + 22 * dist;
  for (var i = 0; i < n; i++) (function (i) {
    var sp = null, ox = (Math.random() - 0.5) * (spread || 0.5), oz = (Math.random() - 0.5) * (spread || 0.5), sx = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.4, st = a + i * (170 / n);
    function at(t) { var x = from[0] + sx + (to[0] + ox - from[0] - sx) * t, z = from[2] + (to[2] + oz - from[2]) * t; return [x, groundY(x, z) + 0.6 + Math.sin(Math.PI * t) * h, z]; }
    tracks.push({ a: st, b: st + fly,
      on: function () { sp = fxSprite('arrow', color === undefined ? FX_INK : color, 9); sp.scale.set(1.25, 0.24, 1); },
      step: function (u) { var p = at(u), q = at(Math.min(1, u + 0.05)); sp.position.set(p[0], p[1], p[2]); sp.material.rotation = u < 0.96 ? fxAngle(p, q) : sp.material.rotation; sp.material.opacity = u < 0.1 ? u * 10 : 1; },
      off: function () { if (sp) fxKill(sp); } });
  })(i);
  return a + 170 + fly;
}

/* ---------- 공격·전법·계략 한 번을 그려 보인다 ----------
   res 는 규칙 쪽 결과(actEv 가 붙인 aid·tid·처음 자리·나중 자리 포함). onImpact 는 타격 순간에 한 번(숫자 띄우기·소리).
   돌려주는 값은 걸리는 시간(밀리초). */
function animAct(res, done, onImpact) {
  var fin = function () { if (done) done(); };
  if (!R3 || !R3.ready || !res || res.ac0 === undefined) { if (onImpact) onImpact(); fin(); return 0; }
  var A = R3.units[res.aid] || null, T = res.tid >= 0 ? (R3.units[res.tid] || null) : null, B = res.tbase >= 0 ? R3.bases[res.tbase] : null;
  var pa = hexPos(res.ac0, res.ar0), pt = hexPos(res.c, res.r);
  var pa1 = res.aAlive ? hexPos(res.ac1, res.ar1) : pa, pt1 = (T && res.tAlive && res.tc1 !== undefined) ? hexPos(res.tc1, res.tr1) : pt;
  var ranged = hdist(res.ac0, res.ar0, res.c, res.r) > 1, tracks = [], hit = 170, end = 0, i;
  var ang = fxAngle(pa, pt), big = res.kind === 'tactic' && !res.fail, crit = !!res.crit;
  var touched = [];
  function hold(o, p) { if (!o) return; o.anim = true; o.gone = false; fxPut(o, p); o.g.visible = true; if (o.ring) o.ring.visible = true; touched.push(o); }
  hold(A, pa); hold(T, pt);
  if (A) fxFace(A, pa, pt);
  if (T && res.kind !== 'ploy') fxFace(T, pt, pa);
  var fired = false;
  function impact() { if (fired) return; fired = true; if (onImpact) onImpact(); }
  function lunge(o, from, to, a, k, back) {                 // 다가갔다가 제자리로
    if (!o) return;
    tracks.push({ a: a, b: a + 170, on: function () { sfx('step'); }, step: function (u) { fxPut(o, fxLerp(from, to, k * easeOut(u))); } });
    if (back !== false) tracks.push({ a: a + 190, b: a + 430, step: function (u) { fxPut(o, fxLerp(from, to, k * (1 - easeIO(u)))); } });
  }
  function slashAt(a, p, col, size, rot) { fxMark(tracks, a, 380, 'arc', col, p, size * 0.7, size * 1.25, rot === undefined ? ang - 1.2 + Math.random() * 0.5 : rot, { fade: 1.6 }); fxMark(tracks, a, 300, 'spark', FX_SEAL, p, size * 0.4, size * 0.95, Math.random() * 6.28, { fade: 1.2 }); }
  function tgtHome() { return T ? pt : (B ? [B.g.position.x, B.g.position.y, B.g.position.z] : pt); }
  var TH = tgtHome(), TO = T || B;

  if (res.kind === 'ploy') {
    // 계략: 붓 고리가 퍼지고, 대상에 효과
    fxMark(tracks, 0, 520, 'enso', FX_INDIGO, pa, 0.8, 2.6, Math.random() * 6.28, { dy: 0.35, fade: 1.1, spin: 1.2 });
    tracks.push({ a: 0, b: 0, on: function () { sfx('page'); } });
    hit = 300;
    if (res.ploy === '소화') { fxMark(tracks, 120, 700, 'puff', FX_INDIGO, pa, 1.2, 4.2, 0, { dy: 0.3, alpha: 0.9 }); }
    else if (res.success) {
      if (res.ploy === '화계') {
        for (i = 0; i < 5; i++) fxMark(tracks, hit + i * 70, 620, 'flame', 0xffffff, [pt[0] + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.9, pt[1], pt[2] + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.5], 0.5, 1.5, 0, { dy: 0.5, aspect: 1.5, rise: 0.5, fade: 1 });
        fxMark(tracks, hit, 420, 'spark', FX_FIRE, pt, 0.8, 2.2, 0, {});
        fxHurt(tracks, hit + 60, T, pt, 0.14);
      } else {
        fxMark(tracks, hit - 60, 900, 'swirl', FX_INDIGO, pt, 2.6, 1.1, 0, { dy: 0.8, spin: -5, fade: 0.8 });
        fxHurt(tracks, hit + 200, T, pt, 0.08);
      }
    } else {
      fxMark(tracks, hit, 520, 'puff', FX_WASH, pt, 0.8, 1.7, 0, { dy: 0.7, rise: 0.4 });
      if (res.reflected) { fxMark(tracks, hit + 260, 800, res.ploy === '화계' ? 'flame' : 'swirl', res.ploy === '화계' ? 0xffffff : FX_INDIGO, pa, 1.0, 1.9, 0, { dy: 0.7, spin: res.ploy === '화계' ? 0 : -4 }); fxHurt(tracks, hit + 300, A, pa, 0.12); }
    }
    tracks.push({ a: hit, b: hit, on: impact });
    end = hit + 720;
  } else {
    var tac = res.kind === 'tactic' ? res.tac : '', pre = 0;
    if (res.kind === 'tactic') {                             // 전법: 먼저 기합(인주빛 고리)
      fxMark(tracks, 0, 420, 'enso', FX_SEAL, pa, 0.9, 2.3, Math.random() * 6.28, { dy: 0.35, spin: 1.6, fade: 1.2 });
      if (A) tracks.push({ a: 0, b: 240, step: function (u) { var s = 1 + 0.16 * Math.sin(Math.PI * u); A.sp.scale.set(A.sp.userData.w * s, A.sp.userData.h * s, 1); } });
      tracks.push({ a: 0, b: 0, on: function () { sfx('shout'); } });
      pre = 230;
    }
    var bowLike = ranged || tac === '화시' || tac === '관시' || tac === '난사';
    if (bowLike) {
      // 쏘는 공격: 몸을 젖혔다가 화살 무리
      if (A) tracks.push({ a: pre, b: pre + 260, step: function (u) { fxPut(A, fxLerp(pa, pt, -0.10 * Math.sin(Math.PI * u))); } });
      var n = tac === '난사' ? 14 : (tac === '관시' ? 1 : (big ? 9 : 6)), colA = tac === '화시' && !res.fail ? FX_FIRE : FX_INK;
      tracks.push({ a: pre + 50, b: pre + 50, on: function () { sfx('bow'); if (n > 1) sfx('arrows'); } });
      if (tac === '관시' && !res.fail) {                       // 꿰뚫는 한 줄기
        var far = fxLerp(pa, pt, 1.9);
        fxMark(tracks, pre + 120, 420, 'stroke', FX_SEAL, fxLerp(pa, far, 0.55), 4.2, 5.6, ang, { aspect: 0.11, fade: 1.4 });
        hit = pre + 200;
      } else hit = fxVolley(tracks, pre + 60, pa, pt, n, colA, tac === '난사' ? 1.9 : 0.55) - 40;
      fxMark(tracks, hit, 320, 'spark', tac === '화시' ? FX_FIRE : FX_INK, TH, 0.6, 1.5, Math.random() * 6.28, {});
      if (tac === '화시' && !res.fail) for (i = 0; i < 3; i++) fxMark(tracks, hit + i * 80, 560, 'flame', 0xffffff, [pt[0] + (i - 1) * 0.4, pt[1], pt[2]], 0.5, 1.3, 0, { dy: 0.5, aspect: 1.5, rise: 0.4 });
    } else if (tac === '선풍' && !res.fail) {                 // 주위를 한 바퀴 휩쓴다
      fxMark(tracks, pre, 620, 'enso', FX_INK, pa, 1.4, 4.3, 0, { dy: 0.45, spin: 5.5, fade: 1.1 });
      fxMark(tracks, pre + 60, 560, 'enso', FX_SEAL, pa, 1.0, 3.6, 2, { dy: 0.5, spin: 6.5, fade: 1.3, alpha: 0.8 });
      hit = pre + 230;
    } else if ((tac === '돌격' || tac === '돌진' || tac === '돌파') && !res.fail) {
      // 기병 돌격: 먼지를 일으키며 들이받는다. 밀려난 적과 따라 들어간 자리까지
      var dashTo = tac === '돌파' ? pa1 : fxLerp(pa, pt, 0.62);
      tracks.push({ a: pre, b: pre + 210, on: function () { sfx('charge'); fxDust(pa[0], pa[1], pa[2], 1.3); },
        step: function (u) { var p = fxLerp(pa, dashTo, easeIO(u)); if (A) fxPut(A, [p[0], p[1] + Math.abs(Math.sin(u * 9)) * 0.12, p[2]]); if (u > 0.3 && u < 0.36) fxDust(p[0], p[1], p[2], 1.2); } });
      fxMark(tracks, pre + 40, 460, 'stroke', FX_INK, fxLerp(pa, pt, 0.5), 2.6, 3.9, ang, { aspect: 0.2, fade: 1.5, dy: 0.35, alpha: 0.75 });
      hit = pre + 170;
      if (A && tac !== '돌파') tracks.push({ a: hit + 60, b: hit + 330, step: function (u) { fxPut(A, fxLerp(dashTo, pa1, easeIO(u))); } });
    } else {
      // 근접: 달려들어 벤다
      var k = (tac === '돌출' || tac === '나선첨' || tac === '이단첨') && !res.fail ? 0.5 : 0.4;
      lunge(A, pa, TH, pre, k, tac !== '이단첨' || res.fail);
      hit = pre + 150;
      if (tac === '이단첨' && !res.fail && A) {                // 두 번 찌른다
        tracks.push({ a: pre + 190, b: pre + 300, step: function (u) { fxPut(A, fxLerp(pa, TH, k * (1 - 0.6 * easeIO(u)))); } });
        tracks.push({ a: pre + 300, b: pre + 420, step: function (u) { fxPut(A, fxLerp(pa, TH, k * (0.4 + 0.75 * easeOut(u)))); } });
        tracks.push({ a: pre + 440, b: pre + 680, step: function (u) { fxPut(A, fxLerp(pa, TH, k * 1.15 * (1 - easeIO(u)))); } });
        fxMark(tracks, pre + 400, 360, 'stroke', FX_SEAL, fxLerp(pa, TH, 0.9), 2.2, 3.4, ang, { aspect: 0.14, fade: 1.5 });
      }
      if ((tac === '돌출' || tac === '나선첨' || tac === '이단첨') && !res.fail) fxMark(tracks, hit - 20, 380, 'stroke', FX_INK, fxLerp(pa, TH, 0.85), 2.2, 3.6, ang, { aspect: 0.15, fade: 1.5 });
      if (tac === '나선첨' && !res.fail) fxMark(tracks, hit, 620, 'swirl', FX_SEAL, TH, 0.6, 2.2, 0, { spin: 7, fade: 1.2 });
      if (tac === '횡치' && !res.fail) fxMark(tracks, hit - 30, 460, 'arc', FX_INK, TH, 2.6, 4.4, ang - 1.57, { fade: 1.4 });
      if (tac === '갈퀴' && !res.fail) fxMark(tracks, hit - 40, 440, 'arc', FX_SEAL, [TH[0], TH[1] - 0.15, TH[2]], 1.5, 2.9, ang + 1.57, { fade: 1.5 });   // 발을 거는 갈고리 획
    }
    // 타격
    if (!bowLike && tac !== '선풍') slashAt(hit, TH, big || crit ? FX_SEAL : FX_INK, big ? 2.5 : (crit ? 2.3 : 1.8));
    if (TO) fxHurt(tracks, hit + 20, TO, TH, big || crit ? 0.2 : (B && !T ? 0.09 : 0.13));
    if (B && !T) for (i = 0; i < 3; i++) fxMark(tracks, hit + 40 + i * 70, 620, 'puff', FX_WASH, [TH[0] + (i - 1) * 0.9, TH[1], TH[2] + 0.2], 0.7, 1.8, 0, { dy: 0.25, rise: 0.5, alpha: 0.9, order: 2.6 });
    tracks.push({ a: hit, b: hit, on: function () { impact(); if (big || crit || res.fell) fxShake(big ? 5 : 3.5, 240); } });
    end = hit + 420;
    // 부가 대상(횡치·선풍·관시·난사)
    if (res.extra) for (i = 0; i < res.extra.length; i++) (function (xh, i) {
      var p = hexPos(xh.c, xh.r), ou = UAT[K(xh.c, xh.r)], oo = ou ? R3.units[ou.id] : null;
      fxMark(tracks, hit + 60 + i * 70, 340, 'arc', FX_INK, p, 1.2, 2.0, Math.random() * 6.28, { fade: 1.6 });
      if (oo && !oo.anim) fxHurt(tracks, hit + 80 + i * 70, oo, p, 0.11);
      end = Math.max(end, hit + 460 + i * 70);
    })(res.extra[i], i);
    // 밀려남
    if (T && res.tAlive && (pt1[0] !== pt[0] || pt1[2] !== pt[2])) {
      tracks.push({ a: hit + 40, b: hit + 300, on: function () { fxDust(pt[0], pt[1], pt[2], 1.1); }, step: function (u) { fxPut(T, fxLerp(pt, pt1, easeOut(u))); } });
      end = Math.max(end, hit + 340);
    }
    if (res.slam) fxMark(tracks, hit + 120, 420, 'spark', FX_SEAL, pt1, 0.8, 1.9, 0.5, {});
    // 연전
    if (res.dmg2) { slashAt(hit + 300, pt1, FX_INK, 1.7, ang + 1.3); fxHurt(tracks, hit + 320, T, pt1, 0.12); end = Math.max(end, hit + 700); }
    // 혼란
    if (res.confused) { fxMark(tracks, hit + 260, 800, 'swirl', FX_INDIGO, pt1, 2.0, 1.0, 0, { dy: 1.0, spin: -5, fade: 0.9 }); end = Math.max(end, hit + 900); }
    // 반격
    if (res.counter && !res.killed) {
      var c0 = Math.max(end - 60, hit + 380), ah = bowLike ? pa : pa1;
      if (T && !ranged) { lunge(T, pt1, ah, c0, 0.3); slashAt(c0 + 150, ah, FX_WASH, 1.5, ang + Math.PI - 1.0); }
      else if (T) fxVolley(tracks, c0, pt1, ah, 4, FX_WASH, 0.5);
      else if (B) { fxVolley(tracks, c0, TH, ah, 6, FX_WASH, 0.6); }
      fxHurt(tracks, c0 + (T && !ranged ? 170 : 330), A, ah, 0.11);
      (function (melee) { tracks.push({ a: c0 + (melee ? 150 : 0), b: c0 + (melee ? 150 : 0), on: function () { sfx(melee ? 'counter' : 'bow'); } }); })(!!(T && !ranged));
      end = Math.max(end, c0 + 620);
    }
    // 일기토: 크게 엇갈린 두 획
    if (res.duel) {
      var mid = fxLerp(pa1, pt1, 0.5), d0 = end;
      fxMark(tracks, d0, 620, 'stroke', FX_SEAL, mid, 2.4, 4.4, 0.7, { aspect: 0.17, dy: 0.8, fade: 1.2 });
      fxMark(tracks, d0 + 110, 620, 'stroke', FX_INK, mid, 2.4, 4.4, 2.44, { aspect: 0.17, dy: 0.8, fade: 1.2 });
      tracks.push({ a: d0, b: d0, on: function () { sfx('duel'); } });
      tracks.push({ a: d0 + 110, b: d0 + 110, on: function () { fxShake(5, 260); } });
      end = d0 + 780;
    }
  }
  // 궤멸
  if (res.killed && T) { fxVanish(tracks, end - 120, T, pt1); tracks.push({ a: end - 60, b: end - 60, on: function () { sfx('rout'); fxStamp(res.tc1 !== undefined ? res.tc1 : res.c, res.tr1 !== undefined ? res.tr1 : res.r, '궤멸'); } }); end += 520; }
  if ((res.selfDead || !res.aAlive) && A) { fxVanish(tracks, end - 120, A, pa1); end += 480; }
  if (res.destroyed) { fxMark(tracks, hit + 80, 800, 'splat', FX_INK, pt, 0.8, 2.4, 1, { dy: 0.3, alpha: 0.75, order: 2.6 }); end = Math.max(end, hit + 700); }
  tracks.push({ a: end, b: end, on: impact });
  return fxTimeline(end + 30, tracks, function () {
    for (var q = 0; q < touched.length; q++) { var o = touched[q]; o.anim = false; o.c = -1; o.sig = ''; if (o.sp && !o.gone) { o.sp.material.color.setRGB(1, 1, 1); o.sp.scale.set(o.sp.userData.w, o.sp.userData.h, 1); } }
    cv.style.transform = ''; requestDraw(); fin();
  });
}

/* 성이 넘어가는 장면: 성이 떨리고 먼지가 일며 낙관이 찍힌다 */
function fxFall(b, text) {
  if (!R3 || !R3.ready) return 0;
  var o = R3.bases[b.id], p = hexPos(b.c, b.r), tracks = [], i;
  if (o) fxHurt(tracks, 0, o, [o.g.position.x, o.g.position.y, o.g.position.z], 0.2);
  for (i = 0; i < 6; i++) fxMark(tracks, i * 60, 760, 'puff', FX_WASH, [p[0] + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 2.6, p[1], p[2] + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 1.2], 0.9, 2.4, 0, { dy: 0.3, rise: 0.7, alpha: 0.95, order: 2.6 });
  fxMark(tracks, 80, 900, 'enso', FX_SEAL, p, 1.6, 5.2, Math.random() * 6.28, { dy: 0.3, spin: 1.2, fade: 1.2, order: 2.7 });
  fxShake(5, 320); fxStamp(b.c, b.r, text || '함락', 180);
  return fxTimeline(900, tracks);
}
/* 출진: 성에서 걸어 나온다 */
function fxDeploy(u, b, done) {
  var o = R3 && R3.ready ? R3.units[u.id] : null;
  if (!o) { if (done) done(); return; }
  var p0 = hexPos(b.c, b.r), p1 = hexPos(u.c, u.r);
  o.anim = true; fxFace(o, p0, p1);
  anim(430, function (t) {
    var e = easeOut(t), p = fxLerp(p0, p1, e), s = 0.55 + 0.45 * e;
    fxPut(o, [p[0], p[1] + Math.sin(Math.PI * t) * 0.18, p[2]]);
    o.sp.scale.set(o.sp.userData.w * s, o.sp.userData.h * s, 1); o.sp.material.opacity = Math.min(1, 0.2 + t * 1.6);
  }, function () { o.sp.scale.set(o.sp.userData.w, o.sp.userData.h, 1); o.anim = false; o.c = -1; o.sig = ''; fxDust(p1[0], p1[1], p1[2], 1.2); requestDraw(); if (done) done(); });
}

function ico(k) { return '<i class="ico">' + ICO[k] + '</i>'; }
function thumb(key, cls) { return '<img class="th' + (cls ? ' ' + cls : '') + '" alt="" src="' + SPRITES[key].u + '">'; }
function days(turns) { return fmt(turns * 10) + '일'; }
function unitFoodTurns(x) { return x.troops > 0 ? Math.floor(x.food / Math.ceil(x.troops / (uHas(x, '둔전') ? 40 : 20))) : 0; }

function toast(msg, ms) {
  var t = $('toast'); t.textContent = msg; t.classList.add('on');
  clearTimeout(toastT); toastT = setTimeout(function () { t.classList.remove('on'); }, ms || 2400);
}
function showSheet(s) { SHEET = s; var el = $('sheet'); el.className = 'on' + (s.cls ? ' ' + s.cls : ''); el.innerHTML = s.render(); }
function refreshSheet() {
  if (!SHEET) return;
  var el = $('sheet'), body = el.querySelector('.sh-body'), st = body ? body.scrollTop : 0;
  el.innerHTML = SHEET.render();
  body = el.querySelector('.sh-body'); if (body) body.scrollTop = st;
}
function closeSheet() { showHome(); }
/* 창은 겹쳐 열 수 있다. push 로 열면 지금 창을 아래에 깔아 두고, ×(또는 바깥 누르기)는 한 단계만 뒤로 간다.
   명령을 마쳤을 때처럼 한꺼번에 닫을 때는 closeDialog(). */
var MSTACK = [], MPAINT = 0;
function dlgScroll() { var b = $('modal').querySelector('.dlg-b'); return b ? b.scrollTop : 0; }
function paintDialog(st) {
  var m = $('modal'); m.innerHTML = '<div class="dlg">' + MODAL.render() + '</div>'; m.classList.add('on');
  if (st) { var b = m.querySelector('.dlg-b'); if (b) b.scrollTop = st; }
  MPAINT = Date.now();
  paintRet();
}
function showDialog(d, push) { if (push && MODAL) MSTACK.push({ d: MODAL, st: dlgScroll() }); else MSTACK.length = 0; MODAL = d; paintDialog(0); }
function backDialog() {
  if (MODAL && MODAL.lock) return;                                   // 끝난 판의 창처럼 닫으면 안 되는 창
  if (MSTACK.length) { var p = MSTACK.pop(); MODAL = p.d; paintDialog(p.st); } else closeDialog();
}
function refreshDialog() {
  if (!MODAL) return;
  var m = $('modal'), b = m.querySelector('.dlg-b'), st = b ? b.scrollTop : 0;
  m.innerHTML = '<div class="dlg">' + MODAL.render() + '</div>';
  b = m.querySelector('.dlg-b'); if (b) b.scrollTop = st;
}
function closeDialog() { MSTACK.length = 0; MODAL = null; var m = $('modal'); m.classList.remove('on'); m.innerHTML = ''; paintRet(); }
function dlgHead(title) { return '<div class="dlg-h"><h2>' + title + '</h2><button class="x" data-a="close" type="button" aria-label="닫기">×</button></div>'; }
function delegate(root, getH, onClose) {
  root.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var el = e.target.closest ? e.target.closest('[data-a]') : null;
    if (root.id === 'modal' && Date.now() - MPAINT < 200) return;      // 창이 막 바뀐 직후의 누름(연달아 두 번 누름)은 버린다 — 새 창의 엉뚱한 단추에 닿지 않게
    if (!el || !root.contains(el)) { if (root.id === 'modal' && e.target === root) backDialog(); return; }
    var a = el.getAttribute('data-a'), hs = getH();
    if (root.id === 'sheet' && UI.busy) return;                         // 움직이는 장면이 나오는 동안에는 아래 창의 단추를 받지 않는다
    audioInit(); bgmKick(); sfx('ui');
    if (a === 'close') { onClose(); return; }
    if (hs && hs.on && hs.on[a]) hs.on[a](el);
  });
  root.addEventListener('input', function (e) {
    var el = e.target.closest ? e.target.closest('[data-i]') : null; if (!el) return;
    var hs = getH(), a = el.getAttribute('data-i');
    if (hs && hs.on && hs.on[a]) hs.on[a](el);
  });
}

/* ---------- 공통 조각 ---------- */
function porTag(o, cls) { var u = o ? POR_URL[o.por] : ''; return '<i class="por' + (cls ? ' ' + cls : '') + '"' + (u ? ' style="background-image:url(' + u + '),var(--porbg)"' : '') + '></i>'; }
/* 장면 초상(3:4): 장수 정보·소개 창에서 쓴다. 장면이 없으면 큰 흉상 */
function sceneTag(o) { var u = o ? sceneUrl(o.por) : ''; return u ? '<i class="por sc" style="background-image:url(' + u + '),var(--porbg)"></i>' : porTag(o, 'lg'); }
function st5(o, hi) {
  var ks = [['ldr', '통'], ['war', '무'], ['int', '지'], ['pol', '정'], ['cha', '매']], s = '<span class="st5">';
  for (var i = 0; i < 5; i++) s += '<span><i>' + ks[i][1] + '</i> <b' + (hi === ks[i][0] ? ' class="hi"' : '') + '>' + o[ks[i][0]] + '</b></span>';
  return s + '</span>';
}
function skTag(o) { return o.sk ? '<em>' + o.sk + '</em>' : ''; }
function facChip(f) { return '<span class="chip" style="background:' + (f >= 0 ? G.factions[f].color : '#8d877b') + '">' + (f >= 0 ? G.factions[f].name : '빈 땅') + '</span>'; }
function offStatus(o) {
  if (o.st === 'travel') return '이동 중 (' + days(o.eta) + ')';
  if (o.unit !== null) return '출진 중';
  return o.acted ? '행동 완료' : '대기';
}
function offRow(o, opt) {
  opt = opt || {};
  return '<button class="row' + (opt.sel ? ' sel' : '') + (opt.dim ? ' dim' : '') + '" type="button" data-a="' + (opt.a || 'off') + '" data-v="' + o.id + '">' + porTag(o) +
    '<span class="mid"><span class="nm">' + (opt.badge ? '<span class="hi">' + opt.badge + '</span> ' : '') + o.n + skTag(o) + '</span><span class="ds">' + (opt.ds !== undefined ? opt.ds : st5(o, opt.hi)) + '</span></span>' +
    (opt.rt ? '<span class="rt">' + opt.rt + '</span>' : '') + '</button>';
}

/* 무장 선택 표(열 제목을 누르면 정렬). opts: {title, list, min, max, hi, key(o), rt(o), rtHead, preview(os), ok, auto(n)→ids} */
function pickOfficers(opts, cb) {
  var sel = [], max = opts.max || 1, min = opts.min || 1, sortK = null, sortDir = -1, autoN = 0;
  if (!opts.list.length) { toast('행동할 수 있는 무장이 없습니다'); return; }
  var COLS = [['ldr', '통'], ['war', '무'], ['int', '지'], ['pol', '정'], ['cha', '매']];
  function sorted() {
    var L = opts.list.slice();
    if (sortK) L.sort(function (a, b) { return (b[sortK] - a[sortK]) * -sortDir; }); else L.sort(function (a, b) { return opts.key(b) - opts.key(a); });
    return L;
  }
  showDialog({
    render: function () {
      var list = sorted(), i, j, h = dlgHead(opts.title) + '<div class="dlg-b tbl"><div class="th-row"><span class="c-n">무장</span>';
      for (j = 0; j < 5; j++) h += '<button type="button" class="c-s' + (sortK === COLS[j][0] ? ' on' : (!sortK && opts.hi === COLS[j][0] ? ' hi' : '')) + '" data-a="sort" data-v="' + COLS[j][0] + '">' + COLS[j][1] + (sortK === COLS[j][0] ? (sortDir < 0 ? '▾' : '▴') : '') + '</button>';
      h += '<span class="c-x">' + (opts.rtHead || '특기') + '</span></div>';
      for (i = 0; i < list.length; i++) {
        var o = list[i], si = sel.indexOf(o.id);
        h += '<button class="tr' + (si >= 0 ? ' sel' : '') + '" type="button" data-a="pick" data-v="' + o.id + '">' + porTag(o, 'sm') + '<span class="c-n">' + (si >= 0 && max > 1 ? '<i class="ord">' + (si + 1) + '</i>' : '') + o.n + '</span>';
        for (j = 0; j < 5; j++) h += '<span class="c-s' + (opts.hi === COLS[j][0] ? ' hi' : '') + '">' + o[COLS[j][0]] + '</span>';
        h += '<span class="c-x">' + (opts.rt ? opts.rt(o) : (o.sk || '—')) + '</span></button>';
      }
      var os = sel.map(off), strip = '';
      if (os.length) { strip = '<div class="selstrip">'; for (i = 0; i < os.length; i++) strip += '<span>' + porTag(os[i], 'md') + '<b>' + (max > 1 ? (i + 1) + '. ' : '') + os[i].n + '</b></span>'; strip += '</div>'; }
      h += '</div>' + strip + '<div class="dlg-f"><div class="pv">' + (os.length ? (opts.preview ? opts.preview(os) : os.length + '명 선택') : (max > 1 ? '무장을 고르세요 (최대 ' + max + '명)' : '무장을 고르세요')) + '</div>' +
        (opts.auto ? '<button class="btn auto" type="button" data-a="auto">자동</button>' : '') +
        '<button class="btn pri" type="button" data-a="ok"' + (os.length < min ? ' disabled' : '') + '>' + (opts.ok || '실행') + '</button></div>';
      return h;
    },
    on: {
      sort: function (el) { var k = el.getAttribute('data-v'); if (sortK === k) sortDir = -sortDir; else { sortK = k; sortDir = -1; } refreshDialog(); },
      pick: function (el) {
        var id = +el.getAttribute('data-v'), i = sel.indexOf(id);
        if (i >= 0) sel.splice(i, 1); else if (max === 1) sel = [id]; else if (sel.length < max) sel.push(id); else { toast('최대 ' + max + '명입니다'); return; }
        refreshDialog();
      },
      auto: function () {                       // 누를 때마다: 효과가 가장 큰 조합 ↔ 한 명만
        var n = (max > 1 && autoN % 2 === 1) ? 1 : max; autoN++;
        sel = opts.auto(n);
        toast(max === 1 ? '가장 알맞은 무장을 골랐습니다' : (n > 1 ? '효과가 가장 큰 조합 ' + sel.length + '명 — 한 번 더 누르면 한 명만' : '한 명만 쓸 때 가장 나은 무장'), 2200);
        refreshDialog();
      },
      ok: function () {
        if (sel.length < min) return;
        var os = sel.map(off);
        if (opts.keep) { cb(os); return; }                       // 다음 창이 이 표 위에 겹쳐 열린다
        var m = MODAL, stk = MSTACK.slice(), sc = dlgScroll();
        closeDialog();
        if (cb(os) === false && !MODAL) { MSTACK = stk; MODAL = m; paintDialog(sc); }   // 명령이 안 됐으면(금·행동력 부족 등) 표를 그대로 둔다
      }
    }
  }, !!opts.push);
}
function ids(os) { return os.map(function (o) { return o.id; }); }

/* 무장 상세 */
function showOfficer(o) {
  var apt = ['창', '극', '노', '기', '병기'], a = '';
  for (var i = 0; i < 5; i++) a += '<div class="' + o.apt.charAt(i) + '">' + apt[i] + '<b>' + o.apt.charAt(i) + '</b></div>';
  var bars = '', ks = [['ldr', '통솔'], ['war', '무력'], ['int', '지력'], ['pol', '정치'], ['cha', '매력']];
  for (i = 0; i < 5; i++) bars += '<div class="bar"><span>' + ks[i][1] + '</span><i><u style="width:' + o[ks[i][0]] + '%"></u></i><b>' + o[ks[i][0]] + '</b></div>';
  var where = o.st === 'free' ? '재야 (' + G.bases[o.loc].n + ')' : o.st === 'captive' ? '포로 (' + G.bases[o.loc].n + ')' : (o.unit !== null ? '출진 중' : G.bases[o.loc].n);
  showDialog({
    render: function () {
      return dlgHead(o.n + ' ' + (o.f >= 0 ? facChip(o.f) : '')) + '<div class="dlg-b"><div class="card">' + sceneTag(o) + '<div class="bars">' + bars + '</div></div>' +
        '<div class="apt">' + a + '</div>' +
        (o.sk ? '<p><b class="hi">특기 · ' + o.sk + '</b><br>' + SKILLS[o.sk] + '</p>' : '<p class="note">특기 없음</p>') +
        '<div class="stats"><div><span>소재</span><b>' + where + '</b></div>' + (o.f >= 0 ? '<div><span>충성</span><b>' + o.loy + '</b></div><div><span>지휘</span><b>' + fmt(cmdCap(o)) + '</b></div><div><span>공적</span><b>' + fmt(o.merit) + '</b></div>' : '') + '</div></div>';
    }, on: {}
  }, true);                                              // 목록 같은 창 위에서 열었으면 ×로 그 창에 돌아간다
}

/* ---------- HUD ---------- */
function pendingUnits() { return facUnits(G.player).filter(function (u) { return !u.done && !(u.dest >= 0) && !u.moved; }); }
function refreshHud() {
  if (!G) return;
  var F = G.factions[G.player], L = off(F.lord);
  $('hudY').textContent = G.year + '년'; $('hudM').textContent = G.month + '월 ' + ['상순', '중순', '하순'][G.xun];
  var hf = $('hudFac'); hf.children[0].style.background = F.color; hf.children[1].style.backgroundImage = POR_URL[L.por] ? 'url(' + POR_URL[L.por] + '),var(--porbg)' : ''; hf.children[2].textContent = F.name + '군';
  $('hudAPv').textContent = F.ap; $('hudAPb').style.width = Math.round(F.ap / 255 * 100) + '%';
  var g = 0, fd = 0, t = 0;
  facBases(G.player).forEach(function (b) { g += b.gold; fd += b.food; t += b.troops; });
  facUnits(G.player).forEach(function (u) { t += u.troops; });
  $('hudRes').innerHTML = '<span>' + ico('gold') + '<b>' + fmt(g) + '</b></span><span>' + ico('food') + '<b>' + fmt(fd) + '</b></span><span>' + ico('troops') + '<b>' + fmt(t) + '</b></span><span>' + ico('dur') + '<b>' + facCities(G.player).length + '</b>성</span>';
  var n = pendingUnits().length;
  $('btnNext').querySelector('.n').textContent = n ? n : '';
  $('btnNext').style.display = facUnits(G.player).length ? '' : 'none';
}
function afterAction() { requestDraw(); refreshHud(); if (SHEET && SHEET.home) refreshSheet(); if (G.over) showGameOver(); }

/* ---------- STATUS 창: 늘 떠 있는 세력 현황 + 지금 할 일 ---------- */
var HOME = { open: true };
function facTotals(f) {
  var t = { gold: 0, food: 0, troops: 0, gi: 0, fi: 0, cities: 0, gates: 0, off: 0, units: 0, idle: 0 };
  facBases(f).forEach(function (b) {
    t.gold += b.gold; t.food += b.food; t.troops += b.troops; t.gi += goldIncome(b); t.fi += foodIncome(b);
    if (b.kind === 'city') t.cities++; else t.gates++;
    t.idle += idleOfficers(b).length;
  });
  facUnits(f).forEach(function (u) { t.troops += u.troops; t.units++; });
  t.off = facOfficers(f).length;
  return t;
}
function minFacCost() { var m = 1e9; for (var i = 0; i < FAC_ORDER.length; i++) m = Math.min(m, FAC[FAC_ORDER[i]].cost); return m; }
/* 쉬는 무장이 있는 거점에서 지금 해 볼 만한 일 (많아야 두 가지). tab = 첫 번째 일을 할 수 있는 탭 */
function baseAdvice(b) {
  var n = idleOfficers(b).length, tips = [];
  if (!n) return null;
  if (b.kind === 'city') {
    var empty = b.plots.filter(function (p) { return !p.fac; }).length, men = freeOfficers(b).length + b.captives.length;
    if (b.order < 60) tips.push(['치안 ' + b.order + ' → 순찰', 'home']);
    if (men) tips.push(['등용할 인재 ' + men + '명', 'men']);
    if (empty && b.gold >= minFacCost()) tips.push(['빈 부지 ' + empty + '곳 → 시설 건설', 'fac']);
    if (hasFac(b, 'barracks') && b.gold >= 300 && b.order >= 60) tips.push(['징병', 'home']);
    if (b.morale < 60 && b.troops >= 1000) tips.push(['기력 ' + b.morale + ' → 훈련', 'home']);
    if (!tips.length) tips.push(['탐색으로 인재·재물 찾기', 'men']);
  } else {
    if (b.morale < 60 && b.troops >= 1000) tips.push(['기력 ' + b.morale + ' → 훈련', 'mil']);
    if (b.troops >= 1000) tips.push(['출진 가능', 'mil']);
    if (!tips.length) return null;
  }
  return { n: n, tips: tips.slice(0, 2).map(function (t) { return t[0]; }), tab: tips[0][1] };
}
/* 지금 할 일: 급한 것부터. 항목 {cls, ic, num, t, s, go:[종류, id], lb} */
function todoList() {
  var f = G.player, F = G.factions[f], out = [], bs = facBases(f), us = facUnits(f), seen = {};
  bs.forEach(function (b) {
    var es = enemyUnitsNear(f, b.c, b.r, 4); if (!es.length) return;
    var t = 0; es.forEach(function (e) { t += e.troops; });
    es.sort(function (x, y) { return hdist(b.c, b.r, x.c, x.r) - hdist(b.c, b.r, y.c, y.r); });
    out.push({ cls: 'warn', ic: '!', t: b.n + ' 가까이에 다른 세력 부대 ' + es.length + '개', s: facName(es[0].f) + '군 ' + uName(es[0]) + (es.length > 1 ? ' 등' : '') + ' · 모두 ' + fmt(t) + '명 · ' + hdist(b.c, b.r, es[0].c, es[0].r) + '칸 거리', go: ['unit', es[0].id], lb: '보기' });
  });
  us.forEach(function (u) {
    if (u.done || !attackTargets(u).length) return;
    seen[u.id] = 1;
    out.push({ cls: 'warn', ic: '⚔', t: uName(u) + ' — 적과 맞닿았습니다', s: '부대를 고른 뒤 붉은 칸의 적을 누르면 공격·전법·계략', go: ['unit', u.id], lb: '명령' });
  });
  pendingUnits().forEach(function (u) {
    if (seen[u.id]) return; seen[u.id] = 1;
    out.push({ ic: '▶', t: uName(u) + ' — 명령을 기다립니다', s: UNIT_TYPES[u.type].name + ' ' + fmt(u.troops) + '명 · 갈 곳을 눌러 행군시키거나 대기', go: ['unit', u.id], lb: '명령' });
  });
  us.forEach(function (u) {
    var ft = unitFoodTurns(u);
    if (ft <= 3) out.push({ cls: 'warn', ic: '!', t: uName(u) + ' — 군량이 ' + days(ft) + '분뿐', s: '아군 거점에 들어가면 보급됩니다', go: ['unit', u.id], lb: '보기' });
  });
  if (F.ap >= 10) {
    var nb = 0, more = 0, lordLoc = off(F.lord).unit === null ? off(F.lord).loc : -1;
    bs.slice().sort(function (x, y) { return (y.id === lordLoc) - (x.id === lordLoc) || (y.kind === 'city') - (x.kind === 'city') || idleOfficers(y).length - idleOfficers(x).length; }).forEach(function (b) {
      var a = baseAdvice(b); if (!a) return;
      if (nb >= 5) { more++; return; }
      nb++;
      out.push({ ic: String(a.n), num: 1, t: b.n + ' — 쉬는 무장 ' + a.n + '명', s: a.tips.join(' · '), go: ['base', b.id, a.tab], lb: '열기' });
    });
    if (more) out.push({ ic: '+', t: '다른 거점 ' + more + '곳에도 쉬는 무장이 있습니다', s: '목록에서 거점을 고르세요', go: ['list', 'city'], lb: '목록' });
  } else out.push({ ic: '…', t: '행동력이 거의 없습니다 (' + F.ap + ')', s: '내정·출진 명령은 다음 턴에 다시 내릴 수 있습니다' });
  var mar = us.filter(function (u) { return u.dest >= 0; });
  if (mar.length) out.push({ cls: 'cool', ic: '▸', t: '행군 중인 부대 ' + mar.length + '개', s: mar.slice(0, 2).map(function (u) { return uName(u) + ' → ' + destName(u.dest); }).join(' · ') + (mar.length > 2 ? ' …' : '') + ' — 턴을 마치면 나아갑니다', go: ['unit', mar[0].id], lb: '보기' });
  var need = 0; out.forEach(function (t) { if (t.cls !== 'cool' && t.go) need++; });
  out.need = need;
  out.push({ cls: 'end', ic: '✓', t: need ? '할 일을 마쳤으면 턴 종료' : '할 일을 모두 마쳤습니다', s: '턴 종료를 누르면 열흘이 흐르고, 그동안 일어난 일을 차례로 보여 줍니다', go: ['end'], lb: '턴 종료' });
  return out;
}
function gotoBase(b, tab) { selectBase(b, true, tab); jumpTo(b.c, b.r, Math.max(cam.z, 0.8)); }
function gotoUnit(u) { selectUnit(u, true); jumpTo(u.c, u.r, Math.max(cam.z, 0.8)); }
function goTodo(g) {
  if (g[0] === 'unit') { var u = UMAP[g[1]]; if (u) gotoUnit(u); }
  else if (g[0] === 'base') gotoBase(G.bases[g[1]], g[2]);
  else if (g[0] === 'list') showLists(g[1]);
  else if (g[0] === 'end') doEndTurn();
}
function homeSheet() {
  var st = { todo: [] };
  return {
    home: true, cls: 'home' + (HOME.open ? '' : ' fold'),
    on: {
      fold: function () { HOME.open = !HOME.open; saveOpts(); showHome(); requestDraw(); },
      go: function (el) { var t = st.todo[+el.getAttribute('data-v')]; if (t && t.go) goTodo(t.go); },
      lord: function () { showOfficer(off(G.factions[G.player].lord)); },
      lists: function (el) { showLists(el.getAttribute('data-v')); }
    },
    render: function () {
      if (!G) return '';
      var f = G.player, F = G.factions[f], L = off(F.lord), T = facTotals(f), i;
      st.todo = todoList();
      var need = st.todo.need;
      var h = '<div class="sh-head hm-head"><button class="porb" type="button" data-a="lord" aria-label="군주 정보">' + porTag(L, HOME.open ? 'xl' : 'md') + '</button><div class="hm-id">' +
