        elif hREL[r, c] >= TH: t = 'h'
        else: t = 'p'
        T[(c, r)] = t

# ---------- 강 ----------
def densify(pts, step=0.3):
    out = [pts[0]]
    for i in range(1, len(pts)):
        (x0, z0), (x1, z1) = pts[i - 1], pts[i]; L = math.hypot(x1 - x0, z1 - z0); n = max(1, int(L / step))
        for k in range(1, n + 1): out.append((x0 + (x1 - x0) * k / n, z0 + (z1 - z0) * k / n))
    return out
def simplify(pts, eps):
    if len(pts) < 3: return pts
    (x0, z0), (x1, z1) = pts[0], pts[-1]; L = math.hypot(x1 - x0, z1 - z0) or 1e-9
    dm, im = 0, 0
    for i in range(1, len(pts) - 1):
        d = abs((pts[i][0] - x0) * (z1 - z0) - (pts[i][1] - z0) * (x1 - x0)) / L
        if d > dm: dm, im = d, i
    if dm <= eps: return [pts[0], pts[-1]]
    return simplify(pts[:im + 1], eps)[:-1] + simplify(pts[im:], eps)
RIVERS = []     # dict(name, major, pts[world])
def add_river(name, major, lonlat):
    a = np.asarray(lonlat, dtype=np.float64)
    wx, wz = ll2w(a[:, 1], a[:, 0])
    pts = list(zip(wx.tolist(), wz.tolist()))
    # 지도 안쪽 구간만
    seg = []
    for p in pts:
        if -4 <= p[0] <= WX + 4 and -4 <= p[1] <= WZ + 4: seg.append(p)
        else:
            if len(seg) > 1: RIVERS.append(dict(name=name, major=major, pts=seg))
            seg = []
    if len(seg) > 1: RIVERS.append(dict(name=name, major=major, pts=seg))
for f in json.load(open(os.path.join(GEO, 'rivers10.geojson')))['features']:
    g = f['geometry']; p = f['properties']; nm = p.get('name')
    if not g or (nm not in MAJOR_RIVERS and nm not in MINOR_RIVERS): continue
    lines = [g['coordinates']] if g['type'] == 'LineString' else g['coordinates']
    for line in lines:
        a = [(x, y) for x, y in line if LL_BOX[0] <= x <= LL_BOX[2] and LL_BOX[1] <= y <= LL_BOX[3]]
        if len(a) < 2: continue
        if nm == 'Huang':
            a = [(x, y) for x, y in a if x <= 113.55]            # 현대 하류는 버린다
            if len(a) < 2: continue
            if abs(a[-1][0] - 113.55) < 0.6: a = a + HAN_HUANGHE[1:]
            elif abs(a[0][0] - 113.55) < 0.6: a = HAN_HUANGHE[::-1][:-1] + a
        add_river(nm, nm in MAJOR_RIVERS, a)
RIVERHEX = set()
for rv in RIVERS:
    if not rv['major']: continue
    for (x, z) in densify(rv['pts'], 0.25):
        h = w2hex(x, z)
        if inmap(*h) and T[h] != 's': T[h] = 'w'; RIVERHEX.add(h)
print('rivers', Counter(r['name'] for r in RIVERS))

# ---------- 거점: 실제 위치 → 최소 간격 보정 → 헥스 ----------
kinds = ['city'] * len(CITY_LL) + ['gate'] * len(GATE_LL)
sizes = [CITY_LL[n][2] for n in CITY_LL] + ['G'] * len(GATE_LL)
P = np.stack(ll2w(ll[:, 0], ll[:, 1]), axis=1)            # 참 위치(월드)
Q = P.copy()
def mind(i, j): return (MIN_CC if kinds[i] == 'city' and kinds[j] == 'city' else MIN_CG) * SQ3
for it in range(600):
    Q += 0.02 * (P - Q) if it < 540 else 0
    for i in range(len(Q)):
        for j in range(i + 1, len(Q)):
            d = Q[i] - Q[j]; L = np.hypot(*d); m = mind(i, j) + 0.3
            if L < m:
                push = (m - L) / 2 * (d / (L or 1)); Q[i] += push; Q[j] -= push
disp = np.hypot(*(Q - P).T) / SQ3
print('base displacement (hex): mean %.2f  max %.2f (%s)  moved>0.5: %d' % (disp.mean(), disp.max(), names[int(disp.argmax())], int((disp > 0.5).sum())))
BASES = []; used = {}
for i, n in enumerate(names):
    h = w2hex(Q[i, 0], Q[i, 1])
    if T[h] in 'sw' or h in used:
        cand = sorted((((center(*v)[0] - Q[i, 0]) ** 2 + (center(*v)[1] - Q[i, 1]) ** 2, v) for d_ in (1, 2, 3) for v in ring(h[0], h[1], d_) if T[v] not in 'sw' and v not in used))
        h = cand[0][1]
    used[h] = n
    BASES.append(dict(name=n, kind=kinds[i], c=h[0], r=h[1], size=sizes[i], true=[round(float(P[i, 0]), 2), round(float(P[i, 1]), 2)], lat=float(ll[i, 0]), lon=float(ll[i, 1])))
BIDX = {b['name']: i for i, b in enumerate(BASES)}
BASEHEX = {(b['c'], b['r']): i for i, b in enumerate(BASES)}
snap = [math.hypot(center(b['c'], b['r'])[0] - P[i, 0], center(b['c'], b['r'])[1] - P[i, 1]) / SQ3 for i, b in enumerate(BASES)]
print('final offset from true position (hex): mean %.2f  median %.2f  max %.2f (%s)  >1.5: %s' % (np.mean(snap), np.median(snap), max(snap), names[int(np.argmax(snap))], [names[i] + ' %.1f' % s for i, s in enumerate(snap) if s > 1.5]))

for b in BASES:
    T[(b['c'], b['r'])] = 'p'
    if b['kind'] == 'city':
        for h in ring(b['c'], b['r'], 1):
            if T[h] in 'mh': T[h] = 'p'
        for h in ring(b['c'], b['r'], 2):
            if T[h] == 'm': T[h] = 'h'
        for h in ring(b['c'], b['r'], 3):
            if T[h] == 'm': T[h] = 'h'

# ---------- 도로(A*) ----------
def vnoise(x, y, s, seed):
    def rnd(i, j):
        n = (i * 374761393 + j * 668265263 + seed * 2147483647) & 0xffffffff
        n = (n ^ (n >> 13)) * 1274126177 & 0xffffffff
        return ((n ^ (n >> 16)) & 0xffff) / 65535.0
    x /= s; y /= s
    i, j = int(math.floor(x)), int(math.floor(y)); fx, fy = x - i, y - j
    fx = fx * fx * (3 - 2 * fx); fy = fy * fy * (3 - 2 * fy)
    a = rnd(i, j) * (1 - fx) + rnd(i + 1, j) * fx; b = rnd(i, j + 1) * (1 - fx) + rnd(i + 1, j + 1) * fx
    return a * (1 - fy) + b * fy
ROAD = set()
COST = {'p': 1.0, 'h': 1.7, 'm': 5.0, 'w': 5.0, 's': 1e9}
def line_dev(h, a, b):
    (x0, y0), (x1, y1), (x2, y2) = center(*h), center(*a), center(*b)
    L = math.hypot(x2 - x1, y2 - y1) or 1.0
    t = max(0.0, min(1.0, ((x0 - x1) * (x2 - x1) + (y0 - y1) * (y2 - y1)) / (L * L)))
    return math.hypot(x0 - (x1 + t * (x2 - x1)), y0 - (y1 + t * (y2 - y1))) / SQ3
def astar(a, b):
    pq = [(0, a)]; g = {a: 0}; prev = {}
    while pq:
        f, u = heapq.heappop(pq)
        if u == b: break
        if f - dist(u, b) * 0.6 > g[u] + 1e-9: continue
        for v in nbrs(*u):
            if v in BASEHEX and v != b: continue
            t = T[v]
            if t == 's': continue
            cst = COST[t]
            if t != 'w':
                vx, vz = center(*v); cst *= 0.7 + 0.9 * vnoise(vx, vz, 11.0, 23) + 0.35 * vnoise(vx, vz, 4.0, 5)
            if v in ROAD: cst = min(cst, 0.55) if t != 'w' else 2.5
            cst += 0.06 * max(0.0, line_dev(v, a, b) - 3.0)
            ng = g[u] + cst
            if v not in g or ng < g[v] - 1e-9:
                g[v] = ng; prev[v] = u
                heapq.heappush(pq, (ng + dist(v, b) * 0.6, v))
    if b not in g: return None
    path = [b]
    while path[-1] != a: path.append(prev[path[-1]])
    return path[::-1]
EDGE_PATHS = []
for e in EDGES:
    an, bn = e.split('-'); A = BASES[BIDX[an]]; B = BASES[BIDX[bn]]
    p = astar((A['c'], A['r']), (B['c'], B['r']))
    assert p, e
    EDGE_PATHS.append((BIDX[an], BIDX[bn], p))
    for h in p[1:-1]:
        ROAD.add(h)
        if T[h] == 'm': T[h] = 'h'

GATEHEX = [(b['c'], b['r']) for b in BASES if b['kind'] == 'gate']
for h in list(ROAD):                                           # 산길 넓히기
    if any(dist(h, g) <= 2 for g in GATEHEX): continue
    for v in nbrs(*h):
        if T[v] == 'm': T[v] = 'h'

# ---------- 관문 요충 ----------
def passable(h): return T[h] not in 'ms'
def bfs_path(a, b, banned, limit):
    dq = deque([a]); seen = {a: None}; depth = {a: 0}
    while dq:
        u = dq.popleft()
        if u == b: break
        if depth[u] >= limit: continue
        for v in nbrs(*u):
            if v in seen or v in banned or not passable(v): continue
            if v in BASEHEX and v != b: continue
            seen[v] = u; depth[v] = depth[u] + 1; dq.append(v)
    if b not in seen: return None
    path = [b]
    while seen[path[-1]] is not None: path.append(seen[path[-1]])
    return path[::-1]
PROTECT = set()
for b in BASES:
    PROTECT.add((b['c'], b['r']))
    if b['kind'] == 'city':
        for n in (1, 2, 3):
            for h in ring(b['c'], b['r'], n): PROTECT.add(h)
blocked_added = []
for g in BASES:
    if g['kind'] != 'gate': continue
    gh = (g['c'], g['r']); gi = BIDX[g['name']]
    for n in (1, 2):
        for h in ring(gh[0], gh[1], n):
            if h not in ROAD and h not in BASEHEX and T[h] not in 'sw' and h not in PROTECT:
                if T[h] != 'm': blocked_added.append(h)
                T[h] = 'm'
    ends = [x for (a, b2, p) in EDGE_PATHS if gi in (a, b2) for x in (a, b2) if x != gi]
    if len(ends) < 2: continue
    A = BASES[ends[0]]; B = BASES[ends[1]]; ah = (A['c'], A['r']); bh = (B['c'], B['r'])
    direct = dist(ah, gh) + dist(gh, bh)
    for _ in range(60):
        p = bfs_path(ah, bh, {gh}, int(direct * 1.8) + 6)
        if p is None: break
        cand = [h for h in p[1:-1] if h not in ROAD and h not in PROTECT and h not in BASEHEX and T[h] != 'w' and dist(h, gh) <= 7]
        if not cand: break
        cand.sort(key=lambda h: dist(h, gh))
        T[cand[0]] = 'm'; blocked_added.append(cand[0])

# ---------- 부지 ----------
NPLOT = {'L': 10, 'M': 8, 'S': 6}
PLOTS = {}; claimed = set()
for b in BASES:
    if b['kind'] != 'city': continue
    ch = (b['c'], b['r'])
    def ok(h):
        if h in ROAD or h in BASEHEX or h in claimed or T[h] in 'sw': return False
        return all(dist(h, (o['c'], o['r'])) > 1 for o in BASES if (o['c'], o['r']) != ch)
    r1 = [h for h in ring(*ch, 1) if ok(h)]; r2 = [h for h in ring(*ch, 2) if ok(h)]; r3 = [h for h in ring(*ch, 3) if ok(h)]
    free1 = [h for h in ring(*ch, 1) if T[h] not in 'sw']
    road1 = [h for h in free1 if h in ROAD]
    max_r1 = max(0, len(free1) - max(max(2, len(free1) - 3), len(road1)))
    chosen = r1[:max_r1]
    def adjcount(h): return sum(1 for v in nbrs(*h) if v in chosen)
    pool = r2 + r3
    while len(chosen) < NPLOT[b['size']] and pool:
        pool.sort(key=lambda h: (-(adjcount(h) > 0), dist(h, ch), -adjcount(h), hREL[h[1], h[0]]))
        chosen.append(pool.pop(0))
    assert len(chosen) == NPLOT[b['size']], (b['name'], len(chosen))
    for h in chosen: claimed.add(h); T[h] = 'p'
    PLOTS[b['name']] = chosen

# ---------- 변경(비활성) ----------
near = {}; dq = deque()
for h in list(ROAD) + list(BASEHEX) + list(claimed): near[h] = 0; dq.append(h)
while dq:
    u = dq.popleft()
    if near[u] >= VOID_DIST: continue
    for v in nbrs(*u):
        if v not in near: near[v] = near[u] + 1; dq.append(v)
far = set(h for h in T if h not in near)
# 바깥 변경만 남긴다: 지도 가장자리와 땅으로 이어진 먼 땅. 안쪽에 갇힌 먼 땅은 그냥 땅으로 둔다.
VOID = set(h for h in far if (h[0] in (0, W - 1) or h[1] in (0, H - 1)) and T[h] != 's'); dq = deque(VOID)
while dq:
    u = dq.popleft()
    for v in nbrs(*u):
        if v in far and v not in VOID and T[v] != 's': VOID.add(v); dq.append(v)

# ---------- 숲·황무지 ----------
basehexes = [(b['c'], b['r']) for b in BASES]
for (c, r) in list(T):
    h = (c, r)
    if T[h] not in 'ph' or h in ROAD or h in claimed or h in BASEHEX: continue
    x, z = center(c, r); la, lo = hlat[r, c], hlon[r, c]
    n = 0.6 * vnoise(x, z, 9.0, 7) + 0.4 * vnoise(x, z, 3.4, 11)
    arid = max(min((la - 34.5) / 4.0, 1.0), 0.0) * max(min((109.5 - lo) / 5.0, 1.0), 0.0)
    arid = max(arid, max(min((la - 39.8) / 1.5, 1.0), 0.0) * max(min((117.5 - lo) / 3.0, 1.0), 0.0))
    if T[h] == 'p' and arid > 0.05:
        if n > 0.66 - 0.45 * arid: T[h] = 'd'
        continue
    if T[h] == 'p':
        south = max(min((34.0 - la) / 6.0, 1.0), 0.0); ne = 1.0 if (la > 40.3 and lo > 119.5) else 0.0
        thr = 0.70 - 0.10 * south - 0.10 * ne
        if n > thr and all(dist(h, bh) > 1 for bh in basehexes if abs(bh[0] - c) < 4 and abs(bh[1] - r) < 4): T[h] = 'f'
    elif T[h] == 'h':
        south = max(min((34.5 - la) / 5.0, 1.0), 0.0)
        if n > 0.80 - 0.26 * south and arid < 0.05: T[h] = 'f'      # 남쪽 구릉은 숲이 많다

# ---------- 연결성 ----------
start = (BASES[0]['c'], BASES[0]['r']); seen = {start}; dq = deque([start])
while dq:
    u = dq.popleft()
    for v in nbrs(*u):
        if v in seen or T[v] in 'ms' or v in VOID: continue
        seen.add(v); dq.append(v)
unreach = [b['name'] for b in BASES if (b['c'], b['r']) not in seen]
assert not unreach, unreach

# ---------- 출력 ----------
def tt(c, r): return 'x' if (c, r) in VOID and T[(c, r)] != 's' else T[(c, r)]
tstr = ''.join(tt(c, r) for r in range(H) for c in range(W))
rstr = ''.join('1' if (c, r) in ROAD else '0' for r in range(H) for c in range(W))
slim = dict(W=W, H=H, km=round(HW, 2), t=tstr, road=rstr,
            bases=[dict(n=b['name'], k=b['kind'], c=b['c'], r=b['r'], sz=b['size']) for b in BASES],
            edges=[[a, b, len(p) - 1] for (a, b, p) in EDGE_PATHS],
            plots={n: [list(h) for h in v] for n, v in PLOTS.items()})
with open(OUT_JS, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
    f.write('var MAPDATA=' + json.dumps(slim, ensure_ascii=False, separators=(',', ':')) + ';\n')
full = dict(slim, traw=''.join(T[(c, r)] for r in range(H) for c in range(W)), paths=[[list(h) for h in p] for (a, b, p) in EDGE_PATHS],
            rivers=[dict(name=rv['name'], major=rv['major'], pts=[[round(x, 2), round(z, 2)] for x, z in simplify(rv['pts'], 0.12)]) for rv in RIVERS],
            grid=dict(X0=X0, Y0=Y0, U=U, HW=HW, WX=WX, WZ=WZ), basesFull=BASES, blocked=[list(h) for h in blocked_added])
with open(OUT_JSON, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f: json.dump(full, f, ensure_ascii=False)

cnt = Counter(tstr); n_all = W * H
print('terrain', {k: '%d (%.1f%%)' % (v, 100.0 * v / n_all) for k, v in cnt.most_common()})
play = [ch for ch in tstr if ch not in 'xs']; pc = Counter(play)
print('playable %d: %s' % (len(play), {k: '%.1f%%' % (100.0 * v / len(play)) for k, v in pc.most_common()}))
lens = [len(p) - 1 for _, _, p in EDGE_PATHS]
print('roads %d hexes, edges %d: len median %d  min %d  max %d' % (len(ROAD), len(lens), int(np.median(lens)), min(lens), max(lens)))
print('edge lens', ' '.join('%s-%s:%d' % (BASES[a]['name'], BASES[b]['name'], len(p) - 1) for a, b, p in EDGE_PATHS))
print('blocked', len(blocked_added))

# ---------- 디버그 그림 ----------
SC = 4.0
im = Image.new('RGB', (int(WX * SC), int(WZ * SC)), (20, 30, 60)); d = ImageDraw.Draw(im)
COL = {'p': (196, 200, 140), 'h': (170, 150, 95), 'm': (105, 80, 60), 'w': (60, 120, 220), 's': (30, 60, 130), 'f': (70, 130, 70), 'd': (225, 205, 150), 'x': (60, 58, 55)}
def poly(c, r, k=1.0):
    x, z = center(c, r)
    return [((x + k * math.cos(math.radians(60 * i - 30))) * SC, (z + k * math.sin(math.radians(60 * i - 30))) * SC) for i in range(6)]
for r in range(H):
    for c in range(W):
        t = tt(c, r); col = COL[t]
        if t == 'x':
            raw = COL[T[(c, r)]]; col = tuple(int(v * 0.45) for v in raw)
        d.polygon(poly(c, r), fill=col)
        if (c, r) in ROAD: d.polygon(poly(c, r, 0.5), fill=(150, 40, 20))
for n, v in PLOTS.items():
    for h in v: d.polygon(poly(*h, k=0.7), fill=(255, 255, 255))
for rv in RIVERS:
    if not rv['major']: d.line([(x * SC, z * SC) for x, z in rv['pts']], fill=(90, 150, 240), width=1)
try: font = ImageFont.truetype('/usr/share/fonts/truetype/dejavu/DejaVuSans-Bold.ttf', 13)
except Exception: font = None
for i, b in enumerate(BASES):
    x, z = center(b['c'], b['r']); col = (255, 230, 0) if b['kind'] == 'city' else (255, 60, 60)
    d.ellipse([x * SC - 5, z * SC - 5, x * SC + 5, z * SC + 5], fill=col, outline=(0, 0, 0))
    d.text((x * SC + 6, z * SC - 7), str(i), fill=(255, 255, 255), font=font)
im.save(OUT_DBG)

=====8<===== tools/bake3d.py
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""천하도 v0.2 — 3D 지형 굽기: 높이맵 + 바탕 텍스처 + 강·도로 선.
사용: bake3d.py <geo_dir> <mapdata.json> <out terrain.jpg> <out map3d.js> [TEXW]
"""
import sys, os, json, math, base64, io
import numpy as np
from PIL import Image, ImageDraw, ImageFilter
from scipy import ndimage as ndi
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
from geo import lcc_inv

GEO, MAPJSON, OUT_TEX, OUT_JS = sys.argv[1:5]
TEXW = int(sys.argv[5]) if len(sys.argv) > 5 else 3072
Image.MAX_IMAGE_PIXELS = None
SQ3 = math.sqrt(3)
M = json.load(open(MAPJSON, encoding='utf-8'))
W, H = M['W'], M['H']; G = M['grid']; WX, WZ = G['WX'], G['WZ']
PPU = TEXW / WX; TEXH = int(round(WZ * PPU))
print('texture %dx%d  %.2f px/unit  %.1f px/hex' % (TEXW, TEXH, PPU, PPU * SQ3))
f32 = np.float32

def center(c, r): return (SQ3 * (c + 0.5 * (r & 1)) + SQ3 / 2, 1.5 * r + 1.0)
def hexpoly(c, r, k=1.0):
    x, z = center(c, r)
    return [((x + k * math.cos(math.radians(60 * i - 30))) * PPU, (z + k * math.sin(math.radians(60 * i - 30))) * PPU) for i in range(6)]

# ---------- 지형 분류 래스터 ----------
CLS = {'p': 0, 'f': 1, 'h': 2, 'm': 3, 'd': 4, 'w': 5, 's': 6}
cim = Image.new('L', (TEXW, TEXH), 0); cd = ImageDraw.Draw(cim)
vim = Image.new('L', (TEXW, TEXH), 0); vd = ImageDraw.Draw(vim)
rim = Image.new('L', (TEXW, TEXH), 0); rd = ImageDraw.Draw(rim)     # 거점·부지 주변(평탄)
for r in range(H):
    for c in range(W):
        t = M['traw'][r * W + c]; pl = hexpoly(c, r, 1.03)
        if CLS[t]: cd.polygon(pl, fill=CLS[t])
        if M['t'][r * W + c] == 'x': vd.polygon(pl, fill=255)
for b in M['bases']: rd.polygon(hexpoly(b['c'], b['r'], 1.6), fill=255)
for n, v in M['plots'].items():
    for (c, r) in v: rd.polygon(hexpoly(c, r, 1.05), fill=255)
cls = np.asarray(cim)

def vnoise(cell, seed, shape=(TEXH, TEXW)):
    rs = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    gh, gw = int(shape[0] / cell) + 4, int(shape[1] / cell) + 4
    z = ndi.zoom(rs.rand(gh, gw).astype(f32), cell, order=3)
    return np.clip(z[int(cell):int(cell) + shape[0], int(cell):int(cell) + shape[1]], 0, 1)
def fbm(cell, seed, octs=4, ridged=False):
    out = np.zeros((TEXH, TEXW), f32); amp = 1.0; tot = 0.0
    for o in range(octs):
        n = vnoise(max(2.0, cell / 2 ** o), seed + o * 17)
        if ridged: n = 1.0 - np.abs(2.0 * n - 1.0); n *= n
        out += amp * n; tot += amp; amp *= 0.5
    return out / tot

# 경계를 흐트러뜨린 뒤 부드럽게
yy, xx = np.mgrid[0:TEXH, 0:TEXW].astype(f32)
wamp = PPU * 0.42
wx_ = xx + (vnoise(PPU * 1.3, 101) - 0.5) * 2 * wamp; wy_ = yy + (vnoise(PPU * 1.3, 102) - 0.5) * 2 * wamp
clsw = ndi.map_coordinates(cls, [wy_, wx_], order=0, mode='nearest')
del wx_, wy_
SIG = PPU * 0.36
def mask(v, sig=SIG, src=None): return ndi.gaussian_filter(((clsw if src is None else src) == v).astype(f32), sig)
Mm, Mh, Mf, Md = mask(3), mask(2), mask(1), mask(4)
_lm = np.load(os.path.join(os.path.dirname(MAPJSON), 'landmask.npy'))
_zy, _zx = np.mgrid[0:TEXH, 0:TEXW].astype(f32)
_land = ndi.map_coordinates(_lm, [_zy / PPU / 0.2, _zx / PPU / 0.2], order=1, mode='nearest'); del _zy, _zx
_land = ndi.gaussian_filter(_land, PPU * 0.10) + (vnoise(PPU * 0.5, 444) - 0.5) * 0.25
Ms = ndi.gaussian_filter((_land < 0.5).astype(f32), PPU * 0.12); del _land
Mw = ndi.gaussian_filter((cls == 5).astype(f32), PPU * 0.30)
Mvoid = ndi.gaussian_filter(np.asarray(vim, dtype=f32) / 255.0, PPU * 0.9)
Mbase = ndi.gaussian_filter(np.asarray(rim, dtype=f32) / 255.0, PPU * 0.5)

# ---------- 고도 ----------
dem = np.asarray(Image.open(os.path.join(GEO, 'elev_bump_4k.jpg')).convert('L'), dtype=f32)
q = 4
qy, qx = np.mgrid[0:TEXH:q, 0:TEXW:q].astype(np.float64)
lat, lon = lcc_inv(G['X0'] + (qx + q / 2) / PPU * G['U'], G['Y0'] - (qy + q / 2) / PPU * G['U'])
E = ndi.map_coordinates(dem, [(90.0 - lat) / 180.0 * dem.shape[0] - 0.5, (lon + 180.0) / 360.0 * dem.shape[1] - 0.5], order=1, mode='nearest')
def up(a): return ndi.zoom(a.astype(f32), q, order=1)[:TEXH, :TEXW]
ELEV = np.clip((up(ndi.gaussian_filter(E, 1.0)) - 24.5) * 30.0, 0, None)          # m (근사)
LATM = up(lat); LONM = up(lon)
B_raw = 0.03 + 1.45 * (ELEV / 4000.0) ** 0.85
B = ndi.gaussian_filter(B_raw, PPU * 1.6)
ridge = fbm(PPU * 2.6, 7, 5, ridged=True)
n_mid = fbm(PPU * 1.8, 31, 4)
n_fine = fbm(PPU * 0.5, 57, 3)
flat = 1.0 - 0.85 * Mbase
HGT = B + (B_raw - B) * (0.6 + 1.2 * Mm) * flat
HGT += Mm * (0.26 + 0.95 * ridge ** 1.25) * flat
HGT += Mh * (0.04 + 0.20 * n_mid) * flat
HGT += 0.012 * (n_fine - 0.5)
HGT -= 0.06 * Mw
HGT = HGT * (1 - Ms) + (-0.22) * Ms
HGT = ndi.gaussian_filter(HGT, 0.8)
print('height range %.2f .. %.2f' % (HGT.min(), HGT.max()))

# ---------- 음영 ----------
gy, gx = np.gradient(HGT); gy *= PPU; gx *= PPU
ZX = 1.15
nx, ny, nz = -gx * ZX, np.ones_like(gx), -gy * ZX
nl = np.sqrt(nx * nx + ny * ny + nz * nz)
L = np.array([-0.55, 0.62, -0.56]); L /= np.linalg.norm(L)
shade = (nx * L[0] + ny * L[1] + nz * L[2]) / nl
shade = np.clip(0.50 + 0.80 * (shade - L[1]) / max(1e-6, 1 - L[1]) * 0.5 + 0.5 * (shade / L[1]), 0.52, 1.24)
occ = np.clip(1.0 + 1.2 * (HGT - ndi.gaussian_filter(HGT, PPU * 0.8)), 0.78, 1.12)
del gx, gy, nx, ny, nz, nl

# ---------- 색 ----------
def C(r, g, b): return np.array([r, g, b], f32) / 255.0
def lerp3(a, b, t): return a[None, None, :] * (1 - t[..., None]) + b[None, None, :] * t[..., None]
south = np.clip((34.5 - LATM) / 7.0, 0, 1)
arid = np.clip(np.clip((LATM - 34.0) / 5.0, 0, 1) * np.clip((110.5 - LONM) / 6.0, 0, 1) + np.clip((LATM - 39.5) / 2.5, 0, 1) * np.clip((118.0 - LONM) / 4.0, 0, 1), 0, 1)
moist = np.clip(south * 0.8 + 0.4 * (vnoise(PPU * 6, 88) - 0.5), 0, 1)
col = lerp3(C(166, 172, 106), C(122, 156, 84), moist)                      # 평지: 북쪽 밀밭빛 ↔ 남쪽 초록
col = col * (1 - arid[..., None] * 0.75) + C(205, 182, 128)[None, None, :] * (arid[..., None] * 0.75)
col *= (0.90 + 0.20 * vnoise(PPU * 2.2, 5))[..., None]
hillc = lerp3(C(158, 142, 92), C(112, 132, 74), np.clip(south * 0.9, 0, 1))
hillc = hillc * (1 - arid[..., None] * 0.7) + C(196, 168, 116)[None, None, :] * (arid[..., None] * 0.7)
col = col * (1 - Mh[..., None]) + hillc * Mh[..., None]
forc = lerp3(C(76, 108, 62), C(52, 96, 52), south) * (0.80 + 0.40 * fbm(PPU * 0.45, 13, 2))[..., None]
col = col * (1 - Mf[..., None]) + forc * Mf[..., None]
col = col * (1 - Md[..., None]) + (C(214, 190, 136)[None, None, :] * (0.90 + 0.2 * n_mid)[..., None]) * Md[..., None]
hi = np.clip((HGT - B) / 1.0, 0, 1)
rock = lerp3(C(126, 112, 88), C(152, 142, 124), np.clip(hi * 0.9 + (ELEV - 1500) / 5000.0, 0, 1))
rock = rock * (1 - (south * 0.45 * (1 - hi))[..., None]) + C(86, 108, 70)[None, None, :] * (south * 0.45 * (1 - hi))[..., None]   # 남쪽 산은 숲이 덮는다
snow = np.clip((ELEV + 900 * hi - 4500) / 500.0, 0, 1) * Mm
rock = rock * (1 - snow[..., None]) + C(238, 240, 244)[None, None, :] * snow[..., None]
col = col * (1 - Mm[..., None]) + rock * Mm[..., None]
col *= (shade * occ)[..., None]
col *= (0.94 + 0.12 * n_fine)[..., None]

# 길(희미하게) — 선명한 길은 런타임에 띠로 그린다
def smooth(pts, it=2):
    for _ in range(it):
        out = [pts[0]]
        for i in range(len(pts) - 1):
            (x0, z0), (x1, z1) = pts[i], pts[i + 1]
            out.append((0.75 * x0 + 0.25 * x1, 0.75 * z0 + 0.25 * z1)); out.append((0.25 * x0 + 0.75 * x1, 0.25 * z0 + 0.75 * z1))
        out.append(pts[-1]); pts = out
    return pts
ROADS = [smooth([center(c, r) for c, r in p]) for p in M['paths']]
SS = 2
ov = Image.new('L', (TEXW * SS, TEXH * SS), 0); od = ImageDraw.Draw(ov)
for pts in ROADS: od.line([(x * PPU * SS, z * PPU * SS) for x, z in pts], fill=255, width=max(2, int(PPU * 0.20 * SS)), joint='curve')
roadm = np.asarray(ov.resize((TEXW, TEXH), Image.LANCZOS), dtype=f32) / 255.0
col = col * (1 - 0.62 * roadm[..., None]) + C(196, 170, 124)[None, None, :] * (0.62 * roadm[..., None])

# 물: 작은 강(선) → 큰 강·호수·바다
ov = Image.new('L', (TEXW * SS, TEXH * SS), 0); od = ImageDraw.Draw(ov)
for rv in M['rivers']:
    if not rv['major']: od.line([(x * PPU * SS, z * PPU * SS) for x, z in smooth([tuple(p) for p in rv['pts']], 1)], fill=255, width=max(2, int(PPU * 0.22 * SS)), joint='curve')
minor = np.asarray(ov.resize((TEXW, TEXH), Image.LANCZOS), dtype=f32) / 255.0 * (1 - np.clip(Mm * 0.5, 0, 1))
ov = Image.new('L', (TEXW * SS, TEXH * SS), 0); od = ImageDraw.Draw(ov)
for rv in M['rivers']:
    if rv['major']: od.line([(x * PPU * SS, z * PPU * SS) for x, z in smooth([tuple(p) for p in rv['pts']], 1)], fill=255, width=int(PPU * 0.95 * SS), joint='curve')
major = ndi.gaussian_filter(np.asarray(ov.resize((TEXW, TEXH), Image.LANCZOS), dtype=f32) / 255.0, 1.2)
del ov
wn = 0.92 + 0.16 * vnoise(PPU * 0.8, 71)
rivc = C(74, 128, 156)[None, None, :] * wn[..., None]
bank = np.clip(ndi.gaussian_filter(major, PPU * 0.35) * 1.6 - major, 0, 1)
col = col * (1 - 0.28 * bank[..., None]) + C(196, 186, 140)[None, None, :] * (0.28 * bank[..., None])
col = col * (1 - 0.85 * minor[..., None]) + rivc * (0.85 * minor[..., None])
col = col * (1 - major[..., None]) + rivc * major[..., None]
deep = np.clip(ndi.gaussian_filter(Ms, PPU * 2.6) * 1.7 - 0.6, 0, 1)
seac = lerp3(C(92, 150, 160), C(38, 84, 118), deep) * wn[..., None]
sm = np.clip(Ms * 1.5 - 0.25, 0, 1)
shore = np.clip(1 - np.abs(ndi.gaussian_filter(Ms, PPU * 0.18) - 0.30) / 0.22, 0, 1)
col = col * (1 - 0.35 * shore[..., None]) + C(226, 212, 170)[None, None, :] * (0.35 * shore[..., None])
col = col * (1 - sm[..., None]) + seac * sm[..., None]

# 변경: 채도를 빼고 어둡게
gray = col.mean(axis=2, keepdims=True)
vcol = (col * 0.35 + gray * 0.65) * 0.60 + C(22, 20, 18)[None, None, :]
vk = np.clip(Mvoid * 1.15, 0, 1)[..., None] * 0.92
col = col * (1 - vk) + vcol * vk

img = Image.fromarray(np.clip(col * 255.0, 0, 255).astype(np.uint8), 'RGB').filter(ImageFilter.UnsharpMask(1.6, 55, 2))
img.save(OUT_TEX, quality=84, optimize=True, subsampling=1)
print('texture bytes', os.path.getsize(OUT_TEX))
mini = img.resize((220, int(round(220 * TEXH / TEXW))), Image.LANCZOS); mb = io.BytesIO(); mini.save(mb, 'JPEG', quality=72)

# ---------- 높이맵(메시용) ----------
GX = 2 * W + 2; GZ = int(round(WZ / (WX / (GX - 1)))) + 1
Hs = ndi.gaussian_filter(HGT, PPU * 0.22)
ix = np.linspace(0, TEXW - 1, GX); iz = np.linspace(0, TEXH - 1, GZ)
IZ, IX = np.meshgrid(iz, ix, indexing='ij')
HM = ndi.map_coordinates(Hs, [IZ, IX], order=1, mode='nearest')
hmin, hmax = float(HM.min()), float(HM.max())
hm16 = np.round((HM - hmin) / (hmax - hmin) * 65535.0).astype('<u2')
out = dict(WX=round(WX, 4), WZ=round(WZ, 4), GX=GX, GZ=GZ, hmin=round(hmin, 4), hmax=round(hmax, 4),
           hm=base64.b64encode(hm16.tobytes()).decode('ascii'),
           roads=[[round(v, 2) for p in pts for v in p] for pts in ROADS],
           mini='data:image/jpeg;base64,' + base64.b64encode(mb.getvalue()).decode('ascii'))
with open(OUT_JS, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f: f.write('var MAP3D=' + json.dumps(out, separators=(',', ':')) + ';\n')
print('mesh grid %dx%d  h %.2f..%.2f  map3d.js %d bytes' % (GX, GZ, hmin, hmax, os.path.getsize(OUT_JS)))

=====8<===== tools/bake3d_ink.py
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""천하도 v0.4 — 3D 지형 굽기(수묵): 높이맵은 v0.2와 같고, 바탕 텍스처만 먹의 농담으로 칠한다.
사용: bake3d.py <geo_dir> <mapdata.json> <out terrain.jpg> <out map3d.js> [TEXW]
"""
import sys, os, json, math, base64, io
import numpy as np
from PIL import Image, ImageDraw, ImageFilter
from scipy import ndimage as ndi
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
from geo import lcc_inv

GEO, MAPJSON, OUT_TEX, OUT_JS = sys.argv[1:5]
TEXW = int(sys.argv[5]) if len(sys.argv) > 5 else 3072
Image.MAX_IMAGE_PIXELS = None
SQ3 = math.sqrt(3)
M = json.load(open(MAPJSON, encoding='utf-8'))
W, H = M['W'], M['H']; G = M['grid']; WX, WZ = G['WX'], G['WZ']
PPU = TEXW / WX; TEXH = int(round(WZ * PPU))
print('texture %dx%d  %.2f px/unit  %.1f px/hex' % (TEXW, TEXH, PPU, PPU * SQ3))
f32 = np.float32

def center(c, r): return (SQ3 * (c + 0.5 * (r & 1)) + SQ3 / 2, 1.5 * r + 1.0)
def hexpoly(c, r, k=1.0):
    x, z = center(c, r)
    return [((x + k * math.cos(math.radians(60 * i - 30))) * PPU, (z + k * math.sin(math.radians(60 * i - 30))) * PPU) for i in range(6)]

# ---------- 지형 분류 래스터 ----------
CLS = {'p': 0, 'f': 1, 'h': 2, 'm': 3, 'd': 4, 'w': 5, 's': 6}
cim = Image.new('L', (TEXW, TEXH), 0); cd = ImageDraw.Draw(cim)
vim = Image.new('L', (TEXW, TEXH), 0); vd = ImageDraw.Draw(vim)
rim = Image.new('L', (TEXW, TEXH), 0); rd = ImageDraw.Draw(rim)     # 거점·부지 주변(평탄)
for r in range(H):
    for c in range(W):
        t = M['traw'][r * W + c]; pl = hexpoly(c, r, 1.03)
        if CLS[t]: cd.polygon(pl, fill=CLS[t])
        if M['t'][r * W + c] == 'x': vd.polygon(pl, fill=255)
for b in M['bases']: rd.polygon(hexpoly(b['c'], b['r'], 1.6), fill=255)
for n, v in M['plots'].items():
    for (c, r) in v: rd.polygon(hexpoly(c, r, 1.05), fill=255)
cls = np.asarray(cim)

def vnoise(cell, seed, shape=(TEXH, TEXW)):
    rs = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    gh, gw = int(shape[0] / cell) + 4, int(shape[1] / cell) + 4
    z = ndi.zoom(rs.rand(gh, gw).astype(f32), cell, order=3)
    return np.clip(z[int(cell):int(cell) + shape[0], int(cell):int(cell) + shape[1]], 0, 1)
def fbm(cell, seed, octs=4, ridged=False):
    out = np.zeros((TEXH, TEXW), f32); amp = 1.0; tot = 0.0
    for o in range(octs):
        n = vnoise(max(2.0, cell / 2 ** o), seed + o * 17)
        if ridged: n = 1.0 - np.abs(2.0 * n - 1.0); n *= n
        out += amp * n; tot += amp; amp *= 0.5
    return out / tot

# 경계를 흐트러뜨린 뒤 부드럽게
yy, xx = np.mgrid[0:TEXH, 0:TEXW].astype(f32)
wamp = PPU * 0.42
wx_ = xx + (vnoise(PPU * 1.3, 101) - 0.5) * 2 * wamp; wy_ = yy + (vnoise(PPU * 1.3, 102) - 0.5) * 2 * wamp
clsw = ndi.map_coordinates(cls, [wy_, wx_], order=0, mode='nearest')
del wx_, wy_
SIG = PPU * 0.36
def mask(v, sig=SIG, src=None): return ndi.gaussian_filter(((clsw if src is None else src) == v).astype(f32), sig)
Mm, Mh, Mf, Md = mask(3), mask(2), mask(1), mask(4)
_lm = np.load(os.path.join(os.path.dirname(MAPJSON), 'landmask.npy'))
_zy, _zx = np.mgrid[0:TEXH, 0:TEXW].astype(f32)
_land = ndi.map_coordinates(_lm, [_zy / PPU / 0.2, _zx / PPU / 0.2], order=1, mode='nearest'); del _zy, _zx
_land = ndi.gaussian_filter(_land, PPU * 0.10) + (vnoise(PPU * 0.5, 444) - 0.5) * 0.25
Ms = ndi.gaussian_filter((_land < 0.5).astype(f32), PPU * 0.12); del _land
Mw = ndi.gaussian_filter((cls == 5).astype(f32), PPU * 0.30)
Mvoid = ndi.gaussian_filter(np.asarray(vim, dtype=f32) / 255.0, PPU * 0.9)
Mbase = ndi.gaussian_filter(np.asarray(rim, dtype=f32) / 255.0, PPU * 0.5)

# ---------- 고도 ----------
dem = np.asarray(Image.open(os.path.join(GEO, 'elev_bump_4k.jpg')).convert('L'), dtype=f32)
q = 4
qy, qx = np.mgrid[0:TEXH:q, 0:TEXW:q].astype(np.float64)
lat, lon = lcc_inv(G['X0'] + (qx + q / 2) / PPU * G['U'], G['Y0'] - (qy + q / 2) / PPU * G['U'])
E = ndi.map_coordinates(dem, [(90.0 - lat) / 180.0 * dem.shape[0] - 0.5, (lon + 180.0) / 360.0 * dem.shape[1] - 0.5], order=1, mode='nearest')
def up(a): return ndi.zoom(a.astype(f32), q, order=1)[:TEXH, :TEXW]
ELEV = np.clip((up(ndi.gaussian_filter(E, 1.0)) - 24.5) * 30.0, 0, None)          # m (근사)
LATM = up(lat); LONM = up(lon)
B_raw = 0.03 + 1.45 * (ELEV / 4000.0) ** 0.85
B = ndi.gaussian_filter(B_raw, PPU * 1.6)
ridge = fbm(PPU * 2.6, 7, 5, ridged=True)
n_mid = fbm(PPU * 1.8, 31, 4)
n_fine = fbm(PPU * 0.5, 57, 3)
flat = 1.0 - 0.85 * Mbase
HGT = B + (B_raw - B) * (0.6 + 1.2 * Mm) * flat
HGT += Mm * (0.26 + 0.95 * ridge ** 1.25) * flat
HGT += Mh * (0.04 + 0.20 * n_mid) * flat
HGT += 0.012 * (n_fine - 0.5)
HGT -= 0.06 * Mw
HGT = HGT * (1 - Ms) + (-0.22) * Ms
HGT = ndi.gaussian_filter(HGT, 0.8)
print('height range %.2f .. %.2f' % (HGT.min(), HGT.max()))

# ---------- 음영 ----------
gy, gx = np.gradient(HGT); gy *= PPU; gx *= PPU
ZX = 1.15
nx, ny, nz = -gx * ZX, np.ones_like(gx), -gy * ZX
nl = np.sqrt(nx * nx + ny * ny + nz * nz)
L = np.array([-0.55, 0.62, -0.56]); L /= np.linalg.norm(L)
shade = (nx * L[0] + ny * L[1] + nz * L[2]) / nl
shade = np.clip(0.50 + 0.80 * (shade - L[1]) / max(1e-6, 1 - L[1]) * 0.5 + 0.5 * (shade / L[1]), 0.52, 1.24)
occ = np.clip(1.0 + 1.2 * (HGT - ndi.gaussian_filter(HGT, PPU * 0.8)), 0.78, 1.12)
del gx, gy, nx, ny, nz, nl

# ---------- 색: 수묵담채 ----------
# 종이 바탕 위에 먹의 농담으로 지형을 올린다. 평지는 여백, 산은 준법(皴法) 같은 마른 붓 결과 그늘, 숲은 먹점, 물은 비워 두고 물가만 선과 번짐.
def C(r, g, b): return np.array([r, g, b], f32) / 255.0
PAPER = C(239, 233, 219); INKC = C(30, 28, 26)
south = np.clip((34.5 - LATM) / 7.0, 0, 1)
arid = np.clip(np.clip((LATM - 34.0) / 5.0, 0, 1) * np.clip((110.5 - LONM) / 6.0, 0, 1) + np.clip((LATM - 39.5) / 2.5, 0, 1) * np.clip((118.0 - LONM) / 4.0, 0, 1), 0, 1)
hi = np.clip((HGT - B) / 1.0, 0, 1)
blot = vnoise(PPU * 1.1, 201)                                   # 번짐의 얼룩
blot2 = vnoise(PPU * 3.2, 202)
dry = fbm(PPU * 0.22, 203, 2)                                   # 마른 붓의 갈라짐
sh = np.clip((1.02 - shade) / 0.50, 0, 1)                       # 빛 반대쪽 비탈
lit = np.clip((shade - 1.02) / 0.22, 0, 1)
D = np.zeros((TEXH, TEXW), f32)
# 산: 그늘 + 능선 결 + 마른 붓. 산자락은 안개처럼 비운다
rl = np.clip((ridge - 0.60) / 0.26, 0, 1)
mt = (0.16 + 0.62 * sh ** 0.85 + 0.30 * rl * (0.45 + 0.55 * sh)) * (0.40 + 0.60 * np.clip(hi * 1.25 + 0.12, 0, 1))
mt *= 0.62 + 0.62 * blot
mt = mt * (0.66 + 0.34 * np.clip((dry - 0.28) / 0.30, 0, 1))
mt *= 1.0 - 0.55 * lit
snow = np.clip((ELEV + 900 * hi - 4500) / 500.0, 0, 1)
mt *= 1.0 - 0.85 * snow
# 덧칠한 먹의 층: 농담을 몇 단으로 끊고, 층의 가장자리(물 마른 자국)를 조금 짙게
lay = np.floor(np.clip(mt, 0, 1) * 4.0 + 0.35 * (blot - 0.5)) / 4.0
lay = ndi.gaussian_filter(lay, 0.7)
gyl, gxl = np.gradient(lay)
wet = np.clip(np.sqrt(gxl * gxl + gyl * gyl) * 9.0, 0, 1)
mt = 0.45 * mt + 0.55 * lay + 0.10 * wet
del gyl, gxl, lay
# 능선: 높이의 볼록한 줄기를 가는 붓선으로
Hr = ndi.gaussian_filter(HGT, 1.1)
curv = -ndi.laplace(Hr)
crest = np.clip((curv - 0.004) / 0.010, 0, 1) * np.clip(hi * 1.6, 0, 1)
crest = ndi.gaussian_filter(crest, 0.6) * (0.45 + 0.75 * dry)
mt = mt + 0.42 * crest
del Hr, curv
D += Mm * mt
edge_m = np.clip(1 - np.abs(Mm - 0.42) / 0.20, 0, 1) * np.clip(sh * 1.4 + 0.25, 0, 1)      # 번진 가장자리가 짙게 마른 자국
D += 0.16 * edge_m * (0.5 + blot)
# 구릉: 옅은 번짐과 그늘
D += Mh * (1 - Mm) * (0.06 + 0.30 * sh + 0.10 * np.clip((n_mid - 0.52) / 0.2, 0, 1)) * (0.6 + 0.7 * blot)
# 숲: 먹점(米點). 남쪽일수록 촘촘
rsd = np.random.RandomState(13)
imp = (rsd.rand(TEXH, TEXW) < (0.050 + 0.022 * south)).astype(f32)                 # 점을 찍을 자리
imp *= (0.55 + 0.9 * rsd.rand(TEXH, TEXW)).astype(f32)                             # 점마다 먹의 양이 다르다
dab = np.clip(ndi.gaussian_filter(imp, 1.15) * 9.5 - 0.42, 0, 1)
dab2 = np.clip(ndi.gaussian_filter(np.roll(imp, (3, 2), (0, 1)), 2.1) * 22.0 - 0.55, 0, 1)   # 번진 옅은 점
D += Mf * (1 - Mm) * (0.05 + 0.62 * dab * (0.75 + 0.4 * blot2) + 0.16 * dab2)
del imp
# 황무지: 성긴 점
st = np.clip((fbm(PPU * 0.16, 77, 2) - 0.66) / 0.08, 0, 1)
D += Md * (0.03 + 0.20 * st)
# 평지: 거의 여백. 아주 옅은 얼룩만
D += (1 - np.clip(Mm + Mh + Mf + Md, 0, 1)) * 0.035 * blot2
D *= 1.0 - 0.80 * Mbase                                          # 성과 부지 둘레는 비워 둔다
D = np.clip(D, 0, 1) ** 1.12
col = PAPER[None, None, :] * (1 - D[..., None]) + INKC[None, None, :] * D[..., None]
# 담채: 숲과 남쪽 산에 녹청, 북서의 마른 땅에 황토를 아주 옅게
gk = np.clip(Mf * 0.20 + Mm * south * 0.10 + Mh * south * 0.06, 0, 0.26) * (0.6 + 0.8 * blot2)
col = col * (1 - gk[..., None]) + (col * C(150, 188, 160)[None, None, :] / PAPER[None, None, :]) * gk[..., None]
ok = np.clip(arid * 0.16 * (0.5 + blot2), 0, 0.22)
col = col * (1 - ok[..., None]) + (col * C(226, 196, 142)[None, None, :] / PAPER[None, None, :]) * ok[..., None]

def smooth(pts, it=2):
    for _ in range(it):
        out = [pts[0]]
        for i in range(len(pts) - 1):
            (x0, z0), (x1, z1) = pts[i], pts[i + 1]
            out.append((0.75 * x0 + 0.25 * x1, 0.75 * z0 + 0.25 * z1)); out.append((0.25 * x0 + 0.75 * x1, 0.25 * z0 + 0.75 * z1))
        out.append(pts[-1]); pts = out
    return pts
ROADS = [smooth([center(c, r) for c, r in p]) for p in M['paths']]
SS = 2
# 물: 작은 강은 가는 쪽빛 먹선, 큰 강·호수·바다는 종이를 비우고 물가에 선과 번짐
ov = Image.new('L', (TEXW * SS, TEXH * SS), 0); od = ImageDraw.Draw(ov)
for rv in M['rivers']:
    if not rv['major']: od.line([(x * PPU * SS, z * PPU * SS) for x, z in smooth([tuple(p) for p in rv['pts']], 1)], fill=255, width=max(2, int(PPU * 0.13 * SS)), joint='curve')
minor = np.asarray(ov.resize((TEXW, TEXH), Image.LANCZOS), dtype=f32) / 255.0 * (1 - np.clip(Mm * 0.5, 0, 1))
ov = Image.new('L', (TEXW * SS, TEXH * SS), 0); od = ImageDraw.Draw(ov)
for rv in M['rivers']:
    if rv['major']: od.line([(x * PPU * SS, z * PPU * SS) for x, z in smooth([tuple(p) for p in rv['pts']], 1)], fill=255, width=int(PPU * 0.95 * SS), joint='curve')
major = ndi.gaussian_filter(np.asarray(ov.resize((TEXW, TEXH), Image.LANCZOS), dtype=f32) / 255.0, 1.2)
del ov
WATERC = C(232, 232, 226); INDIGO = C(96, 124, 146)
mk = (0.50 * minor * (0.7 + 0.5 * blot))[..., None]
col = col * (1 - mk) + INDIGO[None, None, :] * 0.72 * mk
rb = np.clip(major * 1.25, 0, 1)[..., None]
col = col * (1 - rb) + (WATERC * 0.985)[None, None, :] * rb
bankl = np.clip(1 - np.abs(major - 0.46) / 0.22, 0, 1) ** 1.5 * (0.45 + 0.5 * blot)             # 강기슭 먹선
col = col * (1 - 0.62 * bankl[..., None]) + INKC[None, None, :] * (0.62 * bankl[..., None])
rw = np.clip(major - 0.6, 0, 1)[..., None] * 0.10                                               # 강 한가운데 옅은 쪽빛
col = col * (1 - rw) + INDIGO[None, None, :] * rw
sm = np.clip(Ms * 1.5 - 0.25, 0, 1)
deep = np.clip(ndi.gaussian_filter(Ms, PPU * 2.6) * 1.7 - 0.6, 0, 1)
near = np.clip(ndi.gaussian_filter(Ms, PPU * 0.9) * 2.0 - 0.15, 0, 1) * (1 - deep)             # 물가에서 바깥으로 번지는 띠
waves = np.clip((np.sin(yy / (PPU * 0.33) + 5.0 * vnoise(PPU * 2.4, 301)) - 0.93) / 0.07, 0, 1) * (0.35 + 0.65 * vnoise(PPU * 1.6, 302)) * np.clip(deep * 1.4 + 0.2, 0, 1)
seac = WATERC[None, None, :] * (1 - (0.10 * near + 0.05 * deep)[..., None]) 
seac = seac * (1 - (0.16 * near + 0.07 * deep)[..., None]) + INDIGO[None, None, :] * (0.16 * near + 0.07 * deep)[..., None]
seac = seac * (1 - 0.20 * waves[..., None]) + INDIGO[None, None, :] * (0.20 * waves[..., None])
col = col * (1 - sm[..., None]) + seac * sm[..., None]
shore = np.clip(1 - np.abs(ndi.gaussian_filter(Ms, PPU * 0.10) - 0.36) / 0.13, 0, 1) ** 1.6 * (0.5 + 0.5 * blot)     # 해안 먹선
col = col * (1 - 0.70 * shore[..., None]) + INKC[None, None, :] * (0.70 * shore[..., None])

# 변경(갈 수 없는 땅): 안개 속으로 — 옅게 비운다
vk = np.clip(Mvoid * 1.15, 0, 1)[..., None] * 0.72
col = col * (1 - vk) + (PAPER * 0.992)[None, None, :] * vk
# 종이 결
col *= (0.985 + 0.03 * n_fine)[..., None]

img = Image.fromarray(np.clip(col * 255.0, 0, 255).astype(np.uint8), 'RGB').filter(ImageFilter.UnsharpMask(1.2, 30, 2))
img.save(OUT_TEX, quality=84, optimize=True, subsampling=1)
print('texture bytes', os.path.getsize(OUT_TEX))
mini = img.resize((220, int(round(220 * TEXH / TEXW))), Image.LANCZOS); mb = io.BytesIO(); mini.save(mb, 'JPEG', quality=72)

# ---------- 높이맵(메시용) ----------
GX = 2 * W + 2; GZ = int(round(WZ / (WX / (GX - 1)))) + 1
Hs = ndi.gaussian_filter(HGT, PPU * 0.22)
ix = np.linspace(0, TEXW - 1, GX); iz = np.linspace(0, TEXH - 1, GZ)
IZ, IX = np.meshgrid(iz, ix, indexing='ij')
HM = ndi.map_coordinates(Hs, [IZ, IX], order=1, mode='nearest')
hmin, hmax = float(HM.min()), float(HM.max())
hm16 = np.round((HM - hmin) / (hmax - hmin) * 65535.0).astype('<u2')
out = dict(WX=round(WX, 4), WZ=round(WZ, 4), GX=GX, GZ=GZ, hmin=round(hmin, 4), hmax=round(hmax, 4),
           hm=base64.b64encode(hm16.tobytes()).decode('ascii'),
           roads=[[round(v, 2) for p in pts for v in p] for pts in ROADS],
           mini='data:image/jpeg;base64,' + base64.b64encode(mb.getvalue()).decode('ascii'))
with open(OUT_JS, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f: f.write('var MAP3D=' + json.dumps(out, separators=(',', ':')) + ';\n')
print('mesh grid %dx%d  h %.2f..%.2f  map3d.js %d bytes' % (GX, GZ, hmin, hmax, os.path.getsize(OUT_JS)))

=====8<===== tools/make_sprites.py
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""지도용 스프라이트: 흰 배경 제거(테두리에서 번지는 흰색 + 갇힌 순백 영역) → 여백 잘라내기 → WebP → src/sprites.js
사용: make_sprites.py <raw_dir> <out_dir> <out_js>"""
import sys, os, io, json, base64
import numpy as np
from PIL import Image
from scipy import ndimage as ndi
RAW, OUT, OUT_JS = sys.argv[1:4]
os.makedirs(OUT, exist_ok=True)
# key: (최대 변 px, 기준점 y(아래에서의 비율))
SPEC = {'city_L': (360, 0.30), 'city_M': (340, 0.30), 'city_S': (320, 0.30), 'gate': (340, 0.24),
        'market': (240, 0.33), 'farm': (240, 0.33), 'barracks': (240, 0.30), 'smithy': (240, 0.30), 'stable': (240, 0.33), 'workshop': (240, 0.30), 'mint': (240, 0.30), 'granary': (240, 0.33),
        'u_sword': (200, 0.16), 'u_spear': (200, 0.14), 'u_halberd': (200, 0.14), 'u_bow': (200, 0.16), 'u_cav': (210, 0.16), 'u_ram': (210, 0.18), 'u_trans': (220, 0.16)}
out = {}
for key, (size, ay) in SPEC.items():
    im = np.asarray(Image.open(os.path.join(RAW, key + '.png')).convert('RGB')).astype(np.float32)
    mn = im.min(axis=2); mx = im.max(axis=2)
    white = (mn >= 236) & (mx - mn <= 14)
    lab, n = ndi.label(white)
    border = set(np.unique(np.concatenate([lab[0], lab[-1], lab[:, 0], lab[:, -1]]))) - {0}
    bg = np.isin(lab, list(border))
    # 갇힌 순백 영역(다리 사이, 창 사이)
    pure = (mn >= 247) & ~bg
    lab2, n2 = ndi.label(pure)
    if n2:
        sizes = ndi.sum(pure, lab2, range(1, n2 + 1))
        bg |= np.isin(lab2, [i + 1 for i, s in enumerate(sizes) if s >= 24])
    alpha = (~bg).astype(np.float32)
    # 가장자리: 흰 기운을 걷어 낸다
    edge = ndi.binary_dilation(bg, iterations=2) & ~bg
    a_e = np.clip((255.0 - mn) / 70.0, 0.0, 1.0)
    alpha[edge] = np.minimum(alpha[edge], a_e[edge])
    a3 = np.clip(alpha, 0.02, 1)[..., None]
    rgb = np.where(edge[..., None], np.clip((im - 255.0 * (1 - a3)) / a3, 0, 255), im)
    rgba = np.dstack([rgb, alpha * 255.0]).astype(np.uint8)
    ys, xs = np.where(alpha > 0.05)
    box = (xs.min(), ys.min(), xs.max() + 1, ys.max() + 1)
    sp = Image.fromarray(rgba, 'RGBA').crop(box)
    k = size / max(sp.size)
    sp = sp.resize((max(1, round(sp.width * k)), max(1, round(sp.height * k))), Image.LANCZOS)
    if key.startswith('u_'):                       # 부대: 밝게 + 옅은 테두리(바탕과 구분)
        arr = np.asarray(sp).astype(np.float32); rgbf = arr[..., :3] / 255.0
        rgbf = np.clip(rgbf ** 0.78 * 1.12, 0, 1)
        al = arr[..., 3] / 255.0
        ring = np.clip(ndi.gaussian_filter(ndi.grey_dilation(al, size=(5, 5)), 0.8), 0, 1)
        oc = np.array([0.98, 0.94, 0.80], np.float32)
        out_a = np.maximum(al, ring * 0.9)
        out_rgb = rgbf * al[..., None] + oc[None, None, :] * (1 - al[..., None])
        sp = Image.fromarray(np.dstack([out_rgb * 255.0, out_a * 255.0]).astype(np.uint8), 'RGBA')
    sp.save(os.path.join(OUT, key + '.png'))
    bio = io.BytesIO(); sp.save(bio, 'WEBP', quality=86, method=6)
    out[key] = dict(u='data:image/webp;base64,' + base64.b64encode(bio.getvalue()).decode('ascii'), w=sp.width, h=sp.height, ay=ay)
    print(key, sp.size, len(bio.getvalue()))
with open(OUT_JS, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f: f.write('var SPRITES=' + json.dumps(out, separators=(',', ':')) + ';\n')
print('sprites.js', os.path.getsize(OUT_JS))

=====8<===== tools/make_sprites_ink.py
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""v0.4 수묵 스프라이트: 흰 바탕을 걷어 내되, 바깥으로 번진 담묵은 반투명 먹으로 남긴다 → 여백 잘라내기 → WebP → src/sprites.js
사용: make_sprites_ink.py <raw_dir> <out_dir> <out_js>"""
import sys, os, io, json, base64
import numpy as np
from PIL import Image
from scipy import ndimage as ndi
RAW, OUT, OUT_JS = sys.argv[1:4]
os.makedirs(OUT, exist_ok=True)
# key: (최대 변 px, 기준점 y(아래에서의 비율))
SPEC = {'city_L': (360, 0.30), 'city_M': (340, 0.30), 'city_S': (320, 0.30), 'gate': (340, 0.24),
        'market': (240, 0.33), 'farm': (240, 0.33), 'barracks': (240, 0.30), 'smithy': (240, 0.30), 'stable': (240, 0.33), 'workshop': (240, 0.30), 'mint': (240, 0.30), 'granary': (240, 0.33),
        'u_sword': (200, 0.16), 'u_spear': (200, 0.14), 'u_halberd': (200, 0.14), 'u_bow': (200, 0.16), 'u_cav': (210, 0.16), 'u_ram': (210, 0.18), 'u_trans': (220, 0.16)}
PAPER = np.array([250, 247, 239], np.float32)
out = {}
for key, (size, ay) in SPEC.items():
    im = np.asarray(Image.open(os.path.join(RAW, key + '.png')).convert('RGB')).astype(np.float32)
    # 흰 점 맞추기(종이처럼 찍힌 바탕 보정): 가장자리 띠의 밝은 쪽
    edge = np.concatenate([im[:10].reshape(-1, 3), im[-10:].reshape(-1, 3), im[:, :10].reshape(-1, 3), im[:, -10:].reshape(-1, 3)])
    wp = np.maximum(np.percentile(edge, 60, axis=0), 215.0)
    im = np.clip(im / wp[None, None, :] * 255.0, 0, 255)
    mn = im.min(axis=2); mx = im.max(axis=2); unit = key.startswith('u_')
    # 바깥: 가장자리에서 '밝은 칸'으로 이어진 곳. 선 사이의 좁은 틈으로 안까지 새어 들지 않게 한 번 깎았다가 되돌린다
    light = (mn >= (232 if unit else 198)) & (mx - mn <= 26)
    core = ndi.binary_erosion(light, iterations=3, border_value=1)
    lab, n = ndi.label(core)
    border = set(np.unique(np.concatenate([lab[0], lab[-1], lab[:, 0], lab[:, -1]]))) - {0}
    outside = ndi.binary_dilation(np.isin(lab, list(border)), iterations=4) & light
    # 바깥의 먹은 흰 종이 위의 먹으로 보고 투명도로 바꾼다: 밝을수록 투명
    a_out = np.clip((252.0 - mn) / (26.0 if unit else 64.0), 0.0, 1.0)
    alpha = np.where(outside, a_out, 1.0).astype(np.float32)
    alpha = np.minimum(alpha, ndi.gaussian_filter(alpha, 0.6) + 0.5)          # 톱니 완화
    a3 = np.clip(alpha, 0.03, 1)[..., None]
    rgb = np.where(outside[..., None], np.clip((im - 255.0 * (1 - a3)) / a3, 0, 255), im)
    # 안쪽의 흰 곳은 종이 빛으로(지도 위에서 너무 새하얗지 않게)
    rgb = rgb * (PAPER / 255.0)[None, None, :]
    rgba = np.dstack([rgb, alpha * 255.0]).astype(np.uint8)
    ys, xs = np.where(alpha > 0.06)
    box = (xs.min(), ys.min(), xs.max() + 1, ys.max() + 1)
    sp = Image.fromarray(rgba, 'RGBA').crop(box)
    k = size / max(sp.size)
    sp = sp.resize((max(1, round(sp.width * k)), max(1, round(sp.height * k))), Image.LANCZOS)
    arr = np.asarray(sp).astype(np.float32); rgbf = arr[..., :3] / 255.0; al = arr[..., 3] / 255.0
    rgbf = np.clip(rgbf ** (1.55 if unit else 1.15), 0, 1)                     # 줄이면서 옅어진 먹을 눌러 준다(부대는 더 짙게)
    if unit:                                                                   # 부대: 종이 빛 테(짙은 산·숲 위에서도 보이게)
        ring = np.clip(ndi.gaussian_filter(ndi.grey_dilation(al, size=(3, 3)), 0.8), 0, 1)
        oc = PAPER / 255.0
        out_a = np.maximum(al, ring * 0.88)
        rgbf = rgbf * al[..., None] + oc[None, None, :] * (1 - al[..., None])
        al = out_a
    sp = Image.fromarray(np.dstack([rgbf * 255.0, al * 255.0]).astype(np.uint8), 'RGBA')
    sp.save(os.path.join(OUT, key + '.png'))
    bio = io.BytesIO(); sp.save(bio, 'WEBP', quality=86, method=6)
    out[key] = dict(u='data:image/webp;base64,' + base64.b64encode(bio.getvalue()).decode('ascii'), w=sp.width, h=sp.height, ay=ay)
    print(key, sp.size, len(bio.getvalue()), 'wp', [int(v) for v in wp])
with open(OUT_JS, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f: f.write('var SPRITES=' + json.dumps(out, separators=(',', ':')) + ';\n')
print('sprites.js', os.path.getsize(OUT_JS))

=====8<===== tools/make_roster.py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""194년 군웅할거 로스터 생성.
기존 群雄割據 삼국지(index.html)의 GEN_DEFS에서 능력치(무력/통솔/지력/정치)를 가져오고,
매력·병과 적성 5종·특기·소속을 붙여 src/roster.js 를 만든다.
usage: make_roster.py <threekingdoms/index.html> <out roster.js>
"""
import sys, re, json

SRC, OUT = sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2]
src = open(SRC, encoding='utf-8').read()
i = src.index('const GEN_DEFS=['); j = src.index('];', i)
BASE = {}
for m in re.finditer(r"\['([^']+)',\s*(-?\d+),\s*(-?\d+),\s*(-?\d+),\s*(-?\d+),\s*(-?\d+),\s*(-?\d+)(?:,\s*(-?\d+),\s*(-?\d+),\s*(-?\d+))?\]", src[i:j]):
    n = m.group(1)
    if n in BASE: continue
    BASE[n] = dict(war=int(m.group(2)), ldr=int(m.group(3)), int=int(m.group(4)), pol=int(m.group(5)),
                   apt3=[int(m.group(8) or 2), int(m.group(9) or 2), int(m.group(10) or 2)])

# 기존 로스터에 없는 인물 (무력, 통솔, 지력, 정치) — 자체 산정
NEW = {

 '등현': (70, 68, 48, 44), '양회': (72, 70, 52, 46), '조위': (70, 74, 62, 58), '방희': (62, 68, 64, 66),

}

# 매력 — 자체 산정 (없으면 공식)
CHA = {'조조': 96, '유비': 99, '손권': 95, '손책': 92, '원소': 92, '원술': 70, '유표': 85, '유장': 72, '공손찬': 75, '마등': 82,
 '장로': 86, '도겸': 78, '공융': 80, '여포': 40, '이각': 30, '곽사': 28, '유요': 70, '엄백호': 35, '왕랑': 68, '공손도': 66,
 '장양': 70, '관우': 93, '장비': 45, '조운': 88, '제갈량': 92, '주유': 93, '육손': 90, '사마의': 79, '순욱': 91, '곽가': 78,
 '가후': 58, '노숙': 89, '여몽': 80, '마초': 82, '황충': 75, '장료': 78, '하후돈': 81, '하후연': 76, '조인': 74, '서황': 70,
